- **H23a3 (as-built, MERGED PR #96 `a1f4ffa`, 6/6 CI green on the FIRST run — Contract 55s, frontend 1m29s, static 1m46s, a11y 2m18s, Pest 4m16s, E2E 13m6s) — tenant branding reaches the ADMIN APP, and the precedence rule that makes "personalization wins" real.** **THE HEADLINE IS THAT H23a1's OWN DOCUMENTED FIX WAS WRONG, AND BUILDING IT IS HOW IT WAS FOUND.** DSR §395 predicted the Blueprint-escape would be closed with an explicit `data-accent="blueprint"` plus a `:root[data-accent='blueprint']` restoring rule. **That rule is (0,2,0) and the tenant ramp's light block is (0,1,0), so it would have outranked the brand in EVERY case — the tenant's colour would never have applied at all.** Not merely more complex: wrong. §395 is CORRECTED to describe what was built rather than left standing. **AS BUILT — PRECEDENCE IS RESOLVED ON THE SERVER, NOT IN CSS:** `app.blade.php` emits the tenant ramp **only when the member has expressed no accent opinion**. Picked Teal → Teal's existing `[data-accent='teal']` rules apply over the base tokens exactly as before; picked Blueprint → the base tokens, which ARE Blueprint, so **no restoring rule needs to exist**; no opinion → the tenant ramp. **Personalization cannot lose a specificity contest it never enters.** The CSS route was rejected on inspection because the ramp needs `:root` + `[data-theme-mode='dark']` + a `prefers-color-scheme` twin — (0,1,0), (0,2,0), (0,3,0) — and the user's teal rule at (0,2,0) would have **TIED** with the tenant's dark block, leaving source order to decide; **G11 already shipped that exact bug once.** **THE SEMANTIC CHANGE THAT MAKES THE ESCAPE EXPRESSIBLE:** `user_ui_preferences.accent_token` widens from `{NULL,'teal'}` to `{NULL,'blueprint','teal'}`. **NULL now means exactly one thing — "no opinion"** — and Blueprint is stored EXPLICITLY. The old mapping collapsed "never chose" and "chose the default" into one row, which is precisely what left a member of a branded tenant unable to ask for the product blue back. **No migration** — the column already permitted the wider domain. `defaultUiTheme()` returns **null** rather than `'blueprint'` for the same reason: a user who has never opened Settings has refused nothing, and `'blueprint'` would have made every un-personalized member silently opt OUT of their own organisation's branding. **READ THROUGH `TenantBrandingService::isActive()`, NEVER `hasBrandRamp()`** — stored and active are different questions and a surface that checks the wrong one ships a Starter+ feature to Free tenants with nothing in the build to notice; `sharedRamp()` is **fail-closed off-tenant** (mirroring `EntitlementService::snapshot()` on the same middleware) and ships **only the twelve tokens**, because the measurements and input hex are the settings card's business and would put ~2KB of contrast tables into every render. The blade's accent check becomes a **real whitelist**, matching the comment two lines above it that already claimed one, and emits `data-accent` **only for Teal** — so §2.9's absence-as-default convention survives at the ATTRIBUTE level unchanged. **TWO VERIFICATION LESSONS WORTH CARRYING:** (1) **a test written in this increment was passing VACUOUSLY and a SIBLING caught it** — `user_ui_preferences` is RLS-scoped to `app.current_user_id`, which the request tears down, so "stores an explicit null" was asserting `toBeNull()` against a row it could not SEE rather than against a null column; it passed for the wrong reason until the next test needed that row to still hold its other axes. Both now re-enter tenant context before reading. (2) **five existing tests failed and were SUPPOSED to** — they pin the old semantics ("stores the blueprint default as NULL", "resolves a NULL accent_token back to blueprint"); each was REWRITTEN to assert the new contract with the reasoning inline rather than having its expected value flipped, and one now also asserts that `AccentToken::fromColumn()` still performs the old collapse, since that helper survives and answers its own narrower question. **Gates: Pest 2276 (+10), full suite green; Vitest 1181; PHPStan exactly 55, zero introduced, verified PER FILE — it read 56 and the extra was mine, a stale `accent: string` annotation on `uiTheme()`; Pint / vue-tsc / controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint / `composer audit` clean. No migration, no route change, no `openapi.json` drift.**

- **H23a2 (as-built, MERGED PR #95 `c40a3de`, 6/6 CI green after ONE red cycle — see the withoutVite note at the end of this bullet) — tenant branding STORAGE plus the admin card, and the card's job is to say what the engine changed.** **STORAGE:** three `tenants` columns — `primary_color` (the hex the tenant TYPED, an INPUT not a rendered value), `brand_ramp` jsonb (the twelve derived hexes + 17 measured ratios + `engine_version`) and `logo_attachment_id` (FK → `attachments`, `nullOnDelete`). Two were spec'd in data-dictionary §114–115 long before anything built them; **`brand_ramp` is the new one and it earns its place for ADR-0014 §D8's reason — three of the five consuming surfaces cannot resolve a CSS custom property AT ALL** (mail clients strip `<style>` and ignore `var()`; dompdf supports neither custom properties nor `oklch()`), so once the ramp must exist as data, re-deriving it on read is duplication with a drift hazard attached. **THE TRAP THAT FAILS SILENTLY, and the reason `BrandingStorageTest` exists at all:** `tenants` is a stancl/tenancy model, and a real column NOT listed in `Tenant::getCustomColumns()` is relocated into the `data` json store and read back as **NULL** — no error, a green write path, and branding that simply never applies. The migration ships the three list additions in the SAME commit, and the test reads the columns back through **raw SQL** so a mis-store cannot hide behind the accessor. That matters more than usual because `migration-lint` keys on `Schema::create` and skips alter-only migrations, so **this migration's own CI green is VACUOUS** (the H25 lesson, verbatim). **ONE THING THE PLAN GOT WRONG AND A TEST CAUGHT:** the migration docblock originally credited `ON DELETE SET NULL` with stopping a deleted logo from rendering. **It does not.** `Attachment` uses `SoftDeletes`, so an ordinary `delete()` leaves the row and the referential action NEVER FIRES — the pointer goes on pointing at a trashed row. What actually saves the render path is the **SoftDeletes global scope on the relation**; the FK earns its keep only on `forceDelete()`. Both halves are asserted separately now, because either one alone teaches the wrong model. **THE GATING ASYMMETRY IS THE DESIGN AND MUST NOT BE TIDIED:** the two WRITES carry `feature:branding` (Starter+, already in `PlanCatalog.php:54` — not re-litigated); **the two REMOVALS deliberately do NOT.** ADR-0012 §D9 applied — a tenant that brands on Starter and downgrades to Free keeps a stored ramp, stops rendering branded, and must retain a path to DELETE what a paid tier let them create; gate the removals and they are stranded with a brand they can neither use nor remove. `BrandingRoutesTest` asserts BOTH directions, because making all four match looks like a cleanup. The same distinction is why `TenantBrandingService::isActive()` (stored AND entitled) is separate from `Tenant::hasBrandRamp()` (stored): confuse them one way and the paid feature is free, the other way and a downgraded tenant loses their own settings card. **THE SNAP DISCLOSURE IS THE POINT OF THE UI, not a nicety** — §D2 discards the tenant's lightness, so what renders is frequently NOT what they typed, and the ADR requires that be DISCLOSED rather than discovered: the card shows the submitted swatch beside the derived one, says plainly whether it was adjusted, previews BOTH themes, and prints all 17 ratios **worst-first by HEADROOM** (a 4.6:1 needing 4.5 is tighter than a 5.2:1 needing 3.0 — sorting by raw ratio would bury the number a sceptic wants). The preview runs the TS twin so the ramp is visible before saving, and both the `adjusted` rule and the ordering live in `brand-ramp.ts` rather than the component because each already has a PHP twin in `BrandingPresenter` and a third inline copy would be a third place to drift. **SVG IS REFUSED AS A LOGO AND THAT IS A SECURITY BOUNDARY, not a limitation:** it is XML that can carry `<script>` and a logo is served same-origin to every respondent of every branded form — stored XSS (threat-model §6). Refused at the request rule AND by the service's CONTENT-SNIFFED allowlist, since `mimetypes:` trusts a client-controlled header. GIF is out too, for the duller reason that an animated logo on every form header is a WCAG 2.2.2 problem nobody would think to check. **The logo is the first attachment whose owner is not form-scoped**, so it stores the morph alias `tenant` — **DELIBERATELY not registered in the global morph map**, following the warning already in `AppServiceProvider` about `audits.auditable_type`: registering it would change how Sanctum's `tokenable_type` and Spatie's `model_type` serialize a Tenant and split existing rows between alias and FQCN. Nothing in this codebase resolves `->attachable`, and `BrandingMorphAliasTest` pins that the absence is deliberate. **FOUR THINGS THE LOCAL GATES CAUGHT THAT REVIEW WOULD NOT:** (1) the design system's OWN `token-references` guard rejected two token names invented in the Vue file — `--mds-color-text-muted` and `--mds-color-border-subtle` do not exist (they are `-text-secondary` / `-border-default`); (2) the role is **`form_editor`, not `editor`**, and `catalogRole()` returns an EMPTY STRING for an unknown name, which surfaces as an invalid-uuid `QueryException` rather than anything legible; (3) **`UploadedFile::fake()->image()` needs GD, which this container lacks** — it throws a LogicException rather than skipping, so the fixture uses real 1×1 PNG bytes exactly as `StorageQuotaEnforcementTest` already does, which also means `getMimeType()` content-sniffs for real; (4) **the tenant GUC is torn down after every HTTP request**, so a mid-test downgrade needs `enterTenant()` before `assignPlanTier()` or the `subscriptions` insert fails RLS with 42501. **Gates: Pest 2266 (+26), full suite green; Vitest 1181 (+4) across 54 files; Pint / vue-tsc / Vite build / controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint / `composer audit` clean. PHPStan exactly 55, zero introduced, VERIFIED PER FILE — it read 57 first and BOTH extras were mine** (an int/string user id, a `BelongsTo` missing its generics), while the `FormField` and `two_factor_secret` entries in the same output are pre-existing phantoms whose line numbers merely moved — and the new method was checked explicitly for the H24b2 docblock-orphaning trap. **No `/api/v1` route added, so no `openapi.json` drift.** **ONE RED CI CYCLE, AND THE LESSON IS REUSABLE: a Pest GET that asserts Inertia renders the ROOT VIEW, so `@vite` runs and needs a manifest — and the CI tests job NEVER builds assets.** Without `$this->withoutVite()` the three page-render tests passed LOCALLY (because `public/build/` survives from a dev build and is gitignored) and failed in CI as **"Not a valid Inertia response"**, which is really a 500 from `ViteManifestNotFoundException` wearing a misleading message. `AnalyticsPageGateTest` and `SuperAdminConsoleTest` already carried the call with that exact comment; this file did not, and these were **the first tests in the repo to GET `/settings`**. Diagnosed by deleting `public/build` locally to reproduce CI's condition exactly, then MUTATION-VERIFIED — the failure returns when the line is commented out. **Any new Pest GET that asserts Inertia needs `withoutVite()`.**

- **H23a1 (as-built, MERGED PR #94 `567742e`, 6/6 CI green on the FIRST run — Contract 45s, frontend 1m17s, static 1m39s, a11y 2m5s, Pest 4m11s, E2E 12m45s) — the tenant brand-ramp engine, and the ADR that reverses a ratified design-system rule.** Ships **ADR-0014 in-PR** (the ADR-0012/ADR-0013 precedent) because it overturns a decision rather than adding one. **WHAT UNBLOCKED IT:** the user answered the costed three-way fork on 2026-08-05 — see the Current Status headline for the three answers and the four-PR split. **THE FACT THE WHOLE INCREMENT TURNS ON, and it is not obvious until you measure: a brand hex is not a token.** Teal — the one hue the design system already lets a USER choose — needed **six tokens per theme** (fill / hover / active / fg / tint / ring) and carries **seventeen measured pairings** in DSR §4.1. So "validate the tenant's colour" describes nothing that can be done: there is nothing to validate until eleven other colours exist, and it is those eleven that decide legibility. The increment therefore ships a **generator**, not a validator. **AS BUILT:** `ColorSpace` (sRGB ⇄ OKLCh + WCAG contrast), `BrandRamp` (the 12-hex value object), `BrandRampGenerator`, `BrandRampException`, a TypeScript twin at `packages/design-system/src/theme/brand-ramp.ts`, and `tests/fixtures/brand-ramp.json`. It holds the tenant's **hue**, caps **chroma** at 0.11 (≈1.3× the most saturated ratified accent — Blueprint measures 0.0832, Teal 0.0651), **discards lightness entirely** and re-derives it per role by sweeping against a fixed ground until all seventeen pairings measure clean. **Lightness is discarded because lightness IS contrast** — keeping the tenant's L would keep the one property that decides whether white button text is legible; the consequence, which H23a2's UI must DISCLOSE rather than let a tenant discover, is that `#FF0000` and `#8B0000` give near-identical ramps and every achromatic input gives the SAME grey ramp (three corpus vectors pin it). **WHY IT CAN ALWAYS SUCCEED — this is what makes it B-snap and not B-strict:** contrast against a fixed ground is monotone in lightness and the sweep covers the whole range, so a compliant answer exists for every hue (**exercised at 5° steps around the wheel, not asserted**). Where chroma cannot survive at the required lightness the gamut mapper gives up **chroma**, holding lightness and hue — that concession IS the snap, and it means the product never tells a paying customer their brand colour is unusable. **THE EVIDENCE THE TARGETS ENCODE THE SYSTEM AND NOT ONE AUTHOR'S TASTE, and it is the strongest thing in the PR: fed Teal's own hue the engine independently re-derives `--mds-accent-teal-600` (`#1B5E5E`) and `--mds-accent-teal-50` (`#E6F2F2`) BYTE-IDENTICALLY, and returns §4.1 rows 1–3 as 7.48 / 9.40 / 12.10.** Targets are **ratios, not ramp steps**, because the two shipped accents *disagree* about steps (dark Blueprint darkens on hover 6.14→9.14→13.00; dark Teal lightens then darkens 6.68→4.82→9.40, itself exceptions-log #6) and *agree* about ratios; the engine's targets are their midpoint, so a tenant ramp lands in the product's own visual register rather than scraping the 4.5 floor. Two roles are **aliases** drawn from both accents unanimously (light `fg` = light `bg`; dark `ring` = dark `fg`) and a third, light `ring` = light `bg_hover`, is where they disagree and is recorded as a choice. **ONE CONSTRAINT DELIBERATELY OMITTED AND THE OMISSION LOOKS LIKE AN OVERSIGHT: fill-versus-canvas.** A button ought to be distinguishable from the page, and WCAG 1.4.11 is the obvious authority — but measuring the shipped system BEFORE writing any code showed **both ratified accents FAIL a 3:1 reading of it in dark mode (Blueprint 2.61, Teal 2.40)**. Adding it would have made the engine stricter than the design system it serves and rejected the product's own colours. §4.1 does not list it; neither does the engine (ADR-0014 §D6). **DUAL-ENGINE, and three implementation choices exist ONLY to serve parity — each a deviation from what one would otherwise write:** (1) **`x ** (1/3)`, never `Math.cbrt`** — cbrt is the BETTER function, correctly rounded where `pow` is not, and that is exactly why it is wrong: PHP has none, so using it would have the two engines computing one quantity by two routes agreeing only to within a few ulp; **matching the WEAKER implementation is what makes the pair exact**. (2) **Fixed trip counts** (a 1001-point lightness scan, a 32-step gamut bisection), never a tolerance-terminated loop, which can take different iteration counts on two platforms and land on opposite sides of an 8-bit rounding boundary. (3) **Clamp, THEN round** — `Math.round` is half-up, PHP's `round()` is half-away-from-zero, and they agree only on non-negative values. **PHP STAYS AUTHORITATIVE** because four of the five consuming surfaces are server-rendered, and **three of them cannot resolve a CSS custom property at all** (mail clients strip `<style>` and ignore `var()`; dompdf supports neither custom properties reliably nor `oklch()`) — which is also the real reason the derived ramp is **STORED rather than re-derived on read**, with `engine_version` making a future target change an explicit, auditable re-derivation instead of a silent repaint of every live tenant. **THE FIXTURE LIVES IN `tests/fixtures/`, NOT `tests/golden/`** — the H21a-A6 precedent that `condition-serializer.json` and `step-projection.json` already follow: no `grammar_version` key, no manifest entry, so **296 sites and 114 vectors are unmoved** (verified; `git diff tests/golden` empty). Its 12 vectors are chosen by **failure mode**, not coverage: an achromatic trio that must produce ONE ramp (divergence = the engine started reading the input's lightness), a maximally saturated yellow that must snap, a chroma at 2.4× the cap, and near-black/near-white vectors *with a hue* straddling the sRGB transfer function's 0.04045 branch. Each engine **re-measures all seventeen pairings from the STORED hexes** rather than trusting the fixture's ratios — comparing stored ratios only would let a fixture hand-edited to green a red build pass both halves. **DOCS: ADR-0014 + five reconciliations.** DSR §2.9's picker prohibition is **NARROWED, not deleted** — it still governs USER personalization (`AccentToken` stays closed at two) and no longer governs TENANT branding, two layers with two rules, stated once so a future reader does not "restore consistency" by collapsing them. **THE COST IS STATED RATHER THAN SOFTENED, in the ADR, the exceptions-log and the DSR alike: the accessibility guarantee moves from STRUCTURAL (no reachable combination CAN be inaccessible, because a human enumerated them all) to PROCEDURAL (every stored ramp was verified at write time) — which is precisely the risk §2.9 was written to close.** Also: exceptions-log **#7**; §4.1 marked as an **executable specification** (its 17 rows ARE `pairings()`, asserted by count and by a 9/8 per-theme split); **§395's absence-as-default invariant amended** for the Blueprint-escape defect H23a3 must close (once a tenant ramp occupies `:root`, absence of `data-accent` stops meaning "product default" and starts meaning "inherit my org's brand", leaving a member no way back — fixed by emitting an explicit `data-accent="blueprint"` only when the tenant has a ramp); `testing-strategy.md`'s dual-engine section; the PRD ADR index. **Gates: Pest 2240 (+39), full suite green; Vitest 1177 (+49) across 54 files; Pint + vue-tsc clean; controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint green; `composer audit` + `npm audit --omit=dev` clean at push time. PHPStan exactly 55, zero introduced, VERIFIED PER FILE — it read 56 first and THE 56TH WAS MINE**, exactly as this tracker predicts: an unnarrowable `$target['fg']` on a union of two array shapes, now spelled `self::TARGETS['dark']['fg']`. **No schema change, no route change, no `openapi.json` drift, no frontend build change.**

- **H22b (as-built, MERGED PR #92 `84c1034`, 6/6 CI green first run) — the custom-domain ADMIN UI, and `is_primary` with it.** H-map row 256 (`ui, S–M`), shipped **full-stack M/L** and the widening is recorded rather than hidden (the H6b/H21a precedent): **three decisions locked with the user up front — (1) its OWN page `/domains` + a sidebar item** (the /webhooks · /integrations · /analytics precedent, not a card in /settings); **(2) the web gates MIRROR THE API EXACTLY**, `feature:custom_domain` on the writes only; **(3) SHIP `is_primary` NOW**, which ADR-0012's *When to Revisit* said belongs with this UI. **THE ONE THING THAT MADE `is_primary` SAFE TO ADD IS A CHECK CONSTRAINT, NOT AN ARGUMENT.** ADR-0012 §D6 leaves `php artisan domains:activate` as the ONLY code path that can set `activated_at`, and `OpenApiContractTest` asserts no activate endpoint exists — so a NEW tenant-reachable write to `domains` had to be provably unable to make an inert hostname serve anything. `domains_primary_requires_live_chk` (`is_primary = false OR activated_at IS NOT NULL`) says it in DDL, the service refuses the same case, and the endpoint 422s: a tenant chooses AMONG hosts an operator activated and can never produce one. **ITS DIRECT CONSEQUENCE IS THE EASIEST THING IN THIS INCREMENT TO GET WRONG:** `deactivate()` MUST clear the flag in the same write, or `domains:activate --deactivate` becomes a constraint violation instead of a deactivation — the operator's "take it offline now" would fail. `ActivateCustomDomainCommandTest` pins it, and `TenantHostTest` pins the consequence (the public host falls back to the next-oldest live domain rather than nowhere). **`default false` IS SAFE HERE FOR THE OPPOSITE REASON §D4 REFUSED A `status` DEFAULT:** false is the FAIL-CLOSED value — it means "no preference expressed", so `TenantUrl::publicHost()` falls through to the pre-existing oldest-activated tiebreak (KEPT, not replaced, with `is_primary DESC` merely prepended) and every row that predates the column emits the host it emitted yesterday. **The first activation elects a primary; the second does not** — a one-domain tenant should not have to choose between one thing and nothing, and repointing outstanding respondent links is the tenant's call, never a side effect of an operator running a command. **`TenantUrl::publicHost()`/`appHost()` are now PUBLIC and the presenter READS them** rather than re-deriving "which host do respondents get": `is_public_host` is not simply `is_primary` (a tenant with no preference still has a public host), so a second implementation would label the right row today and the wrong one the first time the fallback mattered. **Net-new:** `App\Http\Controllers\Tenant\DomainController`, `App\Services\Tenancy\DomainPresenter`, `App\Http\Requests\Tenant\StoreDomainRequest` (a SEPARATE class from the `Api\V1` twin — a web surface importing from a versioned namespace makes an API bump a UI change — but composing the SAME `ClaimableDomain`, so what may be claimed and what routes cannot drift), `resources/js/Pages/domains/Index.vue` + `components/domains/{DomainCard,DnsRecordBlock,DomainSetupGuide,types}`, five web routes, one API twin (`POST /api/v1/domains/{domain}/primary`), and the migration. **THE HONESTY IS THE FEATURE:** there is NO activate button, not even a disabled one (that would imply the tenant is one permission away from it), the `verified` badge reads **"Awaiting setup"** rather than "Verified" (a tenant told only "verified" will reasonably repoint live traffic at an origin holding no certificate — the exact failure §D5's TXT-over-CNAME ordering exists to prevent), and the three `DomainVerificationFailure` cases render as three DIFFERENT instructions because they call for three different actions. **§D9's ASYMMETRY IS NOW FULLY HONOURED, WHICH IT WAS NOT BEFORE:** index + destroy carry no `feature:` gate, but the nav item does — so a tenant downgraded off Business would have had no destination at all. `/settings` therefore links `/domains` whenever the tenant actually HOLDS a custom domain (one query, on one page, deliberately not a shared prop), invisible to everyone else so nothing advertises a tier ADR-0008 §D6 holds from sale. **E2E fixture:** two rows, upsert-keyed so a re-seed CONVERGES (the H24b2 precedent; every other seeder block is `doesntExist()`-guarded and drifts), one pending + one awaiting-operator — and **NEITHER ACTIVATED, which is a constraint on the fixture rather than a gap in it**: an activated row is visible to `Domain`'s global scope and would repoint every resume link in the e2e database onto a hostname the browser cannot resolve, failing the guest-runtime specs as what would look like a public-runtime defect. `E2eSeederIdempotencyTest` pins both the convergence and the never-activated property. Docs: **ADR-0012 amended** (an `> Amended in H22b` block under the `is_primary` revisit trigger + the closed "admin surface is H22b" consequence), data-dictionary §1, deployment-infrastructure §8.1. **Gates:** Pest **2149 passed / 0 failed** (745s), Vitest **1126 passed / 53 files**, PHPStan **55** (the phantom baseline — zero introduced, verified PER FILE), Pint clean, `vue-tsc` clean, build clean, Redocly clean, `composer audit` clean, golden corpora byte-untouched (296 / 114).

- **H24b2 (as-built, MERGED PR #88 `e2326ac0` — RED TWICE before it went green, the first increment since H7 that did not pass on the first run) — the Business-gated `/analytics` page: the surface H24a's substrate and H24b1's primitives were built for.** Five routes on the tenant web group, gated `can:viewAny,SavedReportView` + `feature:advanced_analytics` — the `/api/v1` twins key for key MINUS `ability:`, which is Sanctum token-scope middleware a session cannot carry. A thin `AnalyticsController` + `AnalyticsViewController` over a new `AnalyticsPresenter` doing INLINE mapping (never an `Api\V1` Resource), filters in query params via Inertia partial reloads, saved-view writes as web verbs returning redirect + toast, and the export streaming the SAME `AnalyticsExporter`. **THREE THINGS THE APPROVED SHAPE DID NOT ANTICIPATE, each verified against the tree before being believed.** **(1) `InvalidAnalyticsQueryException` was a 500 on any web route** — `bootstrap/app.php` rendered it only for `api/v1/*`, and the 366-day bound lives in the VO's CONSTRUCTOR by design, so a bookmarked `?from=2024-01-01` exploded; the increment adds the web arm, scoped to `analytics.*` so an off-surface throw keeps its 500, and redirecting to the bare index rather than `back()` because a bookmarked bad URL has no referer. **(2) `SavedReportViewService::resolve()` structurally cannot see a dangling scope node** — it re-parses SHAPE only, so §D8's promised 'stated refusal' was a 404 instead; closed PRESENTATIONALLY in the presenter with two batched existence queries, leaving the substrate untouched. **(3) The draft-suppression off-by-one is one day wide** — `draftMetrics()` suppresses when `fromUtc() < now()-30d`, so spelling the default window `subDays(30)` instead of 29 would suppress BOTH §D5 tiles on every request forever with every gate green over a dead feature; the constant moved to `AnalyticsQuery::DEFAULT_RANGE_DAYS` so the two surfaces cannot disagree, and one Pest assertion at the default range is the only thing that catches it. **TWO DEFECTS WERE FOUND BY LOOKING, NOT BY A GATE** — the standing H24b1 lesson, twice. A bucket label formatted at the QUERY's timezone renders a day early in any negative-offset zone (H24b1 got away with it because the dashboard's zone is hard-coded UTC); the fix formats at UTC and NAMES the zone instead, and `bucket-label.test.ts` catches it by putting the RUNNER in a negative-offset zone, because under a UTC runner the bug is invisible. And with a question selected but its aggregate in flight, the card read 'Pick a question' beside a checked radio — two controls disagreeing on one screen, the same shape as H24b1's two draft tiles. **Four derivations are SHARED with `/dashboard` rather than re-implemented** (bucket formatter, breakdown bars, both draft tiles): `dashboard.test.ts` passes byte-unchanged, which is the proof the extraction moved nothing. **`SavedReportViewService::guardName()` now wraps its write in `DB::transaction()`** — PostgreSQL aborts the whole transaction on a 23505, so catching it and carrying on only worked while this was the outermost transaction; a nested SAVEPOINT contains it and the redirect that carries the field error survives. **The E2E fixture is the blocking obligation, discharged**: a 7th published form (`Programme Uptake`, four `is_queryable` fields + one deliberately unflagged) and 30 relative-dated rows across six forms — form axis overflowing top-5 into `other{count:3,categories:2}` (the PLURAL branch), five source categories with NO overflow (so `other === null` and `other !== null` are both reachable from real data), a non-zero Unassigned on two axes, five empty days, and drafts at denominator 8 / 62.5% / 900s including ONE adversarial `offline_sync` row that §D5's `source IN (guest,manual)` must exclude. **No `doesntExist()` guard** — every write is an upsert on a deterministic `client_submission_uuid`, so a re-seed CONVERGES instead of skipping, and `E2eSeederIdempotencyTest` turns that from prose into a gate (it reddens when the key is swapped for `Str::uuid()`). Index rows are written through the REAL `AnswerIndexProjector`, never `SubmissionPipeline`: the pipeline hard-codes `submitted_at => now()` (re-degenerating the series), fires `SubmissionCreated` whose synchronous listeners would attempt real outbound HTTPS during `db:seed` under the e2e job's `QUEUE_CONNECTION: sync`, and would pollute the delivery ledgers other axe specs assert on. **Gates:** Pint · PHPStan (zero introduced) · controller-gate · migration-lint · `vue-tsc` ×2 · 1120 Vitest · 2000+ Pest · `openapi.json` byte-identical · analytics-axe 13/13 with zero violations · responsive-axe Analytics AND Dashboard green at 375/834/1440 in both themes over the new non-degenerate fixture. **Four mutations were run and all four reddened**, each against a different layer. **ONE DEFECT WAS SHIPPED AND CAUGHT BY CI, NOT BY THIS SESSION'S OWN CHECKS**: the 328-line fixture insertion landed BETWEEN `sampleAnswers()`'s docblock and its declaration, orphaning it — PHPStan named that exact file and it was dismissed as a pre-existing line, when the line number having MOVED was the tell. Fixed in b626dc0 as a pure move. The lesson is recorded as a standing trap: a large insertion displaces a docblock without deleting a line, so `--stat` shows insertions-only and review sees nothing. **One PRE-EXISTING test defect was found and fixed rather than routed around**: `assignPlanTier()` APPENDED a subscription instead of replacing one, so a `beforeEach` baseline plus a per-test override left TWO active rows — and `EntitlementService::resolvePlan()` picks with `latest('created_at')`, which is a TIE for two rows written in the same second, broken by physical row order. Which tier won therefore depended on how much the run had written before, so adding test files ANYWHERE could flip an unrelated suite. It surfaced as `ConnectorOAuthFlowTest`'s native-connectors gate failing OPEN (a 302 to the provider passes `assertRedirect()` and fails only on the assertion after it) and it reproduces on main with `Webhooks + Connectors`, which is how it was confirmed pre-existing. The helper now deletes first; production is unaffected because a tenant has one subscription and real ones are not created in the same second. **MERGE-RUN GATES — THREE RUNS, AND THE HONEST HISTORY IS THAT NEITHER RED WAS THE INCREMENT'S DESIGN.** Run 1 (`6051a2a`): five green — Pest 3m21s, E2E **12m35s**, design-system-a11y 2m4s, frontend 1m25s, contract-tests 59s (so the byte-identical `openapi.json` and the non-degenerate fixture both held from the start) — and `Static analysis` red on the orphaned-docblock PHPStan error above, which also SKIPPED the six gates queued behind it (controller-gate, migration-lint, job-payload lint, composer audit, npm audit, gitleaks). Run 2 (`b626dc0`, the docblock fix): PHPStan green, E2E 12m44s, Pest 3m35s — and `Static analysis` red **again**, now on `composer audit`, over two guzzle advisories published **2026-08-03 21:05–21:07 UTC, hours after the branch was pushed and minutes before run 1 started**: CVE-2026-69246 (**high**, noncanonical host bypasses host-based checks) and CVE-2026-69245 (medium, noncanonical cookie domain keeps subdomain scope), both against `<7.15.2`. **That one matters beyond the gate** — guzzle is the transport under the whole outbound vertical, and both the webhook engine and the Slack connector send TENANT-CONFIGURED URLs through it, so a host check a noncanonical form slips past is SSRF-adjacent on a surface where the destination is attacker-influenced by design. It reddened `main` identically, so it was split out to **PR #89 `3fb52df`** (lockfile only, one patch bump, zero transitive churn) on the PR #61 precedent rather than ridden in on this branch, and `main` was merged back in. Run 3 (`6b1499c4`): **6/6 green** — Pest 3m12s, E2E 12m14s, static analysis 1m54s, a11y 2m19s, frontend 1m21s, contract 46s. **THE STANDING LESSON IS THE SECOND RED, NOT THE FIRST:** a green local run proves nothing about `composer audit`, whose input is a REMOTE advisory feed that changes under a branch that has not. Re-run it immediately before pushing, and when it fires, check whether `main` fails identically — if it does, the fix belongs on `main` in its own PR, not folded into the increment whose diff would then carry an unrelated dependency change.

- **H24b1 (as-built, MERGED PR #87 `69a54bd`, 6/6 CI green first try) — the chart primitives, the chart token family, and the dashboard that reads its own data.** H24b split at the H21d seam: **H24b1** is the design system plus the **ungated** dashboard (`PRD.md:197`, Phase-1 criteria, every tier); **H24b2** is the Business-gated `/analytics` page. This half adds no route, no gate, no migration, no seeder change and no `/api/v1` touch, so `openapi.json` is byte-identical. **THE FIVE SERIES COLOURS ARE DERIVED, NOT PICKED, AND THE DARK RE-POINT IS PROVEN RATHER THAN ASSERTED:** put the light column unchanged on the dark ground and **four of five fail WCAG 1.4.11** (2.51 / 1.83 / **1.56** / 2.86:1); the raw Okabe-Ito anchors fail the white ground from the other end (`#E69F00` = 2.25:1). That is ADR-0011 §D11's "five is derived, not chosen" as arithmetic, and the measured both-theme table now lives in **DSR §4.1** where the ADR assigned it. `--mds-chart-other/-grid/-axis` alias **neutral primitives**, so they need no re-point at all — the dark block already flips the ramp beneath them; only the five series are re-pointed, from `--mds-_chart-dark-1…5` defined once on `:root` (G11's private-custom device, same anti-drift reason). **The sequential scale is deliberately NOT emitted** — no consumer yet, and an unconsumed token is how the corpus grew a false "chart tokens exist" claim in the first place. **NEVER INTERPOLATE A TOKEN NAME:** `var(--mds-chart-series-${n})` makes `token-references.test.ts` hunt for a token literally named `--mds-chart-series-` and fail; every series is a **static CSS class per index**, which also keeps `stroke-dasharray` off presentation attributes. **THE §D12 TABLE IS THE GATE, NOT AXE** — axe cannot see a *missing* text alternative behind a plausible `aria-label`, so each primitive's data table is pinned by Vitest; 163 Storybook stories scanned clean, which proves nothing about that contract and is exactly why the unit assertions exist. **Two shapes are structural decisions:** the value domain **always includes zero** (a truncated axis is a claim the chart makes on its own initiative, and callers cannot switch it off), and a **degenerate domain is the ordinary path** — a quiet 30-day window is all zeros, so `max − min` is 0 and every consumer divides by it; `niceTicks` widens rather than returning one tick, because the alternative is `d="M0 NaN …"`, which draws nothing, throws nothing and fails no gate. The time-series canvas is stretched (`preserveAspectRatio="none"` + `vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"`) with **no text inside the SVG** — it would stretch with the box — so axis labels are HTML on a CSS grid, the y-column bleeding half a line-height at each end so a tick label's *centre* lands on its gridline; and the bar chart's category label is HTML beside a one-`<rect>` SVG, because SVG text has no wrapping and a long form title at 375px is the non-wrapping-row failure `responsive-axe` has caught three times. **ONE DEFECT WAS FOUND BY LOOKING AT THE RENDERED PAGE, NOT BY ANY GATE:** with six saved drafts and none yet converted, the conversion tile read "0% — of 6 saved drafts" while the median tile, sharing one sentence, read "No drafts were explicitly saved in this period" — two tiles disagreeing about whether drafts exist. `conversion_rate` is null only at a zero denominator; `median_seconds` is *additionally* null when the denominator is positive but nothing converted (`percentile_cont` over an empty set returns NULL), so it needs its own sentence. That is §D5's "three states, never two" landing on the UI side, and **the regression test was run against the unfixed component first and genuinely reddened** (the H21c lesson) before being believed. **A recorded flake was FIXED rather than tolerated:** `token-references` has timed out intermittently since H21b and H21d1 found a REAL dangling token behind one such timeout — so "it timed out again" was never safe to wave through. This increment adds files to the very tree it walks, so the 5 s default was replaced with an explicit 30 s budget against a ~2 s walk. **The backend change is one method and it is in the right file:** `top_forms.rows` carried a bare form UUID, so the label is resolved in `DashboardMetricsService`, never in `AnalyticsMetricsService::breakdown()` whose shape is the byte-diffed API response — and the lookup uses **`withTrashed()`**, because `AnalyticsFormSet::visible()` roots an org-wide reader on `Form::withTrashed()`, so a soft-deleted form legitimately appears in a breakdown and would otherwise render as a bar with a number and no name. **Gates:** Pest **1965 passed / 0 failures** (7043 assertions, +3 over H24a's 1962; the 2 warnings are pre-existing); Vitest **1064 passed** (+66); `vue-tsc` clean; Pint clean (826 files); PHPStan **exactly 55, the documented local baseline, none in a touched file**; `controller-gate` 59; `migration-lint` 40; Storybook axe **163 stories / 0 violations** (run via an equivalent host-driven scan — the node container is alpine, so Playwright browsers cannot install there, and the shared `node_modules` is linux-built, so the real runner's esbuild refuses to start on Windows; CI runs the genuine job); Playwright **108/108** including the Dashboard rows now scanning real charts at 375/834/1440 in both themes; `git diff openapi.json` and `git diff tests/golden` both empty, `grammar_version` still at exactly 296. **MERGE-RUN GATES (all six CI jobs green on the first run):** Pest **1965 passed / 7043 assertions** — identical to local, so the `CENTRAL_DOMAIN` override reproduced CI exactly this time; Vitest **48 files**; **`design-system-a11y` 163 tests passed**, which is the number the host-driven stand-in reported story-for-story and is therefore evidence the substitution was faithful rather than merely convenient; Playwright **261 passed** in 9.5 min with **no flaky** (the axe races that fired in H24a's merge run did not recur); contract-tests clean, confirming the byte-identical `openapi.json`.

- **H24a (as-built, MERGED PR #86 `5f9401d`, 6/6 CI green first try) — the analytics substrate.** ADR-0011 implemented rather than re-litigated: no event stream, no beacon, no rollup table; aggregation over submission **metadata**, live under RLS; charting still H24b's. **A post-approval design review found THREE real defects in the approved plan, each verified before folding in. (1) `scopeCountable()` alone would NOT have satisfied §D2** — a local scope applies only at the query ROOT, so a query rooted on `submission_answer_index` inherits neither it nor the SoftDeletes global scope, which is the exact silent over-count D2 exists to prevent. **(2) The `E2eSeeder` tier change would have SILENTLY NO-OPPED** — the block is guarded by an `exists()` check, so CI (fresh DB) goes green while every developer's box stays on Professional and analytics 402s; it ships as an upsert, verified by re-seeding the existing dev DB and reading the plan back. **(3) `date_trunc('day', submitted_at)` is NOT SAFE HERE** — `config/database.php` sets no `timezone` key, so the session `TimeZone` GUC is whatever `postgresql.conf` says (UTC in the container, unknown on ADR-0005's box); the same row buckets differently on different hosts and a saved view is not reproducible. Every bucket uses the **three-argument** `date_trunc(field, source, zone)` (PG 16+; we run postgis:17-3.5) with a validated IANA zone carried on the query object, pinned by a test asserting a 22:00 UTC row lands on the **next** day in Asia/Manila. **TWO MUTATIONS CAME BACK GREEN AND BOTH INDICTED THE CODE — the fourth and fifth increments running where the useful mutation result was a green one.** Deleting the soft-delete half of `applyCountableJoin()` reddened nothing across 31 tests because it had **zero callers** (the aggregator roots on `submissions` and joins OUTWARD to the index, which is strictly better and made the helper dead — deleted, along with a docblock that had drifted into claiming "every join goes through it"); and deleting `TenantContext::applyLocal()` from the export closure reddened nothing because the rows were materialized in REQUEST scope, making all three of §D13's "load-bearing" lines ceremony — the aggregation moved inside the closure and the mutation now reddens 2 tests. A third mutation stays green and indicts the TEST rather than the line, recorded as such in the docblock. **`'can:viewAny,'.SavedReportView::class` SILENTLY AUTHORIZED NOTHING** — `routes/api.php` has no `namespace`, so with the import missing it compiles to the bare string `'SavedReportView'` with no error; Laravel's `Authorize` decides class-vs-route-parameter by testing for a backslash, treated it as a parameter, resolved `null`, and Gate denied without ever reaching a policy. Forcing the policy to `return true` changed nothing, which is what pointed at the argument rather than the policy. **Two testing traps now documented in the suites:** the auth guard **caches** its resolved user, so a second `withToken()` in one test does not re-authenticate (both tokens valid, second request keeps acting as the first owner — an authorization test that always passes); and after a request the tenant GUC is torn down, so an in-process assertion reads "row deleted" when it means "row invisible". **The index addendum stayed ONE by WIDENING rather than adding** — `submissions_analytics_series_idx` on `(tenant_id, submitted_at) INCLUDE (form_id) WHERE status <> 'draft' AND deleted_at IS NULL`, plus H12a's capacity index widened in place to `(tenant_id, form_id, submitted_at)` (strict prefix-superset, so the cap COUNT is unaffected). `deleted_at IS NULL` is what makes an index-only scan possible; its corollary is a **standing rule: no analytics query may use `withTrashed()` on `submissions`**. Shape pinned **structurally, not by `EXPLAIN`** — `ScopeNodeScaleTest:120-128` already recorded that on a small table the planner tie-breaks unpredictably, so a plan-shape assertion measures the cost model, not the property. **Other shapes:** the subtree predicate was **extracted, not copied a third time** (`ResourceGrantResolver::subtreeFormIdsQuery()`, `nodeReach()` its second call site) and was not even copyable since `inactivePaths()` is private; §D6's scope-node axis needs **three** dispositions — forms on a deactivated node fold into *Unassigned* rather than being dropped, or `sum(buckets) < total` on one screen; §D5's denominator is restricted to `source IN (guest, manual)`, a forward-dated defect fixed before it lands (every OCR-staged draft would otherwise turn "median first-save→submit" into encoder review latency); `AnalyticsFieldEligibility` is a `default`-less 31-case match **verified by mutation** to produce `match.unhandled` at L8, and both `hidden` and `calculated` are Indexable, classified by what the projector does rather than by what feels like respondent data. **Gates: Pest 1961 (+86 over H21d2's 1875), ZERO failures, 6998 assertions (the 2 warnings are pre-existing); PHPStan L8 exactly 55 — the documented baseline, zero introduced** (9 `User|null` errors appeared and were narrowed with the `DashboardController` device); Pint clean; `controller-gate` 59 files (parsing lives in FormRequests, which it does not scan); `migration-lint` 40; **`openapi.json` regenerated + Redocly-clean, and the diff includes the ABILITY ENUM in the token-mint schema, not only the eight new paths**; `QueuedMailContractTest` re-run green with `deployment-infrastructure.md` deliberately untouched. **MERGE-RUN GATES (all six CI jobs green on the first run):** Pest **1962 passed** (7020 assertions, +87 over H21d2’s 1875), PHPStan **0 on CI** (the local 55 are phantoms — there is no baseline file, so CI green *is* zero), Playwright **260 passed / 1 flaky** absorbed by `retries: 1`, contract-tests clean. **THE TZ-ENUM LANDMINE DID NOT FIRE BUT IS PERMANENT:** `AnalyticsReportRequest:52`’s `Rule::in(DateTimeZone::listIdentifiers())` puts a **419-entry enum × 4 endpoints** (~1,676 lines) into `openapi.json`, and `contract-tests` byte-diffs it — local PHP is `php:8.4-fpm-alpine` 8.4.23 / tzdata 2026.1 while CI’s `shivammathur/setup-php` **floats** `8.4`. They matched today; a future PHP patch bumping timelib turns the job red on a diff nobody authored. Backlog row filed. **One defect fixed on the way in:** `DashboardController`’s comment claimed the `trends` prop was “pinned by a Pest assertion” and it was not — coverage was service-level only, so a controller-side regression would have been invisible to every gate until H24b. Now pinned key-by-key at the response layer on a FREE tenant, which holds the §D5 ungated line too.

- **H1e (as-built, MERGED PR #85 `3d8f1f3`, 6/6 CI green first try) — ADR-0011, the analytics substrate.** The last unblocked row of the ADR wave, named by *applying* the H-map blockers rather than picking: H22b is blocked by H22a and H23b by H23a, leaving H1e / H22a / H23a / H25 eligible — and H1e alone blocks a vertical (H24a + H24b, whose every other dependency is already merged). **The headline decision is a REVERSAL of its own H-map row 259: there is NO form-engagement event stream in Phase 3.** No `form_events`, no unauthenticated Group-C beacon, no Dexie telemetry queue, no rollup table, no partitioning problem — because none of `PRD.md:203-206`'s three Phase-3 acceptance criteria (cross-form aggregation, saved report views, streamed export) needs one, and the alternative was a fourth unauthenticated ingress class writing the highest-volume table in the system onto a single self-hosted box. **Row 259 and its hazard note in the H-map file are rewritten in place** so the next session cannot build the beacon by accident. **Thirteen sub-decisions**; four are load-bearing enough to restate here. **(1) The two Phase-1 metrics at `PRD.md:198` are AMENDED, not left aspirational** — "started vs completed" is uncomputable for *any* form, because a respondent who abandons leaves no server row at all (a `submissions` row appears only on submit, or on an explicit "Save and finish later" which needs both the tenant entitlement and the form's own flag), and that self-selected population is *hard-deleted* nightly by the draft reaper — so a naive completion funnel returns **100% on a default-configured form and rises every night as the reaper deletes its own denominator**. What ships instead is a draft-conversion rate and a median first-save→submit, both printing their denominator on the tile and both suppressed rather than estimated beyond the retention window; the durable marker is `submissions.last_saved_at IS NOT NULL`, which survives promotion because `promote()` rewrites only `status`/`submitted_at`/`draft_expires_at` and the fresh-submit path never writes it. **(2) Cross-form aggregation is promised over submission METADATA, not answer values** — `submission_answer_index` is opt-in (`is_queryable` defaults false in the migration, in `FormBuilderService`, in every seeder, and is hard-coded false by `FieldLibrary::toBlueprintField()`), top-level-scalar-only, written only at finalize, and **never backfilled**, so flipping the flag indexes future submissions only *and* requires a new version (`SchemaChangeClassifier` classifies it `indexing changed`). For essentially every form alive today an answer-value chart returns nothing; that is disclosed up front rather than discovered by the first customer. Scalar *includes* `likert_scale` and excludes both grid types, multi/cascading select, media, and anything nested in a repeatable section — and the publish gate lets an author flag a field that can never project, so the authoring surface must refuse the promise. **(3) Charting is IN-HOUSE design-system SVG, no library** — the decisive argument is repo-specific and was verified rather than asserted: `build-tokens.mjs` emits `var()` **references**, not resolved colours, and `useTheme` flips `data-theme-mode`/`data-accent` live on the root element, so an SVG mark re-paints for free on a theme change while a canvas library goes stale until someone wires an observer; and a design-system component with a story sits under the merge-blocking axe job by construction while a library inside `resources/js` does not. The categorical series scale is capped at **five**, *derived* from the luminance band that satisfies 3:1 against both the light and the dark surface — which is also why the dark re-point of the series tokens is mandatory, not optional — with the sixth-and-beyond folded into a neutral "Other (N)" bucket. Also recorded: **axe cannot detect a missing text alternative on an SVG that has a plausible `aria-label`**, so the data-table contract is pinned by a Vitest assertion, not by the gate. **(4) The surface is HIDDEN, not locked-with-upsell**, because Business is `is_active = false` and an upgrade CTA would point at a plan nobody can buy; the same fact means H24b's e2e axe fixture must seed a Business-entitled tenant or the new surface ships with zero coverage while the gate stays green. **Four false statements in the corpus were corrected in the same PR** (the standing lesson, fourth instance): `feature-backlog.md` claimed "Analytics-tab shell + chart tokens already exist" — neither exists in any form; ADR-0001 and `technical-architecture.md` asserted "JSONB + GIN" as the hybrid submission model's *built* performance story when **no GIN index exists anywhere** (`submission_answers` has one B-tree, on `(tenant_id, form_version_id)`); `data-dictionary.md` filed `scope_node_id` under §1 `tenants` although its own text says "this form" (moved to §2 `forms`, together with the six schedule/save-and-resume columns that section was missing); and the gating matrix's analytics row was the one capability row that never named its code key. **New standing gotcha — a docs-only PR CAN redden CI.** `ci.yml` carries no `paths:` filter, so all six jobs run in full, and two can bite on markdown: `QueuedMailContractTest` asserts `docs/deployment-infrastructure.md` contains the `--queue=` worker order **byte-exactly** (the only test in the suite that reads a doc — and that paragraph is precisely the queue catalog an analytics ADR is tempted to amend), and gitleaks scans `.md` with the upstream default ruleset and no `.gitleaks.toml`, so an illustrative token or DSN inside an ADR fails `static-analysis`.

- **H21d2 (as-built, MERGED PR #84 `af31026`, 6/6 CI green first try)** — **the structured condition editor** (H-map row 254's WRITE half; **row 254 is now closed**). Doc #27 → **v1.5**, amendments **E1–E5**. **Three decisions locked with the user up front. (1) ONE CLASSIFIER, BY CONSTRUCTION.** H21d1 recorded that `status: 'opaque'` is the predicate the editor needs — but reusing it meant more than calling it: the classification was **fused into the prose** (`say()` returning `null` WAS "opaque") and every helper was module-private, so the only reachable predicate took raw text and computed a sentence as a side effect. So it MOVED: new `condition-model.ts` owns `toCondition(Node) → Condition | null`, and `condition-describer.ts` now renders its sentence FROM that model, making `say()` **total**. Describable ≡ representable definitionally, not by treaty. **The price is stated rather than hidden** — the model must be exactly as expressive as H21d1's set, quirks included (`18 < ${age}` reversed, `${end} > ${start}` field-vs-field, `1 = 1`), because a tidier model would have **silently narrowed the RAIL's prose**; that is why both sides of a comparison are the same control. The guarantee: **`condition-describer.test.ts` passes byte-unchanged**, all 27 cases. **(2) `ConfigPanel` REPLACED, one control in two derived modes** (the `ValidationEditor` precedent — mode computed from the value, never stored): blank/describable → structured rows with an "Edit as text" escape; opaque/invalid → the raw textarea alone, tree not mounted. One stored value, one editor on screen, so §8's "which one wins when they disagree" is answered **by construction** rather than by a precedence rule. **Read-only constrains the ROWS, not the author** — an opaque condition keeps a fully editable textarea, because removing the only way to edit `${age} + 1 > 18` would be a regression dressed as a safety measure. **(3) ONE PR, the whole write path** (the round-trip obligation is "every shape the editor can author", so a model-only PR would owe a test it could not write). **NO NEW WRITE ENDPOINT — the 409 is inherited, not re-implemented.** `relevant_expression` was already on both PATCH payloads with its `version` token, so the editor writes `target.relevant_expression = text; store.touch(uid, kind)` and gets the 600 ms debounce, snapshot dirty-check, version bump, `ConflictDialog` routing and one undo entry per burst with **zero store changes**. §9's "409 test on the new write path" is therefore **redirected and the redirection recorded** (E3): `builderClient.test.ts` is **the first JS-side coverage `builderClient` has ever had** (the 409 was pinned server-side only), plus a store-level group driving a condition edit against a stubbed 409 and asserting BOTH sides land and the stale token is NOT adopted. **THE SERIALIZER CHECKS ITSELF.** `serialize()` re-parses its own output and returns text only if it classifies back to a structurally equal `Condition` — which turns the lexer's 2000-byte / 500-token / 64-depth budgets into one refusal instead of three constants that would drift. Load-bearing because **nothing validates a `relevant_expression` on write**, so bad printed text saves cleanly and ambushes the author at publish. **Three shapes are REFUSED rather than mangled**, each a real limit: a value containing BOTH `'` and `"` (the lexer has **no escape sequences at all**), a number needing exponent notation (`String(1e21)` is `"1e+21"`), and an unusable key. **THE DRIFT SURFACE §8 NAMED IS GUARDED BY A SHARED FIXTURE.** `tests/fixtures/condition-serializer.json` (27 cases) is driven THREE ways — TS `serialize` → text, TS `parse` → condition, and **PHP's `ExpressionParser` in `ConditionSerializerParityTest.php`**, which is the one that catches text only the TS parser accepts. Outside `tests/golden/`, no `grammar_version` key ⇒ **corpora byte-untouched: 296 sites, 114 vectors, `git diff tests/golden` empty**. **Not a sixth R3 mirror pair** — one printer, one language, PHP never prints. **No migration, no `/api/v1` change ⇒ `openapi.json` verified drift-free.** **SIX MUTATIONS RUN, AND THE ONE THAT REDDENED NOTHING WAS THE USEFUL ONE.** Deleting the printer's deep-equality self-check left all 106 tests green — the length-budget case that looks like it exercises the arm is caught one branch earlier by the re-parse *throwing*. The comparison arm IS reachable: `compare eq <field> <empty text>` prints `${a} = ''`, which parses back as the **emptiness idiom**, a different condition. Now pinned, paired with the `blank` condition that legitimately prints the same string so the refusal is a discrimination. **Third increment running where a green mutation indicted the suite, not the code.** **THE `token-references` FLAKE HID A REAL FAILURE AGAIN** — the isolated re-run reported a dangling `--mds-color-text-primary` in the new legend (no such token; the family is `text-body`/`-heading`/`-secondary`/`-muted`). **Two component-test findings were also real**, the notable one being that the editor read its own sentence with no `LabelLookup` and said "age is more than 18" where the rail says "Your age is more than 18" — one reading rendered two ways, the exact divergence the shared classifier exists to prevent. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1875 (+29), ZERO failures; PHPStan L8 55 — exactly the documented local baseline, zero in any H21d2-touched file**; Pint 795; `composer audit` CLEAN; vue-tsc both tsconfigs; **Vitest 974 (+183)** (the 2 failures are the known `token-references` full-suite flake, verified passing in isolation); Vite build — **the admin bundle is unchanged at 141.66 kB and the editor lands in the lazily-resolved `Builder` chunk (79.40 → 95.16 kB)**; `openapi.json` drift-free. **Playwright: 12 new tests carrying 18 axe scans, all green** — 6 `builder-axe` (three viewports x light/dark, each scanning BOTH editor modes and traversing the tree by keyboard) + 6 `responsive-axe` over the seeded nested-group condition. **The full `builder-axe` matrix is 36/36** across all three viewports in both themes, which is the first time it has been clean end to end since H21d1 fixed the dark primary-fill palette. `Logic Notices Demo` gains a multi-select with real options and `(${age} > 18 or ${age} < 5) and selected(${colours}, 'red')`, so one scan sees the choice dropdown, the recursive group control and its indent rule. **Docs: no new number** — Doc #27 → v1.5 with E1–E5, plus reconciliations to `testing-strategy.md`, `architecture/technical-architecture.md` (R3 gains a THIRD KIND of drift surface: a place where syntax is *constructed*), `feature-backlog.md` and `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md` (the workflow-canvas row goes ◐ → ✓).
- **H21d1 (as-built, MERGED PR #83 `774b9ae`, 6/6 CI green after two red cycles that were both REAL — see the gate findings)** — **the read-derived logic canvas** (H-map row 254; classified `ui, **XL**` there, split by H1f into **H21d1** / **H21d2**, and **only H21d1 ships here**). Doc #27 → **v1.4**, amendments **D1–D5**, each inline beside the clause it corrects. **Three decisions locked with the user up front. (1) H21d1 ONLY** — the read half is complete on its own, so the split falls on a real seam: a canvas that writes nothing needs no serializer, no write endpoint and no 409 surface, and every §8 obligation belonging to the editor is left STANDING rather than quietly narrowed. **(2) A `Structure ⇄ Logic` TOGGLE IN THE CENTRE PANE**, not a page — one `useBuilderStore`, so the rail is live and undo/redo is intact, and selecting a node drives the existing `ConfigPanel`, so an author reads the map and edits in place instead of moving between two editors. **(3) RAW TEXT *AND* PLAIN ENGLISH, WITH A HARD FALLBACK.** **No migration, no job, no `/api/v1` change** ⇒ `openapi.json` verified drift-free (web route; Scramble is scoped to `api/v1`); **corpora byte-untouched — `grammar_version` still exactly 296 sites, the validation manifest still 114 vectors, `git diff tests/golden` empty**. **THE LOAD-BEARING ARCHITECTURAL DECISION IS THE SPLIT BETWEEN WHAT IS DERIVED WHERE.** The rail — node order, each raw predicate, its reading, its `${key}` chips, its syntax errors — is derived CLIENT-side from the store and re-parsed between keystrokes with the TS `ExpressionParser` off the engine barrel (the **second** crossing from `resources/js/` into `resources/public-runtime/`, after H21c's `Encode.vue`). The three §6 notices stay on the SERVER behind a new read-only **`GET /forms/{form}/graph`** sidecar (the ScopeNode `impact`/`grants` precedent), refetched after `store.whenIdle()` settles and debounced. **PORTING `StepGraphInspector` TO TYPESCRIPT WAS REJECTED OUTRIGHT** — a sixth R3-style mirror pair with no corpus standing over it, the hazard Doc #27 §9 and amendment A6 argue against at length. The price is a one-save lag, and it is **SHOWN** (a "rechecking" line, and a failed check that says so) rather than hidden, because a stale clean bill of health that LOOKS current is the failure worth avoiding. The refetch is keyed on a **logic fingerprint** (keys, sequences, containment, expressions), not the whole store: a label edit cannot create or clear a cycle, and re-asking per keystroke into a placeholder is a request for an answer that cannot have moved. **A LIVE DEFECT WAS FOUND WHILE PLANNING AND FIXED ON THE WAY IN** (amendment D3): H21d1 is the first caller to run the inspector against an **unvalidated draft**, and only one of its three checks was safe there — `referencesIn()` catches `Throwable` with a docblock naming H21d1 as the reason, but **`emptyAtOpen()` did not**, reaching `ExpressionParser::parse()` through `SemanticValidator` → `evaluateBoolean()`. Unreachable on the publish path (`ExpressionValidationGate` refuses first, inside `publish()`), which is why it never fired in H21a; the **ordinary** case against a draft. **Both settle loops can raise it** (sections are swept separately from fields), so the guard wraps the whole evaluation and both arms are pinned separately — and **both regression tests were run against the unfixed code first**, both throwing the predicted `ExpressionSyntaxException`. **THE NOTICES BECAME ADDRESSABLE RATHER THAN COPIED:** `notices()` returns `GraphNotice` records naming their nodes, `warnings()` is the flash-shaped grouping wrapper reproducing H21a's three sentences byte-for-byte — now pinned in FULL rather than by needle, since the older cases assert with `toContain`, which cannot see a dropped clause (and takes **varargs needles**, the H17 trap). The record carries **two** strings by design: the banner wants one line per kind joining every instance, the canvas wants each instance under its node, and the cycle case proves one string cannot be both — its fragment is `“a” ⇄ “b”`, which reads as nothing alone. Writing the exact-array assertion caught what the needle test could not see: **§4.1's own empty-at-open fixture is ALSO a containment cycle**, so it emits two banner lines. **THE DESCRIBER'S REFUSAL IS THE DELIVERABLE.** One operand it cannot express makes the WHOLE reading opaque — no partial readings — and grouping is preserved rather than flattened, because `(A or B) and C` and `A or (B and C)` are different conditions and rendering both the same way is a confident wrong answer about a form that decides who is asked what. That classifier is exactly the predicate **H21d2** needs for §8's "non-representable ⇒ read-only, never rewritten", built once instead of twice; nothing here PRINTS an expression, so the AST→text printer H21d2 owes still does not exist. **THREE GATE FINDINGS, EACH FROM A DIFFERENT GATE AND EACH REAL:** (a) a `var(--mds-type-body-font-size)` that does not exist, reported by `token-references` — **which fails as a timeout in the full Vitest run**, so it was visible only on the isolated re-run, which is the whole reason that practice exists; (b) a genuine dark-mode `color-contrast` violation in new copy (`--mds-color-danger-text` is `danger-300` in dark: **4.22:1** on `bg-surface` at 13px; the `status-*-fg` family is the on-surface pair at 8.43/6.39); (c) the mutation pass surfaced raw ASCII separator bytes (0x1D/0x1E/0x1F) in the fingerprint source — right delimiting, invisible in an editor, now written as escapes. **One test was CORRECTED rather than made to pass** — a two-field comparison had been written as opaque on a wrong premise. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1846 (+13), ZERO failures; PHPStan L8 55 — exactly the documented local baseline, zero in any H21d1-touched file** (one `arrayValues.list` error appeared and was **fixed**, returning the count to baseline rather than waiving it); Pint 794; `composer audit` CLEAN; controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint; vue-tsc both tsconfigs; **Vitest 791 (+63)** (the 2 failures are the known `token-references` full-suite flake, verified passing in isolation); Vite build — **the admin bundle is unchanged at 141.66 kB and the parser lands in the lazily-resolved `Builder` chunk (69.11 → 79.40 kB), measured against `main` rather than asserted**; `openapi.json` drift-free. **Playwright: the 12 new logic-view scans are green** (6 `builder-axe` + 6 `responsive-axe`, three viewports × light/dark, over a seeded `Logic Notices Demo` draft carrying every state the rail can draw at once); a full `builder-axe` run is 28/30 and both failures are the pre-existing dark `.mds-button--primary` contrast, **verified to reproduce identically on `main`**. **Docs: no new number** (the H-map gives H21d no docs row) — Doc #27 → v1.4 with D1–D5, plus reconciliations to `testing-strategy.md`, `architecture/technical-architecture.md` (R3: the describer and the `${key}` walk are **not** mirrors, and what WOULD have been one was deliberately not built), `feature-backlog.md` and `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md`.
- **H21c (as-built, MERGED PR #82 `061d1ed`, 6/6 CI green — one red cycle that was a Docker Hub outage on the runner, not the code)** — **the encode path's step model** (H-map row 253; classified `full-stack, M` there, corrected to **L** by H1f/Doc #27 §7 and shipped **L**). Doc #27 → **v1.3**, amendments **C1–C5**, each inline beside the clause it corrects. **Closes H1f's defect (b)**, the ninth and last — and corrects the tally while doing so: v1.2 claimed all nine were closed by H21a and H21b, but eight were, and the encode prune was still live at `SubmissionController.php:42` (a bare `$pipeline->submit(...)` with its `SubmissionResult` discarded) when this opened. **No migration, no job, no `/api/v1` change** ⇒ `openapi.json` drift-free; **corpora byte-untouched — `grammar_version` still exactly 296 sites, validation manifest still 114 vectors, `git diff tests/golden` empty**. **Three decisions locked with the user up front. (1) THE ENCODE PAGE MOUNTS `createFormRuntime()`** rather than getting a projection of its own: the store imports only `vue`, `../engine`, `../lib/schema-mapping`, `../lib/prefill`, `../lib/uuid`, `../lib/types` — no api-client, no Dexie, no DOM — so client-side parity is ONE implementation, not two that agree, and the whole of H21b's hardening (terminal state, rescue by identity, `goToStep`'s four resolutions, retain-and-restore) arrives free. **(2) THE KEYABLE-HIDDEN ASYMMETRY IS RESOLVED OUTSIDE THE STEP LIST** — a labelled "Reference fields" block — rather than by giving `StepProjection` and the TS store an audience axis, because keeping the two channels' step lists byte-identical is what makes §9's parity assertion a clean equality. **(3) THE PAYLOAD IS ADDITIVE**: `blocks` stays the render source and the PHP↔TS normalizer duplication it carries is deferred BY NAME in §10 rather than collapsed. **LANDING ORDER WAS SPECIFIED AND FOLLOWED** — §7 says the cheap half "should not wait", so the controller change shipped as its own bisectable commit before any engine work. **THE PARITY SUITE ASSERTS THE INPUT, NOT TWO PROJECTIONS COMPARED** (amendment C4): once the encode page mounts the same store, "both channels produce X" is a tautology about one function wearing a cross-channel costume, so `EncodeStepParityTest` — the shared fixture's THIRD consumer, all 16 cases built as REAL published forms — asserts instead that the encode payload's `version.schema` is byte-identical to `PublicFormPresenter`'s and that rebuilding from that shipped snapshot reproduces each case's taken path. **MUTATION TESTING'S MOST USEFUL RESULT WAS AGAIN A GREEN ONE**: passing `StepProjection` the OMITTED-filtered field list reddens NOTHING, because `EncodeFormPresenter::OMITTED` = {`page_break`,`calculated`} is a strict subset of `PdfFieldRole::Omitted` = {`hidden`,`calculated`,`page_break`} — a relation nothing enforced, between two lists answering different questions. A test now pins it directly. Four more PHP mutations and five Vue ones each reddened a distinct set; the field-level relevance case was ADDED because the section-level one passed against a page that only filtered steps. **Other shapes worth carrying:** the session clock is stamped by the SERVER on this channel (`version.now`) and not by `isoClock(new Date())`, so the server-rendered `steps` and the client's first `visibleSteps` cannot disagree across midnight or a device skew; **Next never blocks** (a keyer transcribing paper may not have the answer, and `attemptNext()` would be a new refusal on a channel whose contract is "the pipeline is the sole authority"), which is *why* Submit raises a form-wide summary whose jump links change step before they focus; the POST body is the FULL answer map, not `effectiveAnswers`, because that is what keeps the prune report meaningful as a divergence alarm; `preserveState` is a predicate, not a constant (a 422 must keep what was typed, a success must not); and `SubmissionPdfPresenter` is deliberately NOT refactored onto `StepProjection` — "shown, and empty" is a sentence only the PDF can form (H6b's A9 notice), so §9's "collapse" ships as the cross-check §9 actually asks for in words. Writing that cross-check caught a real trap: reading the stored document through the wrong relation yields `[]`, every gate reads ABSENT, and the check passes for the one case whose branches are all off. **AN ADVERSARIAL PRE-MERGE REVIEW FOUND THREE REAL DEFECTS, ALL THREE CREATED BY THE DECISION TO MOUNT THE STORE** (amendment C5; 15 raised, 11 refuted, 4 confirmed — two of them the same defect from two lenses). **(a)** The pruned-answer report read a NEIGHBOUR's relevance mask: `repeatFieldRelevance` is index-aligned to the NORMALIZED instance list while the report walked the RAW map, and Stage 1 drops an empty instance and re-indexes — so every dropped row shifted every later mask, naming an answer that was persisted while missing the one actually pruned. Reachable on the ordinary flow (`addInstance()` pushes `{}`, this increment seeds `min_instances` rows, clearing a row's last value deletes the key). Fixed by re-running Stage 1 inside the report and walking the NORMALIZED document, rather than reconstructing which rows were dropped — which would have put a second copy of "what counts as unanswered" in a class with no business owning one. **(b)** `fixed`-prefill hidden values started reaching the payload — the report's docblock asserted they never do, which was TRUE on `main` and which mounting the store falsified — so a server-authored value the keyer never saw was reported as an answer that "was not saved". Fixed by asking *could the keyer have typed this*, which excludes `fixed` and deliberately keeps `url` (on this channel the keyer IS its source). **(c)** The error-summary jump links looked for `field-<address>`, the id the GUEST runtime stamps; this page renders `FieldInput` directly and `MdsFormField` derives its id from `useId()`, so every lookup returned null — the jump changed step and then did nothing, and in single-page mode was inert. Fixed with the page's own `encode-field-<address>` wrapper, focusing the first focusable INSIDE it because a plain `div` ignores `.focus()`. **THE METHODOLOGICAL FINDING IS THE ONE TO CARRY: the first regression test written for (a) PASSED against the broken code.** With one surviving and one pruned row, the broken version names the survivor against its neighbour's mask and the fixed version names the pruned row — and both render the identical string. Two pruned rows separate them by COUNT. Every regression test here was run against the pre-fix code before being believed, and that is the only reason it was caught. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1833 (+42), ZERO failures; PHPStan L8 **55 total, exactly the documented local baseline** — four land in H21c-touched files and all four are the documented model-property phantoms at SHIFTED line numbers, each verified present verbatim on `main` (`respondentUserId: $user->id`, `User::$name`/`$email`, `$field->default_value`), so the increment introduced zero; Pint 791; `composer audit` CLEAN; vue-tsc both tsconfigs; Vitest 728 passed (+17)** (the 2 failures are the known `token-references` full-suite timeout flake, verified passing in isolation); Vite build — **the engine code-splits into its own `useFormRuntime-*.js` chunk (65 kB) rather than entering the 141 kB admin bundle**, since Encode.vue is a lazily-resolved Inertia page; `openapi.json` drift-free; **Playwright 24/24 encode scans green across all three viewports**, including the two pre-existing ones (`Clinic Intake` projects to a single step; `Household Roster` is `single_page_mode: true`, so neither locator moved). A full local run also reports `builder-axe` dark and one offline-conflict failure — **verified to reproduce identically on `main`**, so neither is this branch's, and both are recorded as gotchas rather than chased (CI is the authority on e2e; PR #81 was 6/6 green on that same tree). **Docs: no new number** (the H-map gives H21c no docs row) — Doc #27 → v1.3 with C1–C4, plus reconciliations to `ux/form-filling-ux-flow.md` (§1's parity claim marked as-built and its two surviving divergences named), `data-dictionary.md` (H7's read/render asymmetry now has a named home), `testing-strategy.md` (H21c's five suites, and §7's "mutation testing — not currently planned" line CORRECTED: no tool is adopted, but a hand-run mutation pass is now standing practice and has paid for itself in all three H21 slices), `architecture/technical-architecture.md` §R3 (the fifth mirror pair stays PHP↔TS and does not become a triangle), and `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md` (+2 rows).
- **H21b (as-built, MERGED PR #81 `6f8845b`, 6/6 CI green on the first run)** — **guest-runtime non-linear execution** (H-map row 252; classified `ui, M` there, **shipped M/L** because the scope taken was the whole of Doc #27 §9's H21b obligation rather than only the five live defects — recorded here rather than hidden, on the H6b/H21a precedent). Doc #27 → **v1.2**, amendments **B1–B7**, each inline beside the clause it corrects. **All nine of H1f's recorded defects are now closed** — four by H21a, five here — so **H21c inherits nothing from H1f**. **No migration, no job, no `/api/v1` change** ⇒ `openapi.json` verified drift-free; **corpora byte-untouched — `grammar_version` still exactly 296 sites, `git diff tests/golden` empty**. **Three decisions locked with the user up front. (1) FULL §9 SCOPE**, adding §4.2's rescue-by-identity, §5.3's resume-drift resolution, §5.2's respondent-facing-percentage drop and §4.4's step-level notice to the six defects named in the prompt. **(2) THE REPLAY VERSION GUARD READS `client.fetchSchema()`**, not the token's base64url `vid` claim and not a new field on the mint JSON: `MintResponse` does not carry the minted version, so there was no zero-cost realisation of "compare against the freshly-minted token's", and a schema read costs one GET per form per pass (memoized **per pass**, never module-level, so a republish between drains is seen) while keeping the SPA off `GuestShareTokenService`'s wire format. **(3) THE GUARD APPLIES TO RESUMED-DRAFT ROWS TOO**, accepted as a **recorded narrowing**: the server's draft path skips its own version guard (version-safe by construction — `promote()` re-asserts it), so such a row now parks for review where it would have promoted. It fails safe, costs one review click, and the alternative was a `from_draft` flag on `OutboxRow` read by exactly one branch. **THE FIX FOR THE NEVER-SEEDED `currentStepKey` WAS THE WATCHER'S SOURCE, NOT A BETTER GUARD** — and this is the finding that shaped the store rewrite. It watched `visibleSteps.some(s => s.key === currentStepKey)`, a boolean that is `false` at t=0 on an empty form and **stays** `false` when steps appear; a Vue watcher fires on change, so it never ran at all, the index stayed −1, `attemptNext()` failed its `idx >= 0` guard and returned `advanced: true` with no key change — Next dead forever. One `watch(visibleSteps, (next, prev) => …)` now does four things (seed-or-self-heal · rescue by identity · record the visited set · publish a `StepChange`), and watching the list hands the handler `prev` for free, which is exactly the identity anchor the rescue needs and the current list cannot supply. **`isLastStep` gained `length > 0 &&`** (the `-1 === -1` accident) **and a new `isTerminal` computed carries the view**, because with the guard in place `isLastStep` is correctly *false* at zero steps and the template would otherwise render **Next** instead of §4.1's single labelled Submit. **MUTATION TESTING FOUND A REAL HOLE IN THE PLAN, NOT JUST IN A TEST: §4.2's FORWARD WALK WAS PINNED BY NOTHING.** Doc #27 says "walk outward" but illustrates only the backward case; deleting the forward arm reddened **zero** tests, because no fixture had a vanished step with no surviving predecessor. Two tests were added (a respondent on the FIRST step when it removes itself; the same shape on the resume path) and the mutation then reddened exactly those two — recorded as **amendment B2**, which corrects this document's own specification. Twelve mutations in total, each reddening a distinct set; making the replay schema memo module-global reddens three, which is the cross-test bleed that is also the real bug per-pass scoping prevents. **Other shapes worth carrying:** §4.4's focus rescue could NOT live in `SectionView` the way `FieldRow`'s does — a field survives its own content because the control is `v-if`'d inside a wrapper that stays mounted, whereas a section IS the thing removed — so `PageView` tracks the focused section on the way IN via one `focusin` handler and `SectionView` stamps `data-section-key`; the `<TransitionGroup>` (the repo's first) deliberately omits the standard `position: absolute` on leave, because a section is hundreds of pixels tall and taking it out of flow would draw it over the questions below; `SummaryBanner` gained a `beforeJump` prop because `getElementById` cannot resolve a field on an unmounted step; `RelevanceNote` gained an optional `text` prop so one primitive carries both the field and step scopes; and §5.3's `__lead__` is included in the walk order **only when the version still has section-less fields**, which is what makes case (c) degrade into case (a) the way the doc says. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1791 (+3), ZERO failures** (6477 assertions; the 2 warnings are pre-existing); **PHPStan L8 — 55 total, exactly the documented local baseline, zero in any H21b-touched file** (verified by re-running against a `git stash`ed tree); Pint 786; **`composer audit` CLEAN**; **vue-tsc both tsconfigs**; **Vitest 711 passed (+36)** (the 2 failures are the known `token-references` full-suite timeout flake, verified passing in isolation — it caught a REAL undefined `--mds-type-heading-md-*` token first, since fixed to match `SectionView`'s `h2`); Vite build; `openapi.json` drift-free; **Playwright 16/16 locally**, including the G8c conflict journey the replay change touches. **Docs: no new number** (the H-map gives H21b no docs row) — Doc #27 → v1.2 with B1–B7, plus reconciliations to `ux/form-filling-ux-flow.md` (§3.2 and §4.1 marked as-built;
