§5.2's banner copy; and **§10.1's "no separate announcement for a recount" rule NARROWED**, because branching breaks its premise — a step can now change behind the respondent without any navigation to carry the new N), `testing-strategy.md` (H21b's five suites + the two mutation findings), `data-dictionary.md` (`draft_current_step`'s read-time resolution as-built, and the two drifted docblocks now actually corrected in the migration and `types.ts`), and `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md` (+2 rows, and screen-out's row moved from "mechanism ships with H21" to SHIPPED).
- **H21a (as-built, MERGED PR #80 `a2a6c65`, 6/6 CI green on the first run)** — the **workflow branch backend** (H-map row 251; classified `backend, L` there, corrected to **M** by H1f because the "freeze into `schema_snapshot`" line item is a no-op, and shipped **M/L** — four decisions locked this session widened it, the way H6b recorded its own reclassification rather than hiding it). Doc #27 → **v1.1**, amendments **A1–A7**, each inline beside the clause it corrects. **Net-new:** `App\Support\Forms\StepProjection` + `StepDescriptor`, `App\Services\Forms\StepGraphInspector`, `resources/public-runtime/engine/field-roles.ts`, `resources/public-runtime/lib/schema-mapping.ts::isoClock()`, `tests/fixtures/step-projection.json`, and five test files. **No migration, no job, no `/api/v1` change** ⇒ `openapi.json` verified drift-free (the warnings are computed in the tenant WEB controller, and `config/scramble.php` scopes Scramble to `api/v1`). **Corpora byte-untouched — `grammar_version` still exactly 296 sites, validation still 114**, `git diff` on `tests/golden` empty. **Four decisions locked with the user. (1) THE MIRROR PAIR IS BEHAVIOURAL, NOT SOURCE-PARSING, and it ships in H21a rather than H21c.** Doc #27 §9 prescribed the H17 `PdfFieldRole` device (a Pest test that regex-parses the `.ts`); it was **rejected as vacuous** — that device works because it parses actual DATA (`RENDERS_NOTHING`'s members ARE the rule), whereas a projection's rule is control flow, so parsing it pins a list of predicate NAMES that either side could implement with inverted polarity and still satisfy. Instead a shared fixture outside `tests/golden/` (no `grammar_version` key, so the 296/114 counts do not move) drives 16 cases through BOTH `StepProjection` and `visibleSteps`, each via its own engine. **(2) THE `min_instances` NARROWING IS MIRRORED IN BOTH ENGINES** — and this is the finding that most changed the plan: the bounds check lives in `processRepeats()`, which has a byte-for-byte TS twin that the golden runners also reach, so a PHP-only fix would have been **an R3 break invisible to CI** (every repeat vector in the corpus uses an ordinary `short_text` member). Mirroring forced `RENDERS_NOTHING` down from `lib/schema-mapping.ts` into `engine/field-roles.ts` (the engine must not import from `lib/`; a second copy would be a third definition), with `PdfFieldRoleTest`'s regex repointed — a move its `toBe(1, …)` guard turns into a loud failure rather than a vacuous pass. **(3) `count(${section})` FIXED AT BOTH SCOPES**, not one: the identical always-false trap existed inside a repeat MEMBER's `relevant_expression`, whose context is built from the top-level effective map captured *before* the repeat arrays are merged in. Half-fixing would have shipped the worse defect — the same expression working at one scope and silently failing at the other. **(4) THE CYCLE CHECK USES STRUCTURAL EDGES, not `${key}` references alone** — reference + field→owning-section containment + `skip_if`/`skip_with` edges. Reference-only SCC misses the commonest real cycle (a section gated on a field it CONTAINS, where the gate reads ABSENT forever and the field can never render), while `chained_fixed_point`'s `gate → a → b` stays a DAG and stays silent. **TWO DOC #27 CLAIMS CORRECTED, both found by verification rather than by building: (A1)** `${event_date} >= today()` is **already always-false in BOTH engines** — ordering is numeric-only and `Coercion::NUMERIC_RE` rejects an ISO date — so §3.4's motivating example could never have produced the divergence it described. The clock fix is still right but buys a smaller set (`= today()`, which was true client-side precisely when the operand was EMPTY, plus `count()`/`if()`/`int()`). **(A7)** section and field keys are **NOT globally unique per version** — two independent per-table composite indexes, and every app-level enforcer is table-scoped — so `ExpressionValidationGate.php:57-58`'s comment asserting otherwise is false and is corrected in passing. That is exactly why the section-key seed carries a collision guard: without it, seeding would re-admit the answer of a pruned field, mirrored into both engines so R3 would stay green and nothing would catch it. **THE CLOCK IS UTC AND PHP-SHAPED, and both halves are load-bearing:** the server re-validates at submit under its own UTC clock, so stamping the device OFFSET would make `today()` disagree across the evening window in every UTC+ zone → the client renders a step the server prunes, `passed()` is true because an irrelevant field is never required-checked, and the respondent gets a **201 with silent data loss**; and since both engines return the injected clock verbatim from `now()`, `new Date().toISOString()` (millis + `Z`) would have been a *new* divergence, so `isoClock()` emits Carbon's exact `2026-08-01T12:34:56+00:00`. Frozen at session construction (the H7 `search` precedent that keeps the store DOM-free); a session across midnight keeps its opening date, recorded as a narrowing. **PREDICATE 3 IS SPLIT BY MASK, and getting it wrong annihilates every repeatable step:** `fieldRelevance` is dense over TOP-LEVEL fields only, so a repeat member is *absent* from it (not `false`) — repeat sections read `repeatFieldRelevance` instead, with **zero instances VACUOUSLY visible** because the step is what lets the respondent add the first one. The same predicate gates the server's `min_instances`, which is what closes §4.3 from both directions. It applies to the `__lead__` block too (its fields are top-level), and `step.fieldKeys` is deliberately NOT filtered — that would change `erroredItems` and `attemptNext()` for no gain. **A3's fix is bigger than Doc #27 said:** recomputing the section mask alone *relocates* the bound-exhaustion artifact rather than removing it (the returned field mask was derived under the stale section mask), so the field mask is also tightened by the section gate — a provable no-op at the fixed point, which is why no vector moved. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1788 (+42), ZERO failures** (6460 assertions; the 2 warnings are pre-existing); **PHPStan L8 — 55 total, exactly the documented local baseline, zero in any H21a-touched file**; Pint 786; controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint; **`composer audit` CLEAN**; **vue-tsc both tsconfigs**; **Vitest 675 passed** (the 2 failures are the known `token-references` full-suite timeout flake, verified passing in isolation); Vite build; `openapi.json` drift-free. **Mutation-verified — 7 mutations, each reddening a DISJOINT set, and one of them found a real defect in the test rather than the mechanism:** dropping the post-loop tightening came back **GREEN**, because the assertion had been written as an `if/else` invariant (satisfied by both the fixed and the broken behaviour — the H6b "reachable by two paths pins nothing" shape) *and* because the loop bound `fields + sections + 2` means the FIELD COUNT decides which phase of an oscillation the loop halts on. Probed empirically across three counts: at 3 and 5 fields the tightening is a genuine no-op; at 4 the un-tightened engine returns `sectionRelevance = false` beside `fieldRelevance = true` **and persists that answer**. The fixture was rebuilt at 4 fields with direct assertions, and the mutation then reddened exactly one test per engine. Also proven disjoint: the two `count()` seeds (top-level vs member scope) and the two predicate-3 arms (section vs lead) each redden different tests, so neither pair is merely redundant. **Docs: no new number** (the H-map gives H21a no docs row; the H6a/H7/H17 precedent) — Doc #27 → v1.1 with A1–A7, plus reconciliations to `technical-architecture.md` (§4.3's clock deviation struck as CLOSED + A1's correction; R3 gains the fifth mirror pair as-built **and** a sixth surface — `processRepeats` is dual-engine — plus the note that H21a's own bound-exhaustion fix is R3's "unpinned coincidence" hazard realised), `testing-strategy.md` (the mirror-pair obligation discharged, in a different shape than prescribed, with the reason; H21a's five new suites registered), `data-dictionary.md` (the §3.3 trap struck as FIXED, with the raw-vs-pruned and collision caveats an author needs), and `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md` (+2 rows: `count()`-over-a-repeat branching and the publish-time condition analysis, neither of which any competitor column has).
- **H1f (as-built, MERGED PR #79 `54842c3`, docs-only)** — the **workflow / branching design doc** → **`docs/workflow-branching-design.md` = Doc #27** (next free; `docs/PRD.md:13`'s index topped out at #26 with H1h's Doc #26). **Not an ADR** — the H-map row says "design doc", and **ADR-0010 stays reserved for H1d**. Chosen as the next H-map move because applying every blocker leaves a small eligible set (H1c + H20a/b/c cut to Phase 4; H16a/b/c deferred to Phase 3b; H1d — and therefore H18a/b + H19a/b — blocked on the user's document sample), and of what remains **H1f is the only row that is another row's missing dependency**: the H-map's own ordering paired H17 and H21a as the two rows H6a unblocked, H17 is now done, and H1f gates all four of H21a/b/c/d — the PRD's headline Phase-3 item. Same just-in-time shape as H1g→H15a and H1h→H6a. **No code, no migration, no `ci.yml` change, no `openapi.json` change** — `git diff --stat` is docs-only, and the **golden corpora are byte-untouched: `grammar_version` still exactly 296 sites, validation still 114**. **Eleven decisions locked**, the load-bearing ones being: **(1) the step graph is a TOTAL ORDER FILTERED BY RELEVANCE and the doc declines to call it a graph** — no edge table, no successor column, every "branch" a predicate on a node; the rejected `form_step_edges` costs a tenant-scoped migration + RLS + a new snapshot key + a reachability/termination validator that exists nowhere in `app/`, and would drag R5 back out of H25, against a benefit of exactly one authoring shape ("jump to 7"). **(2) A step is visible iff its section is relevant AND ≥1 of its fields is CURRENTLY relevant** — as built the emptiness test consults `rendersNothing(fieldType)`, a **static type predicate**, and never `fieldRelevance`, so a relevant section whose fields are individually gated off renders a heading + Next + zero questions, which is the grayed-out-skipped step `form-filling-ux-flow.md` §3.2 explicitly rejected, reached through another door; the honest cost (step MEMBERSHIP becomes answer-reactive, the coupling `useFormRuntime.ts:329-334` refused for step TITLES) is stated rather than hidden. **(3) Forward references stay LEGAL, warned not refused** — the piping ban does not transfer because a forward piping hole is *permanently* empty and provably useless while a forward relevance reference is merely *late*; and the retro-gate shape is the real argument: a publish gate is non-retroactive, which sounds safe, but `PublishService` step 9 clones forward, so the **next** publish of a live form fails on a rule its author wrote before the rule existed, and there is no grandfather seam (H5c's `legacyOverrides()` is entitlements-only). **(4) `completeness_percent`'s denominator is PINNED here** (`data-dictionary.md:370` stated none) and stays relevance-UNAWARE — it is *coverage of the authored form*, not position on the taken path; making it relevance-aware would run the full validator on every autosave and would make the inbox column non-comparable between two rows. Knock-on: the respondent-facing "you're 64% done" **is false under branching** (step 3 of 3 at 31% coverage) and is dropped; coverage stays on tenant surfaces. **(5) No new golden vectors — as a CONSEQUENCE, not a concession**: `relevance.json`'s `expected.relevance.sections` map *is* the step projection's input. The step projection becomes a **FIFTH R3 mirror pair, not a fifth corpus** (the H17 `PdfFieldRole` device: a test that PARSES the `.ts`), because a step vector needs a section list, sequences, a repeat topology and a field→section map that the language-neutral vector shape cannot carry — Doc #26 A4's argument. **NINE DEFECTS FOUND WHILE WRITING IT, FIVE OF THEM LIVE, ALL RECORDED AND ASSIGNED, NONE FIXED (docs-only):** (a) **`toSemanticInput()` never passes `now`**, so `today()`/`now()` return ABSENT in the SPA while PHP always stamps a clock — **a live PHP/TS relevance divergence the R3 gate structurally cannot see**, because the golden runner supplies `now` from each vector; a call-site bug, not an evaluator bug → H21a. (b) The **encode path silently drops a keyer's answers** and says "Submission recorded." — it ships no `relevant_expression` at all (zero occurrences in `EncodeFormPresenter`), `makeSemanticValidator` has **zero hits under `resources/js/`**, and `SubmissionController::store()` discards the `SubmissionResult` that would let it report the prune → H21c, whose cheap half (report the pruned keys) is a controller change and should land first. (c) **Offline replay re-mints a token before POSTing**, so the server's version guard compares the current version against itself and can never fire; the outbox row carries `form_version_id` and `checksum` and `replay.ts` reads **neither** → a submission pruned against version N persists against N+1's graph and is marked `synced` → H21b, a `replay.ts`-only change. (d) **`count(${section})` is ALWAYS 0 inside any `relevant_expression`** — the settle context is `array_intersect_key($answers, $relevant)` over top-level FIELD keys only, so the repeat array is pruned before evaluation — while `ExpressionValidationGate` explicitly whitelists the reference and the identical expression works in a `calculated` formula → H21a. (e) **Empty `visibleSteps` renders "Step 1 of 0" over a live Submit that SUCCEEDS**: `findIndex` on `[]` is −1 and `isLastStep` is `-1 === -1` → true; and a form empty at *construction* never seeds `currentStepKey`, so the membership watcher is false→false forever and **Next does nothing, permanently** → H21b. (f) **Submit is a silent dead-end when the failing field is on a NON-CURRENT step** — the banner is step-scoped, so it announces "0 fields need your attention" and renders nothing; near-unreachable pre-H21, routine under it → H21b. (g) The **section and field masks are returned ONE ITERATION APART on bound exhaustion**, and the step model is the first consumer to read both → H21a. (h) **`min_instances` is enforced on a repeatable section that has vanished from `visibleSteps`** → an invisible permanent blocker, same shape as (f) → H21a. (i) **Single-page section removal has no transition, no focus rescue and no announcement** (a bare `v-for`, where the *field* level has all three), and in multi-step mode the retain-and-restore note **never fires for an off-screen step** because no `FieldRow` is mounted — *the data is safe, verified across all four hops; nothing is said* → H21b. **Also corrects two drifted facts:** `technical-architecture.md` §4.3's clock claim is true of PHP only (see (a)); and the migration docblock + `types.ts:341` both assert single-page forms leave `draft_current_step` null, which is false — `RuntimeSession.vue:301` sends it unconditionally and the key is seeded regardless of mode. **Two H-map corrections recorded so H21a is not planned against the wrong shape:** H21a's "frozen into `schema_snapshot`" is a **no-op** (the serializer already emits per-section `relevant_expression`/`sequence`/`is_repeatable` and each field's `section_key` — that *is* the step model), so **H21a is M, not L**, and **H21c is the L**; and H21d's "emit ASTs via `StructuredRuleLowering`" is **not implementable as written** — that class lowers `form_field_validations` rows one-directionally and never touches relevance, and **there is no AST→expression-string printer anywhere in the repo** (verified: every `unparse`/`toExpressionString`/`printAst`/`astToString` hit is the word "unparseable" in an unrelated docblock), so H21d2 must ship a new serializer. **Docs: Doc #27 + reconciliations to nine siblings** — `PRD.md` (index), `ux/form-filling-ux-flow.md` (§3.1/§3.2/§4.1/§5.2 + Related documents), `architecture/technical-architecture.md` (§4.3 ×2, §6.2, R3 → fifth mirror pair + the cycle-parity coincidence, R5 → the H25 move, Appendix A item 5), `data-dictionary.md` (the pinned denominator + the **two undocumented columns** `draft_current_step`/`draft_expires_at`, which had zero occurrences in the file, + the `form_sections.relevant_expression` branching note), `domain-glossary.md` (+**Step**, +**Step graph**, and the existing *Relevant expression* row amended so they point at each other instead of competing), `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md` (+3 rows; it had none for workflow/branching), `testing-strategy.md` (no output-encoding row is owed — branching renders no new surface — but a mirror-pair obligation is), `feature-backlog.md` (screen-out **half-discharged, not closed**; logic-map largely discharged by H21d1), `form-versioning-schema-migration.md` (§3.2 step 2, §4 warn-don't-refuse, §5 the classifier does not diff relevance).
- **H17 (as-built, MERGED PR #78 `aa38a58`, 6/6 CI green on the first run)** — **queued PDF generation** (H-map row 243; `backend+ui, M`, depends H2/H3/H6a, `exports_count` quota). Chosen as the next H-map move because it was the only remaining row carrying a **registered, still-owed obligation from the work just shipped**: Doc #26 §5's table already named its surface, its escaper, its owner and its required test, and `testing-strategy.md` §3 listed it under **Still owed**. **Net-new:** `App\Enums\PdfFieldRole`, `App\Enums\SubmissionPdfOutcome`, `SubmissionPdfPresenter` / `SubmissionPdfRenderer` / `SubmissionPdfStorage` / `SubmissionPdfRequestService`, `App\Services\Attachments\AttachmentScanner`, `App\Support\Forms\LocaleVariant`, `App\Support\Tenancy\TenantUrl`, `App\Jobs\Submissions\GeneratePdfJob`, `SubmissionPdfReadyNotification`, `resources/views/pdf/{submission,_styles}.blade.php` (the FIRST non-Inertia Blade views in the repo), 4 test files. **No migration** (`AttachmentKind::ExportArtifact` already existed, unused), **no `/api/v1` change** ⇒ `openapi.json` verified drift-free — routes are web-only and `config/scramble.php` scopes Scramble to `api/v1` (the H14 precedent, which also dodges the Redocly `operation-summary` rule CI catches and local checks do not). **Four decisions locked with the user. (1) THE TAKEN PATH IS RE-DERIVED, WITH THE CLOCK PINNED** — relevance is persisted NOWHERE (verified across both submission migrations and `SubmissionFinalizer::finalize()`; `SemanticResult`'s masks are computed at submit and discarded), and the only trace is negative and lossy, because `effectiveAnswers()` prunes irrelevant keys while the empty media/geo/composite `unset()`s drop answered-blank ones too — so "key absent" cannot distinguish *never shown* from *shown and skipped*. `validate()` gained a trailing `?string $now` (null keeps every existing caller byte-identical — the H6b A8 device) and the PDF passes the submission's own `finalized_at`; without it a `today()`-dependent `relevant_expression` would be re-evaluated against the day someone clicked Generate. The replay runs over the already-pruned document, so it is a FIXED POINT rather than a literal replay — **pinned directly by an idempotence test**, not argued in a docblock. **(2) STAFF-ONLY, over the existing session-gated `/attachments/{attachment}` route**, so H17 adds no authorisation surface; a respondent-facing receipt is a narrowing, needing a public signed route that exists nowhere in this application. **(3) SUPERSEDE WITHOUT A DELETE** — a deterministic key `tenants/{t}/export_artifact/submissions/{s}.pdf` overwritten in place, one row per submission. `storage_bytes` is a HardBlock gauge summed over non-trashed rows and NOTHING in this codebase reclaims storage (zero `Storage::delete` calls, no reaper), so accumulate-and-delete would have to invent disk reclaim — and there is no safe place to sequence it, because `TenantAwareJob` owns the transaction, exposes no post-commit hook, and `SweepTenantScheduledFormsJob:21-25` records (verified) that `DB::afterCommit` never fires under the suite's outer transaction. Both delete orderings leave a rollback window with a dangling row that charges quota and 404s; overwrite-in-place has none. **(4) Notification + `MailMessage`, NOT the `TenantMail` its own docblock predicted for H17** — the amend-rather-than-implement device: the other predicted consumer (the H9b resume email) already voted this way, all 7 existing emails are Notifications, there is no Blade mail layer at all, and a `TenantMail` is invisible to `job-payload-lint` because it is not `ShouldQueue`. **Quota ordering is load-bearing:** render to memory → scan → charge the **DELTA** (the replaced row already counts, so the full size would bill a regeneration twice) → write. A refusal is CAUGHT and emailed, never rethrown: `$maxExceptions = 3` would re-render three times for a limit that cannot change, and with no bell, no polling and `BROADCAST_CONNECTION=log` a dead-lettered job tells the user nothing. **The in-worker scan gate is net-new and stronger than the upload path** — `ScanAttachmentJob::scan()` was private on a final class, so nothing could reach it; the extracted `AttachmentScanner` is called synchronously on the bytes and an infected PDF never reaches disk, where an upload is stored first and quarantined by a read-time 409. It deliberately does NOT take `WebhookPayloadArchive`'s hardcoded `Skipped`, whose stated justification is "never served to a browser". **TWO PRE-EXISTING DEFECTS FOUND, ONE FIXED HERE:** `domains.domain` stores the SUBDOMAIN LABEL (`'acme'`), not an FQDN, so `TenantMembershipService::buildInviteAcceptUrl()` and `GuestDraftController::resumeUrl()` both emit `https://acme/…`, which resolves nowhere — and both hard-code `https://`, wrong in local dev. `TenantUrl` composes label + host + port + scheme from `app.url` and inherits neither; the two older call sites are deliberately NOT retrofitted (live invite/resume links with their own tests), but the defect is now written down with a helper ready. **`PdfFieldRole::Omitted` is the PHP twin of the TS `RENDERS_NOTHING`** and its parity test PARSES `schema-mapping.ts` rather than comparing to a hand-copied list, because a copy keeps passing after someone edits the `.ts`. It is a `default`-less 31-case match (27 answer / 1 prose / 3 omitted) — the H8/H6a forcing device — and is NOT a reuse of `PipingEligibility`: the two disagree on exactly `hidden` and `calculated`, which H6a widened INTO pipeable for the same reason H17 omits them (their values exist without the respondent acting), so one rule would have to be wrong for one of them. A `calculated` total still reaches the PDF through any label that pipes it — where the respondent read it. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1746 (+43), ZERO failures** (6377 assertions; the 2 warnings are pre-existing); **PHPStan L8 — zero in any H17-touched file** (55 total, exactly the documented local baseline); Pint 780; controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint; **`composer audit` CLEAN**; **vue-tsc both tsconfigs**; **Vitest 639 passed** (1 failure = the known `token-references` full-suite timeout flake, verified 2/2 isolated — and the half that matters, "resolves every `var(--mds-*)`", passed in the full run); Vite build; `openapi.json` drift-free. **Corpora byte-untouched — `grammar_version` still exactly 296 sites and validation still 114**, `git diff` on `tests/golden` empty. **Mutation-verified — 15 mutations, and THREE found real defects rather than confirming existing ones:** dropping the section-relevance skip was **green** (the row filter already empties a non-repeatable section, so only a *repeatable* one exposes it — a missing test, now written); the raw-output structural guard was **passing vacuously** because Pest's `toContain` takes VARARGS NEEDLES, so a "failure message" second argument became a second needle and `not->toContain(a, b)` passes whenever either is absent; and the delta test was **passing vacuously** because `assertSentTimes(2)` counts a QuotaExceeded notification as happily as a Ready one. All three fixed, and every mutation now reddens a disjoint set. **A supply-chain note worth keeping:** dompdf carries **20 published advisories**, all version-bounded, highest `<3.1.6` — and essentially every one is SVG parsing, remote fonts, remote file inclusion or image handling, i.e. exactly the class `isRemoteEnabled = false` closes. Doc #26 pinned that flag before the engine was chosen. `^3.1.6` clears all 20; its sub-deps are the RENAMED `dompdf/php-font-lib` + `dompdf/php-svg-lib` (zero advisories), not the legacy `phenx/*` (which do carry them); and `ext-gd` is absent from the container, so image embedding is unreachable regardless of configuration. CI's `composer audit` runs full-tree with no severity floor and no `--omit`, so loosening the floor is a hard merge block. **Docs: no new number** (the H-map gives H17 no docs row; the H6a/H7 precedent) — reconciliations to `piping-output-encoding-design.md` §5 (row now ✅ as-built, plus the advisory/`ext-gd` note), `testing-strategy.md` §3 (the owed queued-PDF half struck; markdown-mail and spreadsheet remain), `security-threat-model.md` (§7 row pinned; §2's claim that a PDF template does not auto-escape **corrected** — it is Blade, so it does), `data-dictionary.md` (§10 `export_artifact` has its first writer + the deterministic-key rationale; `NotificationType::export_ready` marked still-unbuilt), `technical-architecture.md` R3 (a FOURTH mirror pair that is not a corpus), `domain-glossary.md` (+**Taken path**), `non-functional-requirements.md` (the render holds a transaction open; `$timeout` 60 → 110, bounded by the base class's `< retry_after` invariant), `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md` (+2 rows; it had none).
- **H1h (as-built, MERGED PR #74 `e379cd1`, docs-only)** — the **piping grammar + output-encoding/XSS contract** (H-map rows 167 + 218; a **design doc, not an ADR** — the H1f classification, in the H1a/H1b/H1g docs-only *shape*). New **`docs/piping-output-encoding-design.md` = Doc #26** (next free; `docs/PRD.md:13`'s index topped out at #25) in the `ocr-pipeline-design.md` house form, plus **13 doc reconciliations**. **Why it had to precede code:** H6a lists H1h as its only dependency and itself gates H6b/H7/H17/H21c, and H15a/H15b deferred connector message templating to H6a with `SlackMessageFormatter` as the named seam (its own docblock says so). **What exploration found that shaped it:** `docs/` had **no piping design surface and no output-encoding rule at all** (`\bXSS\b`, `output encoding`, `v-html` = zero hits tree-wide); `app/` has **zero** escaping helpers and zero `{!!`; and **CSP is not a defence** — `PublicRuntimeSecurityHeaders` sets no `default-src`/`script-src` on 4 routes, so encoding is the *sole* control, not one layer of several. **Four decisions locked with the user (AskUserQuestion): (1) SIBLING template grammar** — `TEMPLATE_VERSION = '1.0'` beside a frozen `GRAMMAR_VERSION = '2.0'`, with its own `tests/golden/templates/` + manifest + PHP⇄TS runner. A template yields **text** and participates in no evaluation; it is a different language sharing one lexeme. Rejected the v2.1 bump because both runners assert `grammar_version` **per vector** and the string sits at **288 verified sites** across `tests/golden/**` (286 vectors + 2 manifests) — rewriting all of them in files whose semantics did not change, while falsely signalling to Risk R3 that relevance/constraint moved, and falsifying the corpus's own **"grammar stays v2.0"** marker (which the chosen fork keeps literally true). **(2) DOCUMENTED per-surface rule + one escaping test per surface**, not a `__toString()`-less value object. Compensated rather than hand-waved: every table row names its escaper, **its owning increment and its required test**; the obligation is registered in `testing-strategy.md` §3 (the gate doc) rather than living only in prose; and the weakness — enforcement is a convention, nothing fails the build — is written into `security-threat-model.md` §9 as **item 9** (connector custody stays item 8, per H1g's own PROGRESS note), with the escalation named: adopt the value object if a *second* surface is ever found unescaped. **(3) The contract binds EVERY untrusted value** on a server-rendered surface, not only piped ones — an escaper cannot tell where a string came from, and the failure mode avoided is H6a escaping the piped answer while leaving the form title raw beside it in the same Slack message. **(4) Confirmation piping PINNED**, with net-new `forms.confirmation_message` + `confirmation_message_translations` assigned to **H6a**, since the copy is three hardcoded constants at `resources/public-runtime/App.vue:29-31` and no such column exists in any migration (there were 64 at the time; H6a added the 65th) — so the PRD's own coverage claim becomes satisfiable instead of aspirational. Note the consequence recorded in the doc: it is a `forms` column, so it is **not** frozen per version and its holes validate against the *currently published* version. **The grammar:** `${key}` reused verbatim from `ExpressionLexer::lexReference()` (`app/Services/Expressions/ExpressionLexer.php:161-189`, char class `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*` at `:172`/`:177`); **`$${` is the ONLY escape** and yields a literal `${`, with a bare `$` literal everywhere else — the one deliberate divergence from the expression lexer, which throws `unexpectedToken` on `$`, because in prose a lone `$` is overwhelmingly a currency sign and the escape belongs in the rare case not the common one. Total and unambiguous under leftmost-longest scanning with **no escape-of-escape regress** (`$$${` → literal `$` then literal `${`). Malformed hole = publish error, never silently literal. Caps mirror `ExpressionLexer.php:23-25` (2000 bytes, 20 holes). v1.0 has no operators, functions, nesting, filters, defaults or conditionals — each a named v1.1 revisit trigger. **The piping-source allowlist is a `default`-less exhaustive `match` over all 31 `FieldType` cases** — the H8 `OcrFieldEligibility` device, because every general predicate has a `default =>` arm (`FieldType.php:129`/`:138`/`:161`/`:200`), so a rule composed from them reproduces H8's exact defect one increment later. **Deliberately NOT a reuse of `OcrFieldEligibility`:** piping's predicate genuinely differs in two places — **`calculated` and `hidden` are pipeable** (a running total is the archetypal piping target; a URL-prefilled name exists before the first label renders) where OCR excludes/neutralises them. **19 pipeable / 2 no-answer (`note`, `page_break` = `SchemaValueFormatter::NON_DATA`) / 10 excluded (2 grid + 3 geo + 5 media)** = 31. Recorded gap not inherited: `isMedia()` types are **not** currently banned as *expression* operands while composite/geo are, so piping excludes media explicitly rather than leaning on a ban that does not exist. **THE REAL R3 HAZARD IS NOT THE PARSER** — a hole parser is trivially mirrorable; it is **`SchemaValueFormatter::displayValue()`, which has NO TS twin**, so H6b must build one and every formatting choice becomes a new drift surface. The template vectors must therefore cover **value formatting**, not just parsing, and `Coercion::toStr()` is explicitly **barred** as the primitive for a piped `decimal`/`calculated` (its non-integral float→string parity is documented as unpinned at `Coercion.php:63`). **Scope rules, all decidable from the frozen snapshot:** backward-reference-only over `(section_sequence, sequence)`; same repeat instance for a same-section key; **no cross-repeat and no repeat-to-flat**; and **a section key is never a template hole** (`${roster}` is a legal `count()` operand and an illegal hole). That last pair is exactly why H6a **cannot** simply call `assertReferencesResolve()` unchanged: its `$knownKeys` is a **flat field ∪ section union carrying no scope information** (`ExpressionValidationGate.php:47-61`), so it would accept both. H6a reuses the *walk* and the exception envelope; the predicate is template-specific. **Render vs publish is a clean split:** publish refuses an unresolvable *reference*; render tolerates an unanswered *value* as the **empty string** — never the raw `${key}` token, never `undefined` — and render must never throw (a question with a gap is recoverable; a 500 on a respondent's form is not). **Multi-locale:** each variant independently a template, independently validated; **reference-set parity across locales NOT required** (different grammars legitimately need different references) — the enforceable rule is only that every hole in every variant resolves; and **order is normative: resolve the locale, THEN render**, which is what makes it compose with `form-filling-ux-flow.md` §6's never-blank fallback. Recorded blocker for H6a: **no `*_translations` column has any validation rule anywhere** (zero hits for `translations` across `app/Http/Requests/`; the builder UI cannot author translations at all today), so piping turns unvalidated `jsonb` into a template source. **THREE LIVE DEFECTS the audit found for non-piped strings, each now named and assigned rather than merely observed:** Slack `mrkdwn` is unescaped (the raw form title goes into an `mrkdwn` section, so `<https://evil|text>` is already a live link → **H6a**); **`Markdown::withSecuredEncoding()` is off** (zero hits outside `vendor/`), so `[`/`!`/`*`/backtick in a form name are live markdown in every notification email — script execution is blocked only *incidentally* by `EncodedHtmlString`'s `htmlspecialchars` → **H3/H23**; and there is **no CSV/XLSX formula-injection prefixing** anywhere → **its own row**. **ONE AGENT CLAIM REFUTED BEFORE IT WAS WRITTEN DOWN** (the H1a "grep every claim" lesson, and the most valuable verification of the session): Inertia's `{!! json_encode($page) !!}` (`vendor/inertiajs/inertia-laravel/src/Directive.php:24`) was reported as a live `</script>` breakout sink. **It is not** — `json_encode` escapes forward slashes by default, proven empirically in-container (it emits `<\/script>`). The doc records it as a **dependency to preserve** (a future `JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES` on that path would open it), not a defect. **Two drifted facts corrected in passing:** `technical-architecture.md` §4.3 said "180 expression + **55** validation vectors" — the manifest says **106**; and `PRD.md:13`'s ADR enumeration stopped at 0007, omitting 0008/0009. **Deferred with reasons:** CSP `script-src`/`default-src` hardening (the strongest defence-in-depth available, deliberately not bundled — introducing `default-src` forces every directive to be enumerated at once, and `PublicRuntimeSecurityHeadersTest` asserts the *absence* of `font-src` precisely so a hardening PR must add it in the same change); piping geo/media; banning media as expression operands; `${…}` in an XLSForm `label` cell plus the pre-existing **`sanitizeKeys()` doesn't rewrite `${…}` references** hazard (now recorded as ⚠️ in Doc #16); the rich-text content block as a second HTML-in-labels sink with a genuinely different contract; and **a printed OCR form has no answers at print time**, so a hole renders empty on paper (a narrowing for H18a/H19). **Reconciled in the same PR:** `security-threat-model.md` (§2 +1 surface row; §7 retitled **"Expression Engine Sandboxing & Template Output Encoding"** with a 5-row template table using H1g's **Decided (Doc #26 §n)** convention so ratified design is never misread as shipped mitigation; §9 +item 9), `testing-strategy.md` (§1 +the template suite, §3 +the per-surface encoding bullet incl. the gitleaks caution), `technical-architecture.md` (R3 + §4.3 + the count fix), `form-versioning-schema-migration.md` (§4 +the gate clause; §5's Non-breaking row now excludes text edits that add/remove/repoint a hole), `data-dictionary.md` (`placeholder` documented for the first time, `form_fields.key`, +2 forward-spec `forms` rows, +a Design Note pinning the closed template-bearing column list and why `default_value` is excluded — one column with two sub-grammars is how ambiguity gets built in), `PRD.md`, `domain-glossary.md` (+**piping**/**template**/**hole**, arbitrating the `form_templates` collision the glossary exists for), `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md` (+2 rows — it had **none** for piping), `xlsform-interop-spec.md`, `non-functional-requirements.md` §6, `form-filling-ux-flow.md` §6, `webhook-integration-design.md` (the `SlackMessageFormatter` seam now has a design), `data-privacy-gdpr-compliance.md` §7 (the include-answers revisit trigger now has a named owner: **H6a**, and must be revised *in* that increment, not after). **Every `file:line` in the new doc was grep-verified before push** (13/13 paths resolve; the bare-basename citations are house-style shorthands after a full path). **No code, no migration, no `ci.yml` change, no `openapi.json` change** — `git diff --stat` is docs-only.
- **H7 (as-built, on branch `h7-hidden-prefilled-fields`)** — **hidden / prefilled fields** (H-map row 227; `full-stack, M`, depends H6a only, ungated). **Net-new:** `App\Enums\PrefillSource`, `resources/public-runtime/lib/prefill.ts`, `resources/js/components/builder/PrefillEditor.vue` (a "Prefill" config tab via `FieldType::configEditor()` → `'prefill'`, the G4a/G4b/G5b2/G6 mechanism), `tests/golden/validation/hidden.json`, plus two Pest suites and one Vitest suite. **No migration** (`form_fields.config` is `jsonb` and `default_value` already exists, both already in the frozen snapshot), **no `/api/v1` change** ⇒ `openapi.json` verified drift-free. **Three decisions locked with the user. (1) TWO sources, not three — the H-map's "calculated source" is AMENDED, not implemented.** `FieldType::Calculated` already *is* the expression source: mirrored in both engines, write-back persisted, already `PipingEligibility::Pipeable`. Both compute passes gate on `field_type === 'calculated'` (`SemanticValidator.php:1163`, `semantic-validator.ts:1009`); widening them to `hidden` would duplicate a working path and open a **new R3 mirror surface for zero new capability** — the H8 "amend rather than implement the wrong thing" device. **(2) URL prefill is `hidden`-only** — prefilling a *visible* field is a different feature with its own UX questions and gets its own row. **(3) The encode page renders an editable input for a `url`-sourced hidden field, read-only for a `fixed` one** — that channel has no URL, so a keyer transcribing paper is the only possible source. **The core contract:** `PrefillSource::for()` classifies a hidden field's config into `Fixed | Url | None`. A **`fixed`** field is server-authoritative — its value is written from the authored `default_value` and the submitted value is **never inspected**, on every one of the six ingest channels (a "trusted channel" carve-out was rejected: it would make one form yield different data per channel and leave H20's amount rule true on `guest` and false on `api_import`). A **`url`** field is the only client-writable one, scalar-only under `MAX_VALUE_BYTES = 2000` (`prefill_too_long`, a Stage-1 structural fault). Sourcing is **opt-in and fail-closed** — a field key is never an implicit query parameter, the H8 allowlist lesson. **The prefill pass lives INSIDE `StructuralAnswerNormalizer::normalize()`, and the placement is load-bearing twice:** both write paths (`SubmissionPipeline::submit()` and `SubmissionDraftService::saveDraft()`) call it and then immediately hash with `AnswersContentChecksum`, so this is the one placement that covers drafts *and* keeps the checksum deterministic — a replay that omits the injected value and one that includes it normalise identically, so neither can raise a spurious G8c 409. It needs a **second pass over the FIELDS** because the main loop walks the client's keys and can only visit what it was sent. **The rule that makes everything else safe: a hidden field never produces a respondent-facing error.** Two halves, each earning its keep — the **runtime narrowing** (`hidden` joins `calculated` in both engines' early return) is what makes dropping hidden fields from the SPA's step model *safe* rather than merely tidy, since an error would otherwise put a jump link in the summary banner addressing a row that does not render; the **publish gate** (four refusals: `hidden_field_required`, `hidden_field_has_validations`, hidden-in-a-repeatable-section, `prefill_param_invalid`) is how the author finds out, and it is **not retroactive**, which is precisely why the narrowing ships too. **Three live defects closed in passing, all in the same one-line set:** `hidden`, `calculated` AND `page_break` each rendered *"Not available for manual entry yet (Phase 2)"* — the encode channel's copy — **on a respondent's public form**, because none is in `schema-mapping.ts`'s `SUPPORTED` and `controlFor()` falls through to `'unsupported'`. Nothing pinned it. The new `RENDERS_NOTHING` set deliberately does **not** mirror `EncodeFormPresenter::OMITTED`: H7 makes the two channels asymmetric on purpose (a respondent must never see a hidden field; a keyer must). **Seeding order is the whole SPA rule:** prefill first, `restoreAnswers(initialAnswers)` second, so **a restored draft always wins** — a resume link carries no query string and the value it saved is the one the session was built against. Both go through `restoreAnswers`, so the prefill inherits its flat-key guard free. **The store stays DOM-free** (its own docblock's invariant): `App.vue` reads `window.location.search` once and threads it down. **A deliberate client/server asymmetry, commented at both sites so it does not read as a bug:** an over-cap URL parameter is **dropped** client-side while an over-cap submitted value is **422'd** server-side. They cannot disagree — the SPA never puts one into the answer map, so the fault is reachable only from a direct API post, where a slug a client can act on is the useful answer and swallowing 2KB silently is not. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1703 (+36), ZERO failures** (6207 assertions; the 2 warnings are pre-existing); **PHPStan L8 — zero in any H7-touched file** (55 in the local full analyse, all in untouched files, the documented local baseline; CI is clean); Pint 765; controller-gate / job-payload-lint / migration-lint; **vue-tsc both tsconfigs** (`tsconfig.json` + `tsconfig.sw.json`); **Vitest 635 passed** (the 2 failures are the known `token-references` full-suite **timeout** flake, verified 2/2 isolated — which also confirms every new `var(--mds-…)` resolves); Vite build; **`openapi.json` drift-free**. **Mutation-verified — 6 mutations, each reddening only its intended tests**, and two of them changed the work: neutering `coerceHidden`'s source guard reddens 3 while removing the injection pass reddens a *different* 3, proving the two mechanisms are independently pinned rather than redundantly covering each other; and the checksum-stability test was found to **pass vacuously** under the injection mutation (neither document carried the key), so it now asserts presence as well as equality. **CORPUS COUNT MOVED, deliberately:** the validation corpus is **106 → 114** and `grammar_version` sites **288 → 296**. The expressions and templates corpora are byte-untouched. H7 is the first increment to *extend* an existing corpus, so read the invariant as written — byte-untouched unless an increment deliberately extends one and says so; a changed count with no such note is drift. **Docs: no new number taken** (the H-map gives H7 no docs row, following H6a's "no Doc #27" precedent) — reconciliations to `security-threat-model.md` §4 (+2 rows, **Mitigated (H7)**), `data-dictionary.md` §5 (+the H7 Design Note + `default_value` now has a reader), `form-versioning-schema-migration.md` §4 (+the four gate clauses), `testing-strategy.md` §3 (H7 added a second test to the DOM row rather than a new row — a new *entry point*, not a new surface), `piping-output-encoding-design.md` (§3.1's `hidden` row + §8's "H7's contract is H7's to state" bullet, both now as-built), `ocr-pipeline-design.md` §2 (its `hidden` row named the dropped expression source), `xlsform-interop-spec.md` (+⚠️ — the prefill source has **no XLSForm expression at all**, so an export silently loses it), `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md` (+2 rows; it had none), `domain-glossary.md` (+**Prefill**, kept deliberately distinct from "default"), `technical-architecture.md` §4.3 + R3 (the moved counts).
- **H6b (as-built, MERGED PR #76 `fb2c960`, 6/6 CI green on the first run)** — the **piping runtime** (H-map row 226; classified `ui, M` there, shipped **full-stack, M/L** — three of the four decisions locked this session push PHP work into it). Doc #26 → **v1.2**, amendments **A7–A10**, each inline beside the clause it corrects. **Net-new:** `resources/public-runtime/engine/display-value.ts` (the `SchemaValueFormatter::displayValue()` twin Doc #26 §3.2 called the real R3 hazard) + `engine/template-renderer.ts`, both exported from the barrel — which is not cosmetic, since `tsconfig.json` excludes `*.test.ts`, so the barrel is the only thing that puts them under `vue-tsc`; `lib/schema-mapping.ts::buildTemplateSources()`; a store seam (`labelFor`/`hintFor`/`placeholderFor`/`sectionTitleFor`/`sectionDescriptionFor` + `renderAnswers` + `instanceRenderAnswers` + `RepeatScope`); `TemplateValidationGate::confirmationMessageViolations()`; `SchemaValueFormatter::scalarString()`; and two Pest suites + one Vitest suite (`piping.test.ts`). **Dispatch is on the raw `field_type` STRING, deliberately** — there is no `FieldType` union anywhere in TS, and minting a 31-case one to mirror the PHP enum would create a parallel catalog needing forever-sync, a *new* drift surface in the increment whose purpose is closing one. Only **nine** strings are load-bearing (`HAS_OPTIONS` ×4, `GEO_TYPES` ×3, `yes_no`, `cascading_select`), each vector-pinned, following the `schema-mapping.ts`/`semantic-validator.ts` precedent. **The geo branch is mirrored even though geo is `PipingEligibility::Excluded`** and therefore unreachable through a published hole: `displayValue()` is a SHARED formatter and an incomplete twin fails invisibly the moment anything reuses it, and mirroring it is what let the corpus carry no `engines` key at all. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1667 (+52), ZERO failures** (6096 assertions; the 2 warnings are pre-existing); **PHPStan L8 zero new** — the 3 remaining are the documented `User::$name` baseline, verified identical in count on `main`, and test files are excluded by `phpstan.neon` by design; Pint 763; controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint; **vue-tsc both tsconfigs**; **Vitest 605 passed** (the 2 failures are the known `token-references` full-suite **timeout** flake — `Test timed out in 5000ms`, passes 2/2 isolated); Vite build; **`openapi.json` verified drift-free** (no `/api/v1` controller touched; the A3 change is a tenant web route that generates no operation); **both existing golden corpora byte-untouched, `grammar_version` still at exactly 288 sites**. **No migration, no job, no `/api/v1` change, no DS component change.** **Mutation-verified on both sides** — 8 mutations, each reddening only its intended tests: PHP (bool cast → 2, blank-variant → 1, flat-answers-for-instances → 1) and TS (bool → 2, cascade → 1, blank-variant → 1, float → 2, prototype-unsafe lookup → 1 *after* the vector was fixed). **Two honest findings recorded rather than papered over:** the repeat merge DIRECTION is unobservable (field keys are unique per version, so a flat key and an instance key can never collide — reversing the arguments is a green mutation; what IS pinned is that the instance map is consulted at all), and the `1e-5` float mutation survives because `String(1e-5)` already agrees. **Docs:** Doc #26 v1.2 + reconciliations to `testing-strategy.md` §1+§3 (the DOM/HTML row is **shipped**, H17 still owes the PDF half), `security-threat-model.md` §7 (the XSS row's DOM half now **pinned by H6b**), and `technical-architecture.md` §4.3 + R3 (76 template vectors; R3's "H6b builds the twin" is now as-built).
- **H6a (as-built, MERGED PR #75 `e04e68b`, 6/6 CI green on the first run)** — the **piping backend** (H-map row 225; **backend**, M, depends H1h only), the code half of Doc #26 and the increment gating **H6b/H7/H17/H21c** plus the connector-templating seam H15a/H15b both deferred to it. **Net-new:** `App\Services\Templates` (`TemplateParser` with `TEMPLATE_VERSION = '1.0'`, `TemplateRenderer`, `TemplateScopeResolver`, `TemplateSources`), `App\Exceptions\Templates\TemplateSyntaxException`, `App\Enums\PipingEligibility`, `App\Services\Forms\TemplateValidationGate` (the **third** gate in `PublishService` step 1, over all **nine** template-bearing columns × every locale variant), `forms.confirmation_message`/`_translations` (migration `2026_07_30_000002`), `App\Rules\ValidTemplate`, a builder **Confirmation** modal + a `message-check` glyph, the TypeScript `template.ts` twin, and **`tests/golden/templates/` — 53 vectors, the corpus's third**. **Three PHP surfaces now interpolate**: `SubmissionInboxPresenter` fills holes for real (it alone holds label + catalog + answers), while `EncodeFormPresenter` and `SubmissionExporter::header()` render with an EMPTY answer map — §3.4 forbids emitting the raw `${key}` token even where a hole is structurally unfillable (one header row serves N submissions; the encode page serves a blank keying form). Export **cells** untouched — the spreadsheet-injection row is its own increment, and closing it here would have absorbed another increment's scope and falsified `security-threat-model.md` §9 item 9's count. **The Slack `mrkdwn` defect is CLOSED** — `SlackMessageFormatter::mrkdwn()` escapes `&`/`<`/`>` (`&` first, so the entity it introduces is not re-scanned) applied to each untrusted **variable before interpolation**, never the assembled string whose `*` emphasis is deliberate; all **three** sinks covered (section block, context row, and the `text` fallback, which derives from the already-escaped headline); the Block Kit `url` field left alone as a URL context. Its test is **net-new, not updated**: the class had none, and the repo's only message-body assertion was a `str_contains` on *trusted* copy, so the fix was entirely unpinned. **FIVE Doc #26 amendments (A1–A5)**, each inline beside the clause it corrects, in H8's "amend rather than implement the wrong thing" style — **A1** caps bind only a hole-bearing value (four of the nine columns are `TEXT`/`jsonb` whose only bound is a character-counting FormRequest that XLSForm import and the blueprint materializer bypass, so an unconditional byte cap would refuse to publish a form over a hint that predates piping); **A2** the `(section_sequence, sequence)` ordering tuple is not implementable and becomes *(owning section's `sequence`, field's `sequence`)* with a **tie as rejection**; **A3** the confirmation message validates against the version **being published**, not the outgoing one, which forces the grammar-at-request / references-at-publish split; **A4** slugs are asserted at the inner exception because `PublishValidationException` is message-only; **A5** the no-rejected-template-in-`schema_snapshot` guarantee comes from the **transaction**, not the gate's position — proven by mutation, and no honest test can pin the ordering (`SchemaSnapshotSerializer` is `final` and `PublishService` type-hints it). **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1615 (+102), ZERO failures**; PHPStan L8 clean on every H6a-authored line (8 remaining are the documented local `property.notFound` baseline, all on untouched lines — `FormField`/`FormSection` `@property` docblocks were completed in passing, which also removed two baseline errors); Pint 761; migration round-trip up→down→up + a vacuous-by-design `migration-lint` pass (it returns early on an alter-only migration — its green validates nothing); vue-tsc both tsconfigs; **Vitest 547 passed / 13 skipped** (skips = the PHP-only formatting vectors; the 2 fails are the known `token-references` full-suite timeout flake, verified passing isolated); Vite build; **`openapi.json` regenerated (+16 lines, the public form block only) + `redocly lint` clean**; **both existing golden corpora byte-untouched, `grammar_version` still at exactly 288 sites**; E2E seeder re-verified. **Mutation-verified:** `matrix`→pipeable reddens 3 tests, capping a hole-free value reddens 2, escaping `&` last reddens 3 — and the mutation pass is what exposed A5. **Docs:** Doc #26 → **v1.1 as-built**, plus reconciliations to `security-threat-model.md` (§7's Slack + template-hole rows flipped to **Mitigated (H6a)**, the XSS row flipped **partially**, §9 item 9's "three live defects" → **two**), `testing-strategy.md` §1+§3, `form-versioning-schema-migration.md` §4, `data-dictionary.md` (the two forward-spec rows are now as-built), `technical-architecture.md` §4.3 + R3 (**three** corpora now), `domain-glossary.md`, `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md`, and **corrections to `data-privacy-gdpr-compliance.md` §7 + `webhook-integration-design.md`** — both assumed H6a would make the connector message author-editable; it did **not** (escaping only, fixed layout), so the sub-processor trigger did **not** fire and `include_answers` stays deferred. `xlsform-interop-spec.md`'s `sanitizeKeys()` ⚠️ left as a ⚠️ (H6a does not touch the importer); no Doc #27 taken.
- **H8 (as-built, MERGED PR #73 `ab2de20`)** — the **`ocr_compatible` fix** (H-map row 228; **backend**, S, no deps, no gate, **retroactive by construction**). Closes the ⚠️ as-built gap `docs/ocr-pipeline-design.md` §2 has carried since 2026-07-21: the doc specifies an **allowlist**, the shipped code was a **denylist** (`! $hasGeo && ! $hasMedia && ! anyOf([Calculated])`), so `matrix`/`likert_matrix` evaluated **`true`** against §2's own words, `is_repeatable` was **never consulted** (`compute()` read only `$version->fields()`, never `sections()` — the clause was structurally unreachable), and **every future field type inherited eligibility**. Live, not theoretical: `capability_flags` is a **shipped public API field** (`FormResource:30`, `/api/v1/forms` index+show), so a stale `true` was a wrong API answer being served, and the flag is recomputed **only on publish** — hence retroactivity. **Four decisions locked with the user (AskUserQuestion): (1) charter-exact allowlist membership** — §2's literal 12-item list is an **under-enumeration** (implementing it would disqualify a form for containing one `email` field or one `page_break`, hitting far more forms than the bug), so §2 was **AMENDED** with per-type reasons rather than implemented, and the net behaviour change is exactly `matrix` + `likert_matrix` + repeat groups + nothing-extractable + unresolvable-type; **(2) ship the retroactive backfill NOW** even though the production box deliberately does not exist yet — otherwise retroactivity is a deploy-order *accident*, and the pre-H8 state can only be manufactured for a test while it still exists conceptually; **(3) SNAPSHOT-DRIVEN primitive**; **(4) non-vacuity clause IN**. **(1) The forcing mechanism** = new `App\Enums\OcrFieldEligibility` — a 3-case enum (`Extractable` 17 / `Neutral` 3 / `Excluded` 11) whose `for()` is a **`default`-less exhaustive `match`** over all 31 `FieldType` cases, so a 32nd case is a **PHPStan L8 error** ("Match expression does not handle remaining values", merge-blocking in `static-analysis`) + an `UnhandledMatchError`. **Why not compose the existing predicates:** every one of `isMedia()`/`isGeo()`/`isComposite()`/`isAdvanced()`/`hasOptions()`/`configEditor()` has a `default =>` arm, so `! isMedia() && ! isGeo() && ! isComposite()` reproduces the exact defect. House precedent = `XlsformTypeMap::toXlsform()`'s own docblock. Lives in `app/Enums/` and **not** on `FieldType` (channel policy, the `XlsformTypeMap::isImportable()` line) so the catalog's four 31-arm matches stay untouched. **`neutral` is the load-bearing third value**: `note`/`page_break` hold no answer (≡ `SchemaValueFormatter::NON_DATA`), `hidden` holds one the paper never carries but does **not** obstruct the rest (the `EncodeFormPresenter::OMITTED` precedent — the human twin of this channel omits it and does not refuse the form) — and being *neutral* rather than *extractable* is what makes non-vacuity meaningful. **(2) `CapabilityFlags` rewritten static-pure + snapshot-driven** — `forSnapshot(array $snapshot)` is the **only** input shape; `PublishService` step 8 now consumes the snapshot **step 3 already froze** (it used to re-read live tables to answer a question step 3 had frozen — *that gap is where drift lives*), dropping 2 queries and the injected dependency. One input shape ⇒ the backfill and H18a evaluate the same bytes ⇒ **drift is inexpressible, not merely unlikely**. Deleted the duplicate `GEO_TYPES`/`MEDIA_TYPES` consts in favour of `isGeo()`/`isMedia()` (same membership; the duplicates were the same species of latent bug). Fail-closed throughout: a missing/malformed `fields`/`sections` key yields **null, not `[]`** (a draft's snapshot is literally `[]` — *unevaluable*, not "no fields"), `tryFrom()` never `from()` (a snapshot from a newer schema must not 500), and a section without an explicit `is_repeatable: false` counts as repeatable. **(3) THE MONOTONICITY INVARIANT is load-bearing, not decoration** — H8's permitted set is **disjoint** from the pre-H8 denylist (geo ∪ media ∪ `calculated`) and every structural clause only adds falsity, so `new ⇒ old`. That single fact licenses the backfill to examine **only stored `true`s** (**lossless**, not an optimization) and to write a **constant `false`**. Pinned by a test over `FieldType::cases()`. **A future increment that WIDENS the allowlist reverses it and is next-publish-only — it needs its own backfill.** **(4) The backfill** (`app/Support/Migrations/OcrCompatibilityBackfill.php` + data-only migration `2026_07_30_000001`, the `LegacyOverrideBackfill` shape, `$withinTransaction = false`, `pgsql_privileged`) **restates NOTHING in SQL** — rejected the shorter set-based `UPDATE … field_type NOT IN (…)` precisely because it is the drift this increment exists to prevent (a 32nd type would be classified by the match and *silently not* by the SQL); it read-computes-writes through the same `forSnapshot()`, and a test asserts the SQL text contains **no field-type value and no `is_repeatable`**. Keyset-walked on the uuidv7 PK; **`jsonb_set` on the single `{ocr_compatible}` path** so `has_geofields`/`has_media` and any key a *future* increment adds survive byte-identical; **INNER JOIN** (no published version ⇒ `'{}'` stays `'{}'`); **no `deleted_at` filter** (a restore must not resurrect a stale `true`); **`updated_at` deliberately NOT bumped** (it is an optimistic-concurrency token — `FormBuilderService::rowVersion()`). **NEW guard both precedent backfills lack: `assertPrivileged()` asks `pg_roles` whether `current_user` is superuser-or-BYPASSRLS** — `select 1` proves only that the connection *opens*, and a NOBYPASSRLS role would read zero rows under FORCE RLS with no tenant GUC, flip nothing and pass its own postcondition: a **vacuous green**. Asserted from the *migration*, not the body, which keeps the body **connection-agnostic** — and that is what let this increment write the **effect test the precedents concluded was impossible**: their bodies are inherently cross-tenant, H8's just scans `forms` (plain strict RLS), so the same body on the app connection under a live tenant GUC does real work on real rows. The postcondition counts **violations and demands zero** (not the precedents' `stamped < expected` — H8's invariant is a property of the data), which also makes it race-tolerant: a form published mid-run was published by the fixed code, so it re-derives as eligible. `down()` is a **documented no-op** (the honest reverse is "restore the wrong value"; the flag is derived and recomputable). **Gates — ALL green, in-container: Pest 1513 (+38, ZERO failures** — not even the `DatabaseWorkerPipelineTest` flake — 5692 assertions; the 2 summary warnings are pre-existing, my 4 suites emit none); **PHPStan L8 = 0 on every new/changed file** (the one error in `PublishService` is the pre-existing `FormVersion::$description` baseline, byte-identical on `main` at line 103); Pint (743 files) / controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint; **`openapi.json` verified drift-free**; migration round-trip up→down→up clean, and `migrate:fresh` exercises the guard's zero path. **Mutation-verified, not just green:** classifying `matrix` as extractable reddens 3 tests, deleting the repeat clause 3, deleting non-vacuity 1, and swapping `jsonb_set` for a whole-object overwrite 3. **No frontend work** — grep confirms **zero** TS/Vue references to `capability_flags`/`ocr_compatible`. **Docs:** §2's rule replaced with the four clauses + the verdict table + a *Deviations* paragraph + a *Totality* paragraph; the ⚠️ block replaced by a ✅ **As-built** block recording bullets 1+2 **CLOSED** and bullet 3 **corrected** — the flag still lives on `forms`, but because it is now derived from `schema_snapshot`, an older version's eligibility **is** recoverable (`CapabilityFlags::isOcrCompatible($version)`), so the original bullet's claim was false and `form_versions.capability_flags` was **rejected** (no reader, a write on every draft-producing path, a backfill over every historical version); `data-dictionary.md:150` names the three keys and labels the column a display cache. **Narrowings → H18a/H19:** **H18a must NOT gate on `forms.capability_flags`** (derive per-version); `hidden` is permitted-but-not-extractable, so H18a sources it from the pipeline's default/expression path, never the scan (revisit with H7's value-source taxonomy); `note`/`page_break` are chrome, so the linelist mapper must not allocate columns for them; **`calculated` stays excluded as conservatism, not semantics** (arguably `neutral` on the merits — both `EncodeFormPresenter::OMITTED` and `DraftCompleteness::isAnswerable()` drop it — but reclassifying is a widening); `signature` is the one media type whose artefact *is* on the scan; `cascading_select` is eligible per §2 but the hardest extraction on the list, and dropping it later would need its own backfill (the pattern is reusable); `has_media` remains written-and-unread.
- **H15b (as-built, MERGED PR #72 `5e80872`)** — the **Slack connector UI** (H-map row 239; **ui**; depends H15a; gated **`feature:native_connectors` + `integrations.manage`**, surface HIDDEN for tiers/roles without both — the H14 precedent). The session-authed Inertia surface H15a was built expecting: `config/connectors.php`'s `return_path` was already `/integrations`, and `routes/tenant.php` already said *"H15b builds the Integrations UI that links here."* **Three decisions locked with the user (AskUserQuestion): (1) list + per-RULE detail** (a grant has almost no detail of its own; the rule is what owns a delivery log); **(2) channel picker = `MdsSelect` fed by a JSON sidecar**, not a new DS combobox; **(3) a synchronous "Send test message" IS in scope.** **(1) Routes** — 8 new tenant routes with **shallow nesting**: only `store` binds `{connection}`; the rest are flat `/integrations/rules/{connectionSubscription}`. That is load-bearing, not cosmetic — `disconnect()` **soft-deletes the grant while keeping its rules** (paused), so a nested binding would 404 exactly when a tenant is tidying up after disconnecting. Which forced a **NEW `ConnectionSubscriptionPolicy`** (H15a needed none because the API nests everything under `{connection}`); it repeats `integrations.manage` rather than resolving through `$rule->grant`, which is null in precisely that case. RLS still does the tenant scoping — a cross-tenant id never binds. **(2) The OAuth return is POST-Redirect-GET**: `ConnectionController::index` turns `?connected=` / `?connect_error=` into a **session flash** and 302s to a clean `/integrations`, so the toast rides the bridge the whole app already uses and a refresh has no query string left to re-raise it from (rejected: an Inertia prop scrubbed with `history.replaceState`, which re-implements one-shot semantics the flash already owns). **The error code is untrusted** — the callback forwards the provider's raw `?error=` verbatim and anyone can craft the link — so `ConnectorConnectOutcome` uses it **only as a lookup key** into a closed table with a generic fallback, resolves the provider label through the enum, and lets an **error win over a success** when a crafted link carries both. **(3) Channel listing is a CAPABILITY, not a fifth `ConnectorProvider` method** — that interface is pinned at "four responsibilities, deliberately no more", and listing destinations is not universal (H16's Sheets enumerates spreadsheets). New optional `ListsChannels` + `ConnectorChannel`/`ConnectorChannelPage` + `SlackChannelLister` (cursor-paged `conversations.list`, `types=public_channel`, same guard/timeout/no-redirect discipline, `is_member` → `available`), resolved via a config-driven `ConnectorRegistry::channelListerFor()` that returns **null** for a provider without one. **NO SERVER CACHE, deliberately**: `grep -rn "Cache::" app/` returns **zero** — the repo has never written to a cache at runtime, and a picker is a poor place to introduce the first cache-key tenancy question. The modal holds the list for the page's lifetime with an explicit Refresh. **(4) The sidecar always answers 200** with `error` in the BODY for every provider failure: a non-2xx reaches `builderClient` as a thrown error whose message `scopesClient`-style callers discard, so a Slack outage would show "couldn't load" with no reason. **(5) The test send reuses the adapter's own `deliver()`** — inheriting the SSRF guard, timeouts, no-redirect and Slack's `ok:false`-at-HTTP-200 classification, so it can't drift from a real delivery, and H16's providers get a tester free. It writes no ledger row and burns no quota (both live in the job, not in `deliver()`), and a credential rejection is **reported, not acted on** — revoking stays the job's terminal path so a blip during a voluntary test can't tear down a working grant. Result shape is **byte-identical to `WebhookTester`**, so it rides the existing `testResult` flash, the existing `FlashTestResult` type and the existing `TestResultModal`. **(6) UI** — `integrations/Index.vue` (provider catalog + one card per workspace wrapping its rules `MdsDataTable`) + `integrations/RuleShow.vue`. Two deliberate departures from the webhook detail page they mirror: the log's fourth column is the **Result excerpt, not the HTTP status** (Slack fails at 200, so a status column would read "200" beside a red Failed badge and teach people to distrust the page), and there is **no per-row redeliver** (H15a ships no connector redeliver endpoint; a permanently-disabled button is worse than none). **(7) DS** — a `plug` glyph; two `statusVariant` entries labelled by what the tenant must DO (`refresh_failed` → "Reconnect needed", `revoked` → "Disconnected") + light/dark stories; **`MdsButton` gained `as="a"`/`href`** (mirroring `MdsCard`) because an action that navigates to another origin must be a link, and a disabled anchor needs `aria-disabled` + a dropped href since HTML has no disabled attribute for links; and a **shared fix to `TestResultModal`** — its scrollable `<pre>` had no `tabindex`, an axe `scrollable-region-focusable` failure H14 only escaped because its seeded excerpt was short. **(8) Provider `configured` flag** — false when the deployment has no app credentials, which disables the connect button with an explanation instead of bouncing a tenant off an opaque consent error; it is also what makes the page render sanely in CI, where no Slack credentials exist. **Gates (all in-container): Pest 1475** (+50 across 4 new suites; the 1 full-suite failure is `DatabaseWorkerPipelineTest`, which **passes isolated** — a load flake in a committing worker test with no causal path from this increment); **PHPStan L8 = 0 on every new/changed file** (the one remaining error is the pre-existing `E2eSeeder:102` baseline, byte-identical on `main`); Pint / controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint; **vue-tsc both tsconfigs**; **Vitest 503/505** (the 2 fails are the known `token-references` timeout flake — pass isolated); Vite build; **`openapi.json` unchanged and verified drift-free** (nothing under `routes/api.php` moved). **No migration, no job, no `/api/v1` change.** E2E: `/integrations` + a rule-detail interaction added to `responsive-axe`, and an `E2eSeeder::seedConnections()` (verified end-to-end: 2 workspaces incl. a `refresh_failed` one, 3 rules, a 4-status delivery spread) — the specs never open the rule modal, which is the only control that would call Slack. **Narrowings → H16:** message templating still waits for H6a (`SlackMessageFormatter` is the seam); **public channels only** (`channels:read` doesn't cover private ones and `groups:read` would force every connected workspace back through consent); no connector redeliver UI (the job's `skipMeter` seam exists); no per-tier connection/rule cap; no `conversations.join` auto-repair — the picker warns, it doesn't fix; rules of a disconnected grant vanish from the list but stay reachable by URL, where the page says why.
- **H15a (as-built, MERGED PR #71 `02647db`)** — the **OAuth connector framework + Slack backend** (H-map row 238; ADR-0009; **backend+api, no UI** — H15b), gated **`feature:native_connectors`** (Starter+, ratified by ADR-0008 §D7 and seeded since H5a; **this is its first consumer**) + a **NEW** `integrations.manage` permission. **Four decisions locked with the user (AskUserQuestion): (1) EXTEND `webhook_deliveries`** into the shared outbound ledger rather than a second table; **(2) FIXED Block Kit message** — templating waits for H6a (recorded as a deferred dep, not a blocker: today's envelopes carry ids/metadata, not answers, so there is nothing to pipe that the layout doesn't already say); **(3) HAND-ROLLED `ConnectorProvider` adapter**, no Socialite (it assumes a same-host session-backed redirect); **(4) ADR-0009.** **The linchpin — the session cookie is HOST-ONLY** (`config/session.php:159` ← `SESSION_DOMAIN`, null in both env files), so the central-domain callback has neither session nor tenant, and under FORCE RLS a context-less INSERT there **writes zero rows without erroring**. Identity therefore crosses the host boundary in the OAuth **`state`**: a signed, domain-separated (`connector-oauth-state.v1`), provider-bound HMAC token in the guest share/resume wire format, verified **pre-RLS** by `EstablishConnectorOauthContext` (which then applies tenant+user and `forget()`s in `terminate()`); the return host is read from the **`domains` table** (never echoed) and the outcome rides a **query param, not a flash**. **(1) Schema** — `connections` (grant: `encrypted`-cast **`text`** tokens, `$hidden`, **returned by NO api in any form, not even masked** — unlike a webhook secret, the platform is their only consumer; partial unique `(tenant,provider,external_account_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL` so re-connect updates in place and restores a disconnected row) + `connection_subscriptions` (routing: event_types, form scope, `config` destination, breaker counter) — credentials on the grant, routing on the rule, so one grant feeds many destinations without re-refreshing a token per rule. **(2) The shared ledger** (alter-only `…000003`): `webhook_endpoint_id` → nullable, `+connection_subscription_id`, **XOR CHECK `num_nonnulls(...)=1`** (what makes "which job delivers this row" a *total* branch in `WebhookRetrySweeper`, not a guess), and the old `(endpoint,event_id)` unique replaced by **two PARTIAL uniques** — they must be partial, since a plain unique over a nullable column would let unlimited connector rows share an `event_id` under Postgres NULL semantics and silently end idempotency for half the table. One ledger ⇒ one retry ladder (extracted to a shared `RetryLadder`), one `webhook_deliveries` quota, one log shape. **(3) Slack adapter** — two behaviours that are easy to get wrong: it reports failure with **HTTP 200 + `{"ok":false}`** (transport status is never the verdict), and **bot tokens don't expire by default** (so the refresh sweep correctly SKIPS them; the path exists for rotation-enabled workspaces + H16). A **credential rejection is terminal**: revoke the grant, pause its rules, dead-letter, notify the owner once. **(4) Fan-out** = **four NEW auto-discovered synchronous listeners** (not a second call inside the webhook ones) → `ConnectorEventDispatcher`, so the channels fail independently and **no shipped file was touched to add the channel**; matching adds one condition the webhook twin has no analogue for — the CONNECTION must be active. **(5) Refresh** = `RefreshConnectorTokensJob` (MaintenanceJob) → `RefreshTenantConnectorTokensJob` → `ConnectionTokenRefresher`, `hourly()` against a 2h lead; **never lazy inside a delivery** (that would put a second outbound call in the delivery transaction and let a provider outage stampede every queued delivery). **(6) API** — `GET /connections`, `GET|DELETE /connections/{c}`, full CRUD + delivery log for subscriptions, each with the standing triplet `ability:manage:integrations` + `can:,connection` + `feature:native_connectors`. **Deliberately NO POST/PATCH for a connection** (a grant can only come from the interactive flow; an API accepting a token would be a path to writing a credential we then act with) — `OpenApiContractTest` asserts that **absence**. **(7) RBAC** — `integrations.manage` is the **29th** permission and `manage:integrations` a NEW ability, not a reuse of the webhook pair: `manage:webhooks` tokens are already minted, and reusing that permission would retroactively hand every one of them authority whose blast radius reaches **outside this platform** (the `scopes.manage` rationale, one step sharper). **Gates — ALL green, in-container: Pest 1425 (+91** across 6 new suites — signed-state unit + OAuth flow incl. **a state minted for tenant A never writing into tenant B**, connections API incl. "no representation ever contains a token", fan-out, the committing `Http::fake` delivery job, the XOR ledger + two-way sweeper branch, and the committing refresh sweep); **PHPStan L8 = 0 on every touched file**; Pint / migration-lint / controller-gate / job-payload-lint; **openapi.json regenerated + `redocly lint` CLEAN**; migrations round-trip up→down→up; vue-tsc + Vite build. **6/6 CI green on the FIRST run.** **Narrowings → H15b/H16:** message templating (needs H6a; `SlackMessageFormatter` is the seam), the connect UI + channel picker, no per-tier connection cap (no matrix row; sends are already bounded by the shared delivery quota), the stateless `state` is replayable within its ~10-min TTL (bounded by single-use provider codes), and `APP_PREVIOUS_KEYS` is documented but not operationalized.
- **H1g (as-built, MERGED PR #70 `83c923a`, docs-only)** — **ADR-0009: OAuth Connector Token Custody** (H-map row 217; the H1a/H1b docs-only shape). Written **before** H15a because a connector inverts the trust direction of every outbound integration so far: a webhook secret proves our payloads are ours, an OAuth token lets the platform **act as the tenant inside their Slack workspace**. **Number: ADR-0009** — the next free one; 0009 was earmarked for the cancelled payments ADR that was never written, and the Status block records that so `0008:8`/`:152`'s back-references stay coherent. **The finding that drove the design: the session cookie is HOST-ONLY** (`config/session.php:159` ← `SESSION_DOMAIN`, `null` in both `.env.example:59` and `.env:56`), so a tenant-subdomain session is unreadable on the central host where a provider callback must land (one fixed redirect URI, no wildcard subdomains) — and the two repo comments claiming "SESSION_DOMAIN spans subdomains" (`routes/tenant.php:49`, `routes/admin.php:15`) are **wrong against the config they describe**. **Ten sub-decisions:** §D1 tokens are `encrypted`-cast **`text`** (varchar(255) can't hold the ~290-char at-rest form), `$hidden`, and **never returned in any form — not even masked** (unlike a webhook secret, a connector token has no user-facing consumer); §D2 one central-domain callback per provider, **outside `web`** (a third-party GET carries no session/CSRF token; `state` is the CSRF control); §D3 `state` = a signed **domain-separated HMAC** token (`connector-oauth-state.v1`, the guest share/resume-token wire format), ~10-min TTL, claims `{tid,uid,prov,nonce,exp}`, **provider-bound**; stateless ⇒ replayable within TTL, **accepted** (provider codes are single-use; a replay only rewrites the tenant's own connection); §D4 tenant context adopted from the **verified** state **pre-RLS**, released in `terminate()` — without it a central-domain INSERT under FORCE RLS writes **zero rows and does not error**; §D5 the return host is read from the **`domains` table** (never echoed from request or state) and the outcome rides a **query parameter, not a flash** (the central host cannot write the tenant host's session cookie) — that is what closes the open-redirect; §D6 refresh is a proactive `MaintenanceJob` fan-out with a lead window, **never lazy inside a delivery attempt**; §D7 disconnect **destroys local tokens unconditionally**, provider revoke best-effort; §D8 least-privilege scopes stored per connection; §D9 app credentials in `.env` only + the **`APP_PREVIOUS_KEYS` gap recorded** (`config/app.php:99-106` supports `previous_keys`; it is in no `.env.example` and no doc, so an `APP_KEY` rotation would silently brick every stored token — assigned to Doc #22, NOT shipped by H15a); §D10 a new **`connection` audit alias** with both token columns in `AuditRedactor`'s unconditional list, registered in the same increment that creates the table. **Gate inherited, not coined** — ADR-0008 §D7 already ratified `native_connectors` Starter+ (seeded at `PlanCatalog.php:34,54`, consumed by nothing yet). **Doc reconciliation in the same PR:** `docs/security-threat-model.md` §2 (+2 attack-surface rows), §5 retitled **"Webhook & Connector Security"** with a 9-row connector table whose verdicts are labelled **Decided (ADR-0009)** so ratified design is never misread as shipped mitigation, §9 (+residual item 8); `docs/data-privacy-gdpr-compliance.md` §7 (+the **elective** connector sub-processor bullet — metadata-by-default today, revisit if a per-subscription include-answers opt-in ships). No code, no migration, no `ci.yml` change.
