- **H14 (as-built, MERGED PR #69 `c5be98f`)** — the **webhook management + delivery-log UI + Zapier recipe** (H-map row 237; webhook-integration-design.md §4/§5; **ui**; depends H13a+H13b+H5b; **inherits the `webhooks` Starter+ gate** — no new gate). **Decisions locked with the user (AskUserQuestion): (1) page structure = list + per-endpoint detail** (the `submissions/Inbox`+`Show` precedent); **(2) sub-Starter tiers = HIDE entirely** (not a locked-upsell); **(3) test.ping = PURE INERTIA FLASH** (not a JSON fetch sidecar). **The linchpin:** the existing `/api/v1/webhooks` surface is **bearer-token authed** (`AuthenticateApiToken` + `feature:api_access` + token ability `manage:webhooks`), which a **session cookie can't carry** — so H14 is NOT the SPA calling that API. It's a **session-authed web layer** (`Tenant\WebhookController` + `WebhookEndpointPresenter` + 8 routes in `routes/tenant.php`) that **delegates to the SAME `WebhookEndpointService` + `WebhookTester` + models/policy/enums** the API controllers use → **no `/api/v1` change, no migration, no job, no OpenAPI drift** (Scramble only sees `routes/api.php`). Web routes carry `can:<ability>,WebhookEndpoint` + `feature:webhooks` but **NO `ability:`** (token-only). **(1) Pages** — `resources/js/Pages/webhooks/Index.vue` (summary `MdsStatTile`s for endpoint-cap + monthly-delivery quota, the `ZapierRecipe` card, an `MdsDataTable` of endpoints with status `MdsBadge`s, "Add endpoint" disabled at the plan cap) + `webhooks/Show.vue` (detail `<dl>` + delivery-health card + action bar test/rotate/edit/pause‖re-enable/delete + per-endpoint delivery-log `MdsDataTable` + `MdsPagination` via `?page` partial reload + per-row redeliver disabled unless active). **(2) One-shot secret** — create + rotate flash `newSecret` (`{id,name,secret}`) through the shared `flash` prop bridge (`HandleInertiaRequests`), read once by `SecretRevealModal` (copy-once, `SaveForLater` pattern) then gone; **never a durable prop** (`AuditRedactor` already strips it). `store` redirects to the new endpoint's Show; `rotateSecret` `back()`s. **(3) test.ping** — a normal Inertia POST → `back()->with('testResult', $tester->send($endpoint))`; `TestResultModal` reads the one-shot flash and renders delivered ✓ HTTP/latency or a parsed `[marker]`/error (the tester never throws, so pure-Inertia is safe). **(4) Presenter** maps fields INLINE mirroring `WebhookEndpointResource`/`WebhookDeliveryResource` (does NOT reuse the `Api\V1` resources — cross-surface coupling; cleanly omits `secret`/`payload`/`signature`) and **offset-paginates** deliveries (not the API's cursor) so `MdsPagination` gets `current_page/last_page/total/per_page`; the cap/quota summary comes from `EntitlementService::usage()`/`quota()`. **(5) Gating** — new `auth.can.manageWebhooks` (= `webhooks.manage`); nav item (`nav-model.ts`, `activity` glyph) gated on **both** `manageWebhooks` AND `entitlements.features.webhooks` (a new optional `NavItem.feature` filter in `Sidebar.vue`) so a no-feature tier never sees a destination it would only bounce off (`feature:webhooks` → `FeatureGateException` → `back()+toast`). **(6) DS** — `status-variant.ts` gains 7 webhook descriptors (endpoint active[reused]/paused[warning]/disabled[neutral]; delivery pending/delivering/succeeded/**failed=warning (retries remain) vs dead_lettered=danger (terminal)**) + Badge stories (light+dark) + a `status-variant.test.ts`; **no new DS component** (secret field = readonly `MdsTextInput`+copy; detail = `<dl>`). Zapier recipe = a pure help `MdsCard` (Catch-Hook steps + HMAC footnote), no backend. **Gates — ALL runnable ones GREEN, verified via the Docker containers** (host CLI lacks `pdo_pgsql` + the `rolldown` win32 binding, so run in-container per the env note): **Pint; PHPStan L8 = 0 on the 7 touched app/db/routes files; controller-gate/migration-lint/job-payload-lint; vue-tsc BOTH tsconfigs; `npm run build` (Vite); Vitest full suite 489/490** (the 1 fail = `token-references.test.ts` timing out under full-suite happy-dom load — **passes isolated**, a pre-existing flake, not mine); **Pest `docker exec …-app-1 … pest tests/Feature/Webhooks/*` = 19/19 green, 126 assertions** (`WebhookWebTest` 16 — route Inertia prop-shape via `->component('webhooks/Index', false)`, create/rotate/pause/re-enable-resets-breaker/delete/test-flash/redeliver+404+non-active-toast/authz/SSRF-validation/feature-gate-bounce — + `WebhookEndpointPresenterTest` 3). **CI-only suites — both GREEN on the first PR run:** Storybook axe (new Badge stories reuse existing axe-clean variants) + Playwright responsive-axe (added `/webhooks` + a seeded webhook-detail interaction; `E2eSeeder` seeds 1 active "Zapier" endpoint w/ a delivery spread + 1 breaker-paused "CRM sync"). The full Pest suite was NOT re-run end-to-end (my changes are additive: new files + additive shared-prop/nav keys), but every new webhook test + the Inertia-render path through the changed `HandleInertiaRequests` is green. **Narrowings → H15+:** per-endpoint `include_answers` opt-in still deferred; the locked-upsell state (Option B) noted but not built; a dedicated `dns_rebinding_blocked` delivery status still rides the `[marker]` excerpt.
- **H13b (as-built)** — the **webhook feature extensions** (H-map row 236; webhook-integration-design.md §5; depends H13a; backend+api, **inherits** the `webhooks` Starter+ gate — no new gate). Extends the H13a engine with the five deferred capabilities. **Decisions locked with the user (AskUserQuestion): (1) test.ping = SYNCHRONOUS inline** (no delivery-log row, no `DomainEventType`/CHECK change, no quota touch); **(2) payload archival = OVERSIZED-AT-CREATION threshold**; **(3) manual redeliver = EXEMPT from re-metering**. **(1) Manual redeliver** — `POST /webhooks/{endpoint}/deliveries/{delivery}/redeliver` (`WebhookDeliveryController::redeliver` → `WebhookEndpointService::redeliver`) resets a terminal delivery to a fresh `Pending` attempt (attempt_count→0, cleared response fields) and re-dispatches `DeliverWebhookJob` with a **new scalar `skipMeter` ctor param** — so the delivery-time SSRF re-validation re-runs but the monthly `webhook_deliveries` quota is NOT re-consumed (metered on first send; mirrors auto-retries). Controller guards: nested-ownership **404** (`delivery.webhook_endpoint_id !== endpoint.id`) + not-active **409** (re-enable first, since the job guard only delivers to Active endpoints — the PATCH `status=active` re-enable already resets the breaker, verified in H13a). **(2) test.ping** — `POST /webhooks/{endpoint}/test` (`WebhookEndpointController::test` → new **`WebhookTester`**) builds a synthetic `{event_type:'test.ping'}` envelope (the string lives ONLY in the body, never the DB `event_type` column, which is doubly pinned to `DomainEventType` by an enum cast + a CHECK), runs the **shared** `OutboundUrlGuard` + `WebhookSigner` + no-redirect/timeout POST, and returns `{delivered, response_status, response_time_ms, response_body_excerpt, error}` inline. No delivery row, no quota, works on any endpoint status. **(3) Payload archival** — new **`WebhookPayloadArchive`**: at delivery creation `WebhookEventDispatcher::fanOut` off-loads an envelope over `webhooks.payload_archive_threshold_bytes` (default 32 KiB) to attachment storage (`AttachmentKind::WebhookPayloadArchive`, `uploaded_by` null = system write, skips the staff storage-quota gate), stamps the dormant `webhook_deliveries.payload_attachment_id` seam, and trims the inline `payload` to `{archived:true}`; `DeliverWebhookJob` **reads the full envelope back** before signing (so the signed body is always real). New `webhook_delivery` **morph alias** (morphMap, not enforceMorphMap) owns the archive row; `payload_attachment_id` stays FK-less (as H13a shipped it). It does NOT reuse `AttachmentStorageService::store()` (coupled to `UploadedFile` + a media field) but mirrors its key convention. **(4) Dual-secret rotation grace** — alter-only migration `…000005` adds `webhook_endpoints.secret_previous` (`text`, `encrypted` cast) + `secret_previous_expires_at`. `POST /webhooks/{endpoint}/rotate-secret` (`WebhookEndpointService::rotateSecret`) moves the current secret → `secret_previous` for a configurable window (`secret_rotation_grace_seconds`, default 24h), mints a new `secret` (returned once via `revealSecret()`); **`WebhookSigner::signatureHeaderFor($endpoint,…)`** — the single builder both the job and the tester call — signs with the current secret and, while `activePreviousSecret()` (the lazy grace predicate) is non-null, ALSO the previous one, comma-joining the two `sha256=<hex>` values (Stripe-style) so a receiver mid-rotation verifies with EITHER. `AuditRedactor` redacts both secrets; the resource exposes `secret_previous_expires_at`. **(5) Circuit-breaker auto-disable notification** — `DeliverWebhookJob::finishFailed` dispatches a new queued-mail **`WebhookAutoDisabledNotification`** (H3 substrate; `#[Queue(Mail)]`, scalar payload, on-demand notifiable via `Tenant.owner_user_id → User.email` — the `ReconcileTenantUsageJob::notifyOverage` recipe; `job-payload-lint` EXEMPT) once, on the **edge** when the breaker trips Active→Paused (the job guard guarantees a single fire). **Gates: Pest 1334 (+16 across 6 new files** — redeliver behaviour + redeliver-API guards + test.ping + rotation/dual-sig + archival + auto-disable, all via the committing `Http::fake`/`Notification::fake` recipes); PHPStan L8 **0** on all new/changed files (the ~76 remaining are the known local baseline, none in touched files); Pint / controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint green; **openapi.json regenerated** (+3 paths, **Redocly clean**, `OpenApiContractTest` extended). Migration `2026_07_26_000005`. **CI lesson (from the H13a re-run):** the local openapi verification runs only the drift-diff, NOT `redocly lint` — every controller method that becomes an operation MUST have a PHPDoc first line (Scramble maps it to the operation `summary`; the `operation-summary` rule fails otherwise). H13a's first PR run failed on exactly this (4 H13a methods lacked docblocks); fixed on the branch before merge. **Narrowings → H14:** the management + delivery-log **UI** + Zapier; the per-endpoint `include_answers` opt-in stays deferred (archival is proven via a low test threshold until a large-payload producer exists).
- **H13a (as-built)** — the **webhook engine core** (H-map row 235; webhook-integration-design.md, tech-arch §7.4, data-dictionary §14/§15; depends H2+H4+H5a; gate `webhooks` Starter+). The repo's **first outbound HTTP**. Decisions locked with the user: **(1) monthly `webhook_deliveries` quota = HARD cap** (over → `dead_lettered` `[quota_exceeded]`, not sent); **(2) subscribable events = all four** `submission.created`/`form.published`/`form.opened`/`form.closed`. **(1) Tables** — `webhook_endpoints` + `webhook_deliveries`, strict RLS, uuidv7, **composite FKs** (ADR-0002 §D5): `webhook_endpoints.(tenant_id,form_id)→forms(tenant_id,id)` (added the missing `forms (tenant_id,id)` unique) + `webhook_deliveries.(tenant_id,webhook_endpoint_id)→webhook_endpoints(tenant_id,id)`, both cascade; unique `(webhook_endpoint_id,event_id)` = idempotency; partial retry index `WHERE status='failed'`. **`secret` is `text` not varchar(255)** — the `encrypted` cast (repo's first app-owned) overflows 255; masked-suffix in the API, plaintext once on create. **(2) Enums** — new `WebhookEndpointStatus`/`WebhookDeliveryStatus` (CHECK from `values()`); **reuses `DomainEventType`** for `event_types`/`event_type` per its docblock (NO separate `WebhookEventType`). **(3) Fan-out** — 4 thin **auto-discovered synchronous** listeners (the `MeterSubmissionUsage` shape) → one shared `WebhookEventDispatcher::fanOut()` that matches active + subscribed + form-scoped endpoints and `firstOrCreate`s a delivery per `(endpoint,event_id)` (idempotent; absorbs the H12a sweep's at-least-once) carrying the enriched **`DomainEvent` envelope** as payload (enriched `SubmissionCreated`/`FormPublished` to the §3 shape; `FormOpened`/`FormClosed` already carried `form_id`), then dispatches `DeliverWebhookJob` on the `webhooks` queue. **(4) Delivery** — `DeliverWebhookJob` (`TenantAwareJob`, `#[Queue(Webhooks)]`, scalar `(tenantId,deliveryId)`): first-attempt-only monthly-quota hard-cap + meter (`QuotaGuard::hasRateQuotaRemaining` — the H5c seam's first consumer) → **delivery-time SSRF re-validation** (DNS-rebinding) → **HMAC-SHA256 over `"{occurred_at}.{body}"`** (`WebhookSigner`, `X-Webhook-*` headers) → POST **no-redirect** + 10s timeout → record success (breaker reset) / failure (`next_retry_at` per the **1m→…→72h ladder** in config, dead-letter at max_attempts, **breaker auto-pause at 20** consecutive). The retry ladder lives in `next_retry_at` (NOT `$tries`/`backoff()`); **`SweepWebhookRetriesJob`** (`MaintenanceJob`) fans out `SweepTenantWebhookRetriesJob` → `WebhookRetrySweeper` every 5 min. **SSRF** — new `OutboundUrlGuard` (the single authority for both save-time `PublicHttpUrl` rule + deliver-time re-check; blocks private/loopback/link-local/reserved via `filter_var(...NO_PRIV_RANGE|NO_RES_RANGE)`, allowlist escape hatch). **(5) Entitlements** — new 8th gauge `UsageMetric::WebhookEndpointsCount` (+ CHECK-recreate migration) enforced by `QuotaGuard::assertCanCreate`; `PlanCatalog` endpoint cap Starter 3 / Pro 10 (Business/Enterprise = unlimited placeholders per the existing convention — the matrix's 25 not special-cased); the `webhooks` flag + `webhook_deliveries` quota were **already seeded** (no change). **(6) API** — `WebhookEndpointService` (create hard-caps + mints secret + audits under the pre-existing `webhook_endpoint` alias, secret auto-redacted) + thin `WebhookEndpointController` (CRUD) + read-only `WebhookDeliveryController` (delivery log, excludes payload/signature) + `Store`/`Update` requests + `WebhookEndpointResource` (masks secret) + `WebhookEndpointPolicy` (`webhooks.manage`, registered) + explicit Group-B routes (`ability:manage:webhooks` + `can:` + `feature:webhooks`). **DNS-rebinding/quota outcomes recorded as `[marker]` in `response_body_excerpt`** (keeps the 5-value status enum). **Gates: Pest 1318 (+49 across 6 files)** — signer/SSRF unit, endpoint API (CRUD/masking/feature-gate/cap/authz/RLS), fan-out (idempotency/scoping/all-four-events), delivery job (committing `Http::fake` — repo's first: success/meter/5xx-retry/no-redirect/breaker/dead-letter/SSRF-block/quota), retry sweep (committing), + `ScheduleDeclarationTest`/`DomainEventEnvelopeTest`/`EntitlementEnums`/`EnforcementMode` updated for the 8th metric. PHPStan L8 0 on all new/changed files; Pint / migration-lint / controller-gate / job-payload-lint green; **openapi.json regenerated** (+3 webhook paths, `OpenApiContractTest` extended). Migrations `2026_07_26_000001..000004`. **Narrowings → H13b/H14:** `test.ping`, manual redeliver, payload archival (`payload_attachment_id` is a dormant nullable seam), dual-secret rotation, breaker-open notification, all UI + Zapier; idle-in-transaction during the POST is accepted (no locks, worker-bounded); IPv6-only hosts fail-closed at SSRF.
- **H12b (as-built)** — the **scheduled-forms UI** over the H12a backend (H-map row 234; ungated, all tiers; depends H12a). Plan of record: `…read-progress-md-if-the-starry-matsumoto.md`. **(1) Builder** — a new **`ScheduleModal.vue`** opened from a **"Schedule" toolbar button** (the XLSForm-import `MdsModal` precedent), writing `PATCH /forms/{form}/schedule` via Inertia **`useForm().patch`** so per-field 422s (ordering / IANA tz / positive cap) surface inline via `MdsFormField :error`; native `<input type="datetime-local">` (the DS has no date primitive) + `MdsSelect` tz + `MdsNumberInput` cap; "Clear schedule" sends nulls. `BuilderPresenter` now surfaces the raw `opens_at`/`closes_at`/`timezone`/`max_responses` + the **server-canonical `DateTimeZone::listIdentifiers()`** list (so a client-built list can't drift → no 422). **(2) Timezone interpretation** — `UpdateFormScheduleRequest` parses the naive wall-clock **in the submitted zone** and **NORMALIZES TO UTC** before storage. **The linchpin (caught by a test):** Eloquent's datetime cast serializes a Carbon in its OWN zone as a naive string, and the `timestamptz` column reads that back in the (UTC) session zone — so a non-UTC offset is silently DROPPED unless converted (`CarbonImmutable::parse($raw, $tz)->utc()`); enforcement stays absolute. **(3) Guest runtime** — `SchemaResponse.form` gains a typed `schedule` block; `App.vue` renders a full-screen **closed/opens_soon/capacity_reached** state (`MdsEmptyState`) — **fresh-load only; the resume path is NEVER acceptance-gated** (honours H12a's grace window). **Offline fail-closed**: `RuntimeSession.submit()` blocks an offline submit for a **scheduled/capped** form (its load-time acceptance can flip and can't be re-verified) — **unscheduled forms keep the G8b offline outbox** untouched; a device-local draft still saves. A **mid-fill 403** (`form_closed`/`form_not_open`/`max_responses_reached`) maps to a new **`schedule` `ErrorKind`** → App shows the state. All copy + predicates live in one testable **`lib/schedule.ts`**. **(4) Encode path** — `EncodeFormPresenter` emits the **twin `schedule` block** via a shared pure **`FormScheduleView`** (both presenters delegate → can't drift); `Encode.vue` shows a **banner + disabled submit** when `acceptance != open` (form stays visible for staff). **Gates:** vue-tsc clean (both tsconfigs); Pint / PHPStan L8 **0** on changed files; Pest — `FormScheduleSettingsTest` (+TZ-interpretation, +BuilderPresenter props), new `EncodeFormSchedulePresenterTest` (5), guest + `ScheduledFormAcceptanceTest`/`MaxResponsesCapacityTest` + Forms suites regression-green; Vitest `schedule.test.ts` + `error-normalizer` schedule-403 cases (CI-only on Windows); a seeded **Closed Survey** (`/f/closed-survey`) + a `public-runtime-axe` closed-state scan; **no `openapi.json` drift** (`OpenApiContractTest` green — the guest schedule block delegates to `FormScheduleView` but keeps its `@return` shape). **Decisions locked with the user:** TZ = wall-clock-in-zone stored UTC; builder = modal-from-toolbar-button; encode = banner+disabled-submit; offline fail-closed **scoped** to scheduled/capped forms; resume never acceptance-gated. **Narrowings → H13a:** timezone picker is a native `MdsSelect` (full IANA list, **no typeahead**); native `datetime-local` (no styled DS date primitive) — both deferred; `capacity_reached` still doesn't emit `form.closed` (H12a: synchronous guard only).
- **H12a (as-built)** — the **scheduled-forms backend** (H-map row 233; ungated, all tiers; depends H2 + H4). Plan of record: `…read-progress-md-if-the-drifting-treehouse.md`. Adds an optional open/close window + response cap to the durable form record and enforces both at the write path. **Two decisions locked with the user: (1) draft-vs-close = GRACE WINDOW** (a save-and-resume draft STARTED before `closes_at` may still be promoted after close, bounded by its own 30-day `draft_expires_at`; a NEW submission/draft cannot start once closed); **(2) per-form `forms.timezone` column** (IANA, default `'UTC'` — there is NO `tenants.timezone`; the data-dictionary mention was never migrated), authoring/display metadata only (all enforcement is absolute `timestamptz` vs `now()`). **(1)** Alter-only migration adds `opens_at`/`closes_at` (timestamptz) / `timezone` / `max_responses` (int, null=unlimited) / `schedule_state` (varchar, the once-only event-emission key) to `forms`; a second alter-only migration adds a partial index `submissions (tenant_id, form_id) WHERE status <> 'draft'` for the cap COUNT. No RLS re-emit (row-predicate policies). **(2)** New pure **`FormSchedule`** (isBeforeOpen/isAfterClose/initialState/acceptance) is the one arithmetic source shared by the config writer, sweep, guard and presenter. New **`FormAcceptanceGuard`** (the single enforcement point): `assertCanStart` (fresh submit + NEW draft — throws before opens_at / after closes_at), `assertCanPromote` (grace — refuses only a draft whose `created_at >= closes_at`), `assertCapacity` (the H-map's **transactional COUNT-under-RLS** — form-row `lockForUpdate` + live `count() > max`, run INSIDE `SubmissionFinalizer::finalize()` so both submit + promote share it, after the head row exists → threshold `>` not `+1`; no-op for an uncapped form). Wired into `SubmissionPipeline::submit()` (Stage-2c) + `SubmissionDraftService::saveDraft()` (create branch only — an existing draft keeps autosaving post-close) + `promote()`; the `Form` is loaded RLS-scoped from the version/draft and threaded into `finalize()`. **(3)** New **`FormNotAcceptingSubmissionException`** (entitlement-family shape: `code()` `form_not_open`/`form_closed`/`max_responses_reached` + `status()` 403 + `details()` with the boundary/cap figures), rendered in `bootstrap/app.php` (API envelope / web toast). **(4)** New `DomainEventType::FormOpened`/`FormClosed` (no migration — not DB-pinned) + concrete `FormOpened`/`FormClosed` events (scalar payload, `for(Form)`). **(5)** The **state-flip sweep**: `FormScheduleSweeper` (two locked passes, CLOSE-FIRST so a form past both boundaries closes directly and never emits a spurious open; each pass filters the PRIOR `schedule_state` → exactly-once) run by a `MaintenanceJob`→`TenantAwareJob` fan-out (`SweepScheduledFormsJob`/`SweepTenantScheduledFormsJob`, copy of the reaper), scheduled `everyFiveMinutes()`. **Events are emitted with plain in-tx `event()` (not `ShouldDispatchAfterCommit`/`DB::afterCommit`)** — verified those are UNTESTABLE under `RefreshDatabase`'s uncommitted outer transaction; mirrors `ReconcileTenantUsageJob`'s in-tx side effect (at-least-once; H13 dedupes). **(6)** Config write = `FormService::setSchedule` (`forceFill` explicit keys + recompute `schedule_state`; `FormException::invalidSchedule` backstop) + `UpdateFormScheduleRequest` (conditional `after:opens_at`, IANA tz, `max_responses>=1`) + thin `FormScheduleController` + `PATCH /forms/{form}/schedule` (`can:update,form`, **ungated**). **No audit for the flip** (job-context `AuditLogger` would be malformed; siblings don't audit config writes) — the H4 dep is satisfied by extending the event catalog + emitting. **(7)** `PublicFormPresenter` gains an additive `schedule` block (opens_at/closes_at/timezone/max_responses + live `acceptance` open|opens_soon|closed|capacity_reached + `remaining`); a closed form still returns its schema (enforcement is write-path only). **Gates: Pest 1271 (+30)** — `FormScheduleTest` (pure, in Feature — the datetime cast needs the container's connection resolver), `ScheduledFormAcceptanceTest` (7, incl. the HEADLINE grace test), `MaxResponsesCapacityTest` (4), `ScheduledFormSweepTest` (5, committing `workOneJob` recipe + `Event::fake`), `FormScheduleSettingsTest` (4), `PublicFormSchedulePresenterTest` (5), `ScheduleDeclarationTest` (+1 `*/5 * * * *`); PHPStan L8 0 on new/edited files (also annotated the pre-existing `Form::$save_and_resume` @property H10 left off), Pint / controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint green, **openapi.json regenerated** (additive `schedule` block; `OpenApiContractTest` green). **Narrowings → H12b**: capacity does NOT emit `form.closed` (synchronous guard only; only the time-close boundary emits); presenter `acceptance`/`remaining` are advisory prep; the mint/schema endpoint doesn't 403 a closed form (so H12b renders the closed state); capacity isn't gated at draft-create (authoritative at finalize).
- **H11 (as-built)** — **basic dashboard KPIs** (H-map row 232; ungated, all tiers; depends H2). Replaces the dead landing page — a bare closure route rendering three hardcoded `—` tiles (Forms/Submissions/Members) + two DISABLED "Create form" buttons — with a real controller + aggregation service returning real, **visibility-scoped** counts. **(1)** New **`DashboardMetricsService`** (`final`, bound **`scoped()`** in `AppServiceProvider` beside `EntitlementService`/`QuotaGuard`; shares the request's one `ResourceGrantResolver`). One method `forUser(User): array{forms:int, submissions:int, members:int|null}` — **live COUNT-under-RLS, no new table/job** (the `EntitlementService::computeGauge()` doctrine: a synced counter drifts, a COUNT under the tenant GUC is always correct; the **H2 dep is satisfied by the substrate merely existing** — materialization stays a documented seam if load ever demands it). **forms** = non-archived forms; **submissions** = finalized (`status != draft`, mirroring the H10 inbox default-hide); **members** = active `TenantUser`s (accepted-only, distinct from the billing `ActiveSeats` gauge). **(2) Visibility** reuses the F7 `Submission::scopeVisibleTo` / `dashboard.org.view` boundary: Owner/Admin/Viewer get tenant-wide totals; a Form Editor/Reviewer's forms + submissions are scoped to `ResourceGrantResolver::grantedFormIdsQuery`, and the org-level **Members count is withheld (null ⇒ tile omitted)**. **(3)** Thin single-action **`DashboardController`** (`__invoke` → `Inertia::render('Dashboard', ['kpis' => …])`); the route (`routes/tenant.php`) swaps the closure for the controller and **keeps NO `can:` gate** — every role lands on the dashboard after login, so scoping is the service's job, not the route's. **(4)** New **`MdsStatTile`** design-system primitive (self-contained card surface + icon badge + value + label, tokenized — lifts the hand-rolled `.dash__stat` CSS out of the page per rule #2) + a Storybook story (axe-scanned) + a Vitest mount test; exported from the DS barrel. **(5)** `Dashboard.vue` now `defineProps` the `kpis`, renders `MdsStatTile` tiles (Members only when non-null, values `toLocaleString`'d), turns both disabled buttons into working `router.visit('/forms')` actions **gated on the already-shared `auth.can.manageForms`** (hidden, never dead-disabled), and shows the empty state only when `forms === 0` (refreshed copy). **Gates: Pest 1241 (+4)** — `DashboardMetricsServiceTest` (org-wide counts w/ archived-form + draft-submission exclusion; form-editor own-forms scope + null members) + `DashboardKpisTest` (route prop-shape, both roles); **Vitest +2** (`StatTile`); PHPStan L8 **0** on new files, Pint / controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint green, vue-tsc clean, Vite build green; **no migration, no new job, no `/api/v1` route → no openapi drift**. **Standing lesson (re)learned:** `php -d memory_limit=… artisan test` does NOT lift the runner's limit — `artisan test` spawns a CHILD php process that keeps the container's 128M default and OOMs the full suite; run **`php -d memory_limit=-1 vendor/bin/pest`** directly instead. Narrowings: no time-window/trend metric (would need a `submissions.submitted_at` index + a chart primitive) — all-time counts only; materialized rollup deferred; no client Page-mount test (route-level Pest covers prop→render).
- **H10 (as-built)** — the **save-and-resume UX** over the H9b surface (H-map row 231; form-filling-ux-flow §5.2; depends H9b/H5b; gate `save_and_resume` Starter+). Plan of record: `…read-progress-md-if-the-flickering-robin.md`. **Locked with the user (all three recommended options): include the per-form toggle; reconcile newest-wins non-destructive; persist the current step server-side.** **(1) "Save and finish later"** — a new `SaveForLater.vue` (button + `MdsModal` dialog) in the guest runtime, self-hiding via `useDraftFlow()` when the form hasn't opted in; provided by `RuntimeSession` only when `schema.form.save_and_resume` and not a G8c conflict-review session. It upserts the durable server draft (`ApiClient.saveDraft` → `POST …/draft`, sending the **FULL retained answers**, not the pruned submit set) and shows a copyable `resume_url` + an optional "email me the link" (`finish_later` → the queued `ResumeLinkNotification`). **(2) Welcome-back banner** — resume-link entry (`/f/resume/{token}`) now works end-to-end: `main.ts` reads `data-resume-token`; `App.vue` branches to a **standalone `resumeDraft()`** (`GET …/drafts/{token}` — its own auth context, NOT through the share-token `withFreshToken`), seeds `retainedAnswers` + the draft's uuid + step + locale, and renders `WelcomeBackBanner.vue` ("Welcome back — you're N% complete", page-local, mirrors `SummaryBanner`'s role/focus). **The uuid-reuse is the promote linchpin**: `createFormRuntime` gained `initialClientSubmissionUuid` so a resumed submit routes through `promote()` (flips the same draft row), never a duplicate `submit()`. **(3) Two-tier reconciliation** — a pure `lib/reconcile.ts`: on resume entry, if a same-`form_version_id` local Dexie draft also exists, **newest-wins by last-saved timestamp** (server `last_saved_at` vs local `updated_at`), loser preserved (never deleted), a note on the local-wins case; a checksum mismatch (stale-across-republish) or no local ⇒ server wins silently. Version drift after restore rides the **existing** G8c `handleDrift`→`reschema` remount (which also clears the resume seed). **(4) Tenant-configurable expiry** — net-new `tenants.draft_ttl_days` (nullable ⇒ 30-day default) + a **net-new Owner/Admin tenant-settings surface** (there was none): `TenantSettingsController@updateDrafts` + `UpdateDraftSettingsRequest` (partial-write, `can:tenant.settings.manage`, writes only the subdomain tenant via `forceFill`), a "Drafts" card on `Settings/Index.vue` (`MdsNumberInput`, stamp-once hint). The TTL is a **single choke point**: `GuestDraftController` resolves the tenant's days and passes `SubmissionPayload::$ttlDays`; `SubmissionDraftService::createDraft` stamps `now()+ttl` (fallback 30) — **stamp-once**, the reaper is untouched. **(5) Persisted step** — net-new `submissions.draft_current_step` threaded through `saveDraft`/resume-read; resume-read also returns `last_saved_at` + `locale`. **(6) Per-form toggle** — net-new `forms.save_and_resume` (default false) + `FormService::setSaveAndResume` + `PATCH /forms/{form}/save-resume` (`can:update,form` + `feature:save_and_resume`) + an `MdsCheckbox` in `Builder.vue` (gated on the feature); `GuestDraftController` adds a per-form `save_resume_disabled` 403 (the resume-READ stays allowed so a disabled form never strands saved data). `PublicFormPresenter` exposes `save_and_resume = form.save_and_resume && tenantAllows` (the tenant half mirrors `RequireFeature`'s fail-open, so the SPA flag and the route never disagree). **(7) Admin draft filter** — drafts were *visible by default* in the inbox (no status predicate); `SubmissionInboxPresenter` now **default-hides `status=draft`** unless the status filter opts in, and surfaces `completeness_percent`/`last_saved_at`/`draft_expires_at` ("Saved · N%" in `Inbox.vue`). **Not needed (verified, not skipped):** the "web `SubmissionException` render" the plan floated — the encoder promote route is `/api/v1` (already 409 `submission_version_superseded`); no web caller hits `promote()`, so the deliberate web-default stands. **Gates: Pest 1237 (+9)** — `DraftSettingsTest` (4), `GuestDraftRuntimeTest` (+4: per-form 403 / step round-trip / TTL-from-setting / no-restamp), `SubmissionInboxTest` (+1 default-hide); **Vitest +15 (CI)** — `reconcile` (5), `api-client` saveDraft/resumeDraft (6), `runtime` seeded-uuid (2), `components` resume-mode (2); **new `public-runtime-axe` save-for-later dialog scan** (E2eSeeder opts clinic-intake into save-and-resume). PHPStan L8 **0 errors** (full analyse), Pint / controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint green, **vue-tsc clean**, **openapi.json regenerated** (additive: `save_and_resume` on the guest schema, `last_saved_at`/`draft_current_step`/`locale` on resume-read, `save_resume_disabled` 403; `OpenApiContractTest` green). Migrations `…000008` (tenant TTL) / `…000009` (draft step) / `…000010` (form toggle), all alter-only. **Narrowings → H12a**: the draft-vs-**close** rule stays a forward seam (closed-form/scheduled/`max_responses` don't exist yet; only version-superseded is real today, and it's handled via the existing API 409 + drift path). Local-wins reconciliation does **not** push to the server (phase-1 autosave is device-local) until the next explicit "finish later" — acceptable this phase. Reaped-draft attachment GC still a future increment.
- **H9b (as-built)** — the **save-and-resume surface** over the H9a substrate (H-map §H9b, row 230; form-filling-ux-flow §5.2; depends H9a/H3). Makes the inert draft substrate reachable. **(1) The resume token** extends `GuestShareToken`/`GuestShareTokenService` (still stateless HMAC, no DB row) with a `sid` (draft `submissions.id`) claim + a `typ: 'resume'` discriminator, signed with a **second domain-separated key** (`guest-resume-token.v1`, `config/guest.resume_token`, 30-day TTL) so share↔resume tokens can't be confused at BOTH the key layer and the claim layer (`mintResume()`/`verifyResume()`; `verify()` rejects a resume-typed payload). **(2) A pre-RLS `EstablishGuestDraftContext`** (the `EstablishGuestTenantContext` sibling) verifies the resume token → `TenantContext::apply` → `forget()` in `terminate()`. **(3) Group-C** (`api/v1/public`): `POST f/{shareToken}/draft` (`GuestDraftController` → `saveDraft`, mints+returns a resume token, and on `finish_later`+contact-email queues the resume email) + `GET drafts/{resumeToken}` (`GuestDraftResumeController` → the draft's answers + a fresh short-lived share token, so a resumed device drives the ordinary schema/save/submit endpoints — **no resume-token-keyed write routes needed**); **both gated `feature:save_and_resume` (Starter+)** — a paid convenience, not never-block. `POST f/{shareToken}/submissions` is now **draft-aware**: an existing draft for the uuid is `saveDraft`-then-`promote`d, honouring the H9a "same-uuid finalize goes through `promote()`, never `submit()`" invariant (skips the `form_updated` check on that branch; promote() runs its own version guard). A thin web shell `GET /f/resume/{resumeToken}` (`GuestFormController@resume`, subdomain) opens the SPA with a fresh share token + `data-resume-token` (the emailed link works now; the answer-restore UX is H10). **(4) Group-B** `POST submissions/{submission}/promote` (`SubmissionPromoteController`, `ability:write:submissions` + new `SubmissionPolicy::promote` `can:` gate) finalizes a draft recording the acting encoder — the OCR review-and-confirm seam (H18/H19). **(5) The resume email** = a queued `ResumeLinkNotification` (`#[Queue(Mail)]`, scalar payload, on-demand notifiable — the `TenantInvitationNotification` pattern; H3's first product consumer; in `job-payload-lint` `EXEMPT_JOBS`). **(6) The draft-expiry reaper** = cross-tenant `ReapExpiredDraftsJob` (`MaintenanceJob`, fans out) + `ReapTenantDraftsJob` (`TenantAwareJob`, **`forceDelete()`s** `status=draft AND draft_expires_at < now()` under RLS — HARD-delete so the partial-unique index frees the re-used uuid; `submission_answers` cascades), scheduled `dailyAt('03:40')`; a partial index `submissions (tenant_id, draft_expires_at) WHERE status='draft'` (alter-only migration `…000007`). **(7)** `SubmissionConflictException::draftAlreadyFinalized()` now carries a distinct `draft_already_finalized` 409 code (via `code()`; `contentConflict()` keeps `submission_conflict`). **Gates: Pest 1228 (+~26)** — `GuestShareTokenServiceTest` (+5 resume/cross-type), `GuestDraftRuntimeTest` (13: upsert/overwrite/email/gate/resume-read/RLS/draft-aware-finalize/409/shell), `SubmissionPromoteApiTest` (4), `DraftReaperTest` (2 committing, `workOneJob` recipe), `ScheduleDeclarationTest` (+1 cron), `OpenApiContractTest` (+3 paths). PHPStan L8 back to the 77 local baseline (two new errors fixed in build: annotated `Submission::$guest_contact_email`, `firstOrFail()` for the resume-URL host); Pint / controller-gate / job-payload-lint / migration-lint green; **openapi.json regenerated, no drift** (+3 paths, guest ones `security:[]`). Decisions locked with the user: gate the guest draft routes now; the admin route = encoder promote/confirm; send the resume email in H9b. **Narrowings → H10/H12**: the draft-vs-close/version-drift grace window (a draft on a superseded version fails promote with `submission_version_superseded`) stays H1f/H12a; the tenant-configurable expiry surface + per-form toggle + SPA restore UX (welcome-back banner, Dexie↔server reconciliation) are H10; reaped-draft attachment GC is a future increment.
- **H9a (as-built)** — the **server-side DRAFT substrate** (H-map §H9a; technical-architecture §4.1; docs/ux/form-filling-ux-flow §5; depends H2), the foundation shared by save-and-resume (H10) + OCR (H18/H19). **Backend-only — no route, no feature gate, no OpenAPI change; the suite stays inert.** The linchpin: a draft is written repeatedly under one `client_submission_uuid` with changing content, so the pipeline's same-uuid-different-content **409 is SUSPENDED for drafts and RE-ARMED on promotion**. **New `SubmissionDraftService`** (singleton service seam, *not only* a route — an OCR job can stage a draft with no guest request / no uuid): **`saveDraft()`** runs Stage-1 normalize (a draft rejects MALFORMED, not INCOMPLETE) + the Stage-2a published-version guard, SKIPS Stage-3 + the 409 check, and find-or-creates by uuid — the first save creates a `status=draft` row (stamping `completeness_percent` + `last_saved_at` + `draft_expires_at = now()+30d` via `DRAFT_TTL_DAYS`), every later save OVERWRITES the answer doc in place (`lockForUpdate` + in-txn `status===Draft` re-check; a 23505 create-race folds into the update = last-writer-wins). **`promote()`** runs the FULL Stage-3 EXACTLY ONCE (before any txn opens, so a semantic failure leaves the draft resumable + untouched), then flips the SAME row `draft→submitted` IN PLACE (a stable `submissions.id` for H9b's resume token; `submitted_at` set, `draft_expires_at` nulled, **source preserved**), delegating the shared Stage-4 tail; fires `SubmissionCreated` **once** (so H5b metering counts a draft only on promotion — never on a save). **Re-arm is AUTOMATIC**: the finalized row stores `AnswersContentChecksum::of($normalizedDraftAnswers)` — byte-identical to what `submit()` would store for the same raw answers — so a later same-uuid `submit()` replay is the ordinary 200 no-op / 409. **The Stage-4 tail is extracted into a new `SubmissionFinalizer`** (media re-point + `submission_answers` upsert + typed `submission_answer_index` + PostGIS `submission_geo_index` + the `created` audit) that BOTH `SubmissionPipeline::submit()` and `promote()` reuse — **`submit()` stays byte-identical** (proven by `SubmissionPipelineTest` passing UNMODIFIED); the answer-doc write moved `create`→`updateOrCreate` so promotion overwrites the draft's own row. **Migration**: alter-only `Schema::table('submissions', …)` adds `completeness_percent`/`last_saved_at`/`draft_expires_at` (per-SUBMISSION — resume is per-submission; the already-migrated `submission_answers` pair is per-ANSWER) — **no RLS re-emit** (row-predicate policies), **no status CHECK** (`'draft'` is already the enum + the column default), alter-only so migration-lint skips it. **New pure `DraftCompleteness`** (relevance-UNAWARE % = answered / answerable over the normalized answers; excludes note/page_break/calculated/hidden; a repeatable section = one unit; div-0 → 0 — drafts must not run the engine). **`SubmissionConflictException::draftAlreadyFinalized()`** (the save-vs-promote race; reuses the 409 `submission_conflict` envelope — a distinct API code is deferred to H9b's route). **Gates: Pest +17** — `SubmissionDraftServiceTest` (11, incl. the HEADLINE proving checksum-suspend-across-N-saves / re-arm-on-promote / Stage-3-once, + geo/media/double-promote/source-preserve/no-uuid-OCR) + `DraftCompletenessTest` (6 unit); `SubmissionPipelineTest`/G4/G5/G6/`AuditSitesTest` (80) regression green **unmodified**; PHPStan L8 ZERO on every new file (local 77-baseline unchanged); Pint / migration-lint / controller-gate / job-payload-lint green; **no `openapi.json` drift** (no `/api/v1` route; there is no `SubmissionResource`, so the new columns surface nowhere). Decisions taken (engineering defaults): `draft_expires_at` **stamp-once** (not sliding); promote **preserves source**; double-promote = **idempotent no-op**; `submit()` **left byte-identical** — the "a same-uuid finalize goes through `promote()`, never `submit()`" invariant is H9b/H18a's to honour. **Narrowings → H9b/H10**: the resume token (extends `GuestShareToken`, scoped to draft `submissions.id`) + pre-RLS verify middleware + Group-C upsert/resume + Group-B admin-draft route + the draft-expiry reaper (`MaintenanceJob`, needs H3) + the tenant-configurable 30-day setting (H10); the softDeletes × partial-unique-index re-used-uuid edge is **pre-existing (not worsened)** — H9b's reaper should hard-delete expired drafts (or re-scope the index to `deleted_at IS NULL`); when H12a lands, its `max_responses` COUNT + close predicate must filter `status != 'draft'`; a true two-connection lock-race is asserted only structurally (the `RefreshDatabase` single-txn limit).
- **H5c (as-built)** — the **entitlement RETRO-GATE** (ADR-0008 §D4/§D5/§D7, pricing-matrix §2/§3; depends H5b), closing the three things H5b deferred. **(1) The grandfather storage + merge-day backfill.** New strict-RLS **`legacy_overrides`** table — one row per tenant, a `feature_flags` JSONB override map, **`bigint identity` PK** (internal per-tenant state, the `usage_counters` precedent → no `HasUuidv7`; also makes the raw-SQL backfill trivial, no uuid-in-SQL on PG17). `EntitlementService::legacyOverrides()` now reads it (memoized per tenant — `snapshot()` calls `feature()` once per flag key), consulted AHEAD of the plan flags (the existing `array_key_exists` precedence); `forget()` clears it too. The **merge-day backfill** is the G10a `CollaboratorBackfill` pattern exactly: a *separate*, later-timestamped migration (`2026_07_23_000005`, `$withinTransaction=false`) delegating to a reusable, pure-unit-tested `LegacyOverrideBackfill` body that runs ONE cross-tenant `INSERT … SELECT FROM tenants … ON CONFLICT (tenant_id) DO NOTHING` on **`pgsql_privileged`** (BYPASSRLS — the app connection under FORCE RLS with no tenant GUC would stamp zero tenants), grandfathering the five keys. On `migrate:fresh` (empty DB) it stamps nothing (born-gated); it only bites against a live, populated DB on deploy. **(2) Feature-gating the five capabilities** via a new **`feature:<key>` middleware** (`RequireFeature`, first consumer of `feature()`) throwing **`FeatureGateException`** (402, `feature_not_available`, `{feature}` details; rendered API-envelope / web-toast like `QuotaExceededException`). **The linchpin decision: the gate blocks ONLY when a plan RESOLVES and denies the feature** — a null (unseeded/dev) plan passes through, the same "no catalog ⇒ no enforcement" stance as `QuotaGuard`'s null-quota, so the ~1150-test plan-free suite stays inert and only the new H5c tests seed plans. Gated (web + `/api/v1`): `xlsform_export`, `form_templates`, `field_library`; **`api_access` = one choke point** on the whole Group-B stack (after `AuthenticateApiToken`) + `POST auth/tokens` mint (list/revoke stay ungated); **`offline_sync`** gates the offline ENTRY only — the guest PWA manifest (in-controller 404, not the redirect-y middleware, since it's not `/api/v1`) + the authenticated `sync/manifest` — **not** `/sw.js` (no tenant context) and **not** `sync/submissions` (never-block replay, §D4), and the main guest form page `/f/{slug}` is untouched (respondents always reach it). **`form_templates`/`field_library` were NOT seeded flag keys** (route names only in Phase 2) → added to `PlanCatalog` at **Starter+** (matrix §3 + this increment). **(3) The `api_requests` monthly-quota 429**: new **`QuotaGuard::assertWithinRateQuota()`** (RateLimit class) + **`RateLimitExceededException`** (429 + `Retry-After` to the period reset) + **`EnforceApiRequestQuota`** middleware after the api_access gate + throttle, before `MeterApiUsage` (so a 429'd request is not itself metered). **A null OR 0 quota never 429s** — 0 is the Free "no access" sentinel, reached only via a grandfather override, so a grandfathered Free tenant's granted `api_access` is not hollowed by its 0 quota. **`webhook_deliveries` = reusable seam** (the guard method + exception work for it; no dispatch code until H13 — no middleware/route). **Client-hide** (rule #3): `entitlements` prop typed in `inertia.d.ts` + a `useEntitlements()` composable; gated affordances hidden in `Builder.vue` (XLSForm buttons, Fields⇄Library toggle) + `forms/Index.vue` (template links, save-as-template). **Gates: Pest 1186 (+35)** across 7 files (`LegacyOverrideBackfill` unit; Feature `LegacyOverride`/`RateQuotaGuard`/`OfflineSyncGate`/`FeatureGateWeb`/`ApiAccessGate`/`ApiRequestQuota`); PHPStan L8 ZERO on every new file (one `nullsafe.neverNull` fixed the H5a `tier()` way — explicit `=== null`); Pint / migration-lint / job-payload-lint / controller-gate green; vue-tsc clean; **no `openapi.json` drift** (middleware-only route changes). Migrations: `legacy_overrides` DDL (`…000004`) + backfill (`…000005`). Narrowing: the backfill's populated cross-boundary run is proven by the migration + the behavioural `LegacyOverrideTest` (not a RefreshDatabase test — the privileged session can't see uncommitted test tenants, the G10a limitation); the super-admin console plan-selector UI stays deferred.
- **H5b (as-built)** — **entitlement ENFORCEMENT** (ADR-0008 §D4/§D8/§D9, pricing-matrix §2/§4; depends H5a). Makes the H5a read-model *bite*, along the three ratified classes. **The enforcement CLASSIFIER is now code, not prose**: new `EnforcementMode` enum (HardBlock/NeverBlock/RateLimit/Unclassified) + `UsageMetric::enforcementMode()`/`isGauge()` (the `ResourceCapacity::anyPermission()` precedent — promoted from the H5a docblock; `values()` untouched so the DB CHECK is unaffected). **Gauges are enforced by a LIVE COUNT/SUM under RLS, never a running aggregate** (the H12a `max_responses` precedent + the G10a/H2 "a synced counter is a landmine" lesson — gauges move *down* on archive/delete/remove): new `EntitlementService::countGauge()` (non-memoized, the guard's authoritative pre-write read) + `liveGauge()` (memoized, for the read-model); `usage()` now branches gauge→live / flow→counter so `snapshot()` shows a just-created form immediately. **`QuotaGuard::assertCanCreate(metric, incoming=1)`** (scoped) throws **`QuotaExceededException`** (402, metric-specific `code()`, `{metric,limit,used}` details; rendered in `bootstrap/app.php` — API envelope, web toast) when `quota !== null && countGauge + incoming > limit`; null quota (unlimited / off-tenant / unseeded catalog) never blocks — **which is why the pre-H5b suite, seeding no plans, is inert**. Three hard-block sites: `FormService::create` (top of its tx — covers template-instantiate transitively, the sole `Form::create`), `AttachmentStorageService::store` (**staff only — `$uploadedBy!==null`; a GUEST respondent's upload is NEVER blocked**, the same never-block principle as submissions, since it is the same choke point for both), `TenantMembershipService::invite` (**reserve-on-invite** — counts Active+Invited, blocks a genuinely-new seat; accept is count-neutral; decline/remove free it). **Never-block `submissions_count`**: a **synchronous, auto-discovered** `MeterSubmissionUsage` listener on the post-commit/created-only `SubmissionCreated` increments via **`UsageMeter`** (concurrency-safe `INSERT … ON CONFLICT (tenant_id,metric,period_start) DO UPDATE`, whole body swallow-and-logged so a broken meter can never lose a respondent's already-committed data). `exports_count` metered at `SubmissionExporter::stream`; `api_requests` metered by a new **`MeterApiUsage`** middleware on the Group-B stack (after auth+throttle — **meter only; the monthly-quota 429 is H5c**, to avoid back-dooring an API feature-gate before the grandfather override exists). **The cross-tenant rollup**: `RollUpUsageCountersJob` (a `MaintenanceJob`, ADR-0007 §D3) fans out one **`ReconcileTenantUsageJob`** (`TenantAwareJob`, `scheduled-maintenance`) per active tenant, which reconciles `submissions_count` from the authoritative `submissions` table (the never-block self-heal), materializes gauge levels + stamps `limit_snapshot`/`subscription_id`, and sends the never-block **`QuotaOverageNotification`** upsell once when submissions first cross quota; scheduled `dailyAt('02:40')`. **Rate-limit binding = forward seam** (decision): queue-fairness ceilings stay **uniform** (pricing-matrix §4); the §D9 `tier_resolver`/`tiers` config is left as the documented Phase-4 seam (untouched). `SuperAdminService::assignPlan` gains a `forget()` (first caller of the H5a seam). **Test-fixture churn**: `E2eSeeder` assigns `acme` a Professional plan (unlimited forms) so the ~10 seeded forms don't trip Free's cap; Pest gains `assignPlanTier()`/`assignUnlimitedPlan()`. **Gates: Pest +37** across 7 files (`EnforcementMode` unit; Feature `FormQuota`/`StorageQuota`/`SeatQuota`/`SubmissionNeverBlock`/`UsageMeter`/`UsageRollup` + extended `EntitlementService`/`ScheduleDeclaration`) — the rollup is a **committing** test (`config('queue.default')='database'` + `workOneJob('scheduled-maintenance')`, the `ProbeMaintenanceJob` precedent). PHPStan L8 ZERO on every new file; Pint / migration-lint / job-payload-lint (`QuotaOverageNotification` in `EXEMPT_JOBS`) green; **no migration** (`usage_counters` exists); no `openapi.json` drift (metering middleware is transparent, no route). **Discovered in build**: Laravel 11+ **auto-discovers** `app/Listeners` — a manual `Event::listen` double-registered the meter (double-counted submissions), so the listener is auto-discovered only. Narrowings: `api_requests`/`webhook_deliveries` monthly-quota enforcement + `api_access`/xlsform/etc. feature-gating are **H5c** (behind the grandfather override); gauge counter rows are reporting-only (`usage()` reads gauges live); an invite-of-an-already-invited-email resend is proven count-neutral by inspection (the `pgsql_auth` re-lookup can't commit under `RefreshDatabase`).
- **H5a (as-built)** — the **entitlement model** (the last of the four backend substrates; data-dictionary §16/§17/§18, ADR-0008; depends H1b/H2/H4). **100% net-new** — no entitlement code existed. Three string enums (`PlanTier` 5, `BillingInterval` 2, `UsageMetric` 7, each with `values()` feeding a DB CHECK); **deliberately NO `SubscriptionStatus`/`PlanStatus`** (`stripe_status` is free text; plans use `is_active`). Three tables: **`plans` is the ONE table with no `tenant_id`/no RLS** (global catalog; **NOT** added to `EXEMPT_TABLES` — migration-lint short-circuits on no-`tenant_id` tables; verified `relrowsecurity=f`); **`subscriptions`/`usage_counters` carry `tenant_id` + strict RLS** (`relrowsecurity=t/t`). `usage_counters.id` is **`bigint identity`** (so `UsageCounter` omits `HasUuidv7`). **`tenants` untouched** — by design no `plan_id`; current plan derives from the active subscription. Kept **Cashier-shaped, no Cashier** (Stripe columns dormant; admin-assigned). **`EntitlementService`** (`scoped()`, memoized per tenant — the `ResourceGrantResolver` precedent): `currentPlan()` = active subscription's plan (`Subscription::scopeActive` = `stripe_status∈{active,trialing}` ∧ `ended_at IS NULL`) else the seeded **`free`** fallback; `feature()`/`quota()`/`usage()` (0 in H5a — metering is H5b) + a `legacyOverrides()` **grandfather seam** (empty until H5c, resolves ahead of plan flags) + `snapshot()`. **Read-only "props" = a shared Inertia prop only** (`entitlements` in `HandleInertiaRequests::share()`, the `ui.theme` precedent) — **fail-closed off-tenant** (`snapshot()` null with no tenant context); **no styled page** (H5a is backend-only per rule #3). **Super-admin plan-assign** (`SuperAdminService::assignPlan` + `TenantAdminController::assignPlan` + `POST /admin/tenants/{tenant}/plan`): **adopt-tenant-context, NOT `elevated()`** (the H-map's literal wording superseded exactly as H4 dropped its own bypass) — one `DB::transaction`, adopt the affected tenant's context, upsert the `default` subscription **and** emit the **`subscription.updated`** audit (`AuditEvent::Updated` + `auditable_type='subscription'`, no enum change) atomically on the app connection, restore in `finally`; records the acting super-admin's `user_id` (`is_system_action=false`). **First new consumer of the H4 `AuditLogger`.** The **console selector UI is deferred** (a fast-follow; the admin `Tenants` page is a styled surface I can't verify locally — the backend assign is fully tested, mirroring H4 shipping the audits API with no UI screen). **`PlanSeeder`/`PlanCatalog`** seed all 5 tiers on the **default** connection (no RLS → no `pgsql_privileged` needed, least-privilege), idempotent on `code`, flags/quotas from pricing-matrix §2/§3 + ADR-0008 §D7 (`native_connectors`/`branding`=Starter+, `custom_domain`=Business), `business`/`enterprise` seeded `is_active=false` (held from sale, still admin-assignable); wired into `DatabaseSeeder`+`E2eSeeder`. **Gates: Pest 1113 (+22)** (`EntitlementEnums` unit; Feature `EntitlementService`/`PlanSeed`/`SubscriptionRls`/`SuperAdminAssignPlan`/`EntitlementsSharedProp`); **PHPStan L8 ZERO on every new H5a file** (full analyse back to the 77-error local baseline; two real new errors fixed in build — a `nullsafe.neverNull` on `tier()` restructured to an explicit `=== null`, and `findOrFail(mixed)` widening to `Plan|Collection` → `where('id',(string)…)->firstOrFail()`); Pint / migration-lint / controller-gate / job-payload-lint green; **no `openapi.json` drift** (no `/api/v1` route — assign is a web route, entitlements is a shared prop); `migrate:fresh --seed` green. Narrowings: usage metering + the rollup job are **H5b**; the grandfather override storage/backfill is **H5c**; the console plan-selector UI + a cross-tenant plan-listing console (an `elevated()` SELECT read) are deferred.
- **H1b (as-built)** — **ADR-0008 (entitlement & metering)**, a **docs-only** PR on the H1a/ADR-0007 precedent (Status/Context/Options/Drivers/Decision[blockquote+Concern|Choice+`Dn` bullets+Method note]/Consequences/Alternatives/When-to-Revisit/Related/References; **NO scorecard**). Ratifies the premises H5a builds on: **D1** admin-assigned plans, **no Cashier** (Stripe columns present but dormant — Phase-4 adoption is additive; `stripe_status` stays free text, no enum/CHECK); **D2** current plan = the active subscription's plan (no `tenants.plan_id`; "active" = `stripe_status∈{active,trialing}` ∧ `ended_at IS NULL`; unsubscribed ⇒ **free**); **D3** one `EntitlementService` reads `plans.feature_flags`+`plans.quotas` (null=unlimited), read-only in H5a; **D4** enforcement split — hard-block (`forms_count`/`storage_bytes`/`active_seats`), **never-block** (`submissions_count` — a respondent's data is never rejected for the tenant's billing), rate-limit (`api_requests`/`webhook_deliveries`); **D5** grandfather **indefinitely** via a per-tenant legacy override applied at H5c (seam ships in H5a); **D6** Business = build+gate, **hold from sale** until Track B (seeded `is_active=false`, admin-assignable), no 99.5% clause in Phase 3; **D7** `native_connectors`=Starter+, branding **unbundled**→Starter+, `custom_domain` stays Business; **D8** period-bounded `usage_counters` + `limit_snapshot`; **D9** usage rollup is a cross-tenant `MaintenanceJob` (ADR-0007 §D3); **D10** super-admin plan-assign audited via the **H4 `AuditLogger`** (`Updated`/`subscription`) under the affected tenant's adopted context — **not** `elevated()` (H-map wording superseded, mirroring H4's own dropped bypass), the first new consumer of the audit substrate. Doc reconciliation: `pricing-feature-gating-matrix.md` §3 gains the `native_connectors`+`branding` rows and an embedded-payments→Phase-4 annotation. Ships **docs-only** — no product code, no migration, no `ci.yml` change.
- **H4 (as-built)** — the **audit-log + ONE event-catalog substrate** (audit-compliance-logging-spec.md, data-dictionary §13/§22, tech-arch §4.1/§7.4), the next consumer of H2. **The `read:audit_log` ability and `audit_log.view` permission ALREADY existed** (seeded Owner+Admin, mapped in `ApiAbilities`) but were dead — no `audits` table/model/`Auditable` trait existed (0 migration hits) — so the catalog stays at **28 permissions** (no seeder/ability change); H4 fills the missing substrate. **Append-only ledger enforced at the DATABASE:** new `TenantIsolation::appendOnly` shape emits strict tenant SELECT + INSERT and **NO update/delete policy** — under FORCE RLS a policy-less command is denied for every role, so "immutable, never deleted" (§13) is a Postgres invariant, not a promise (mutation-proven: a raw UPDATE/DELETE returns 0 rows, the row is byte-unchanged). Deliberately **NOT `nullable_global`** (which widens SELECT to `OR tenant_id IS NULL` and would leak platform rows to every tenant — audits needs the opposite); `tenant_id` FK is **`nullOnDelete`, not cascade** (FK actions bypass RLS; a cascade would wipe the ledger on a tenant hard-delete). **The "one event catalog" is SEPARATE from audit, by design (§4.1 vs §7.4):** audit rows are written IN-transaction (atomic with the change) through one `AuditLogger` — there is **no `Auditable` model observer** in H4 (deferred to the first broad model-lifecycle increment); domain events are a POST-commit forward-seam for H13 webhooks + notifications. New abstract `DomainEvent` carries the envelope `{event_id (uuidv7, generated once), occurred_at, tenant_id, api_version, SCALAR data}` (§D5-safe, so H13 attaches a queued listener without touching an emission site); `DomainEventType` is the one catalog (`submission.created`, `form.published`). Per **user decision 1**, the pre-existing bare `SubmissionCreated` was **folded in** (scalar payload, no model) + the submission `created` audit written in the pipeline; new `FormPublished` raised post-commit by `PublishService`. **`AuditRedactor`** (pure, unit-tested) strips the fixed secret list + PII flags, records stripped names in `redacted_fields`, and handles the §2.1 erasure special case (both sides placeholdered) by construction. **6 `TODO(audits, Phase 1)` sites RETIRED** (grant→`created`, revoke→`deleted` with `old_values` captured *before* the hard-delete, `transferOwnership`→2×`permission_changed` against the `users` row + `tenant.updated`, publish→`published`, suspend/reactivate→`tenant.updated`) + the submission `created` audit. **Super-admin suspend/reactivate — a design refinement discovered in build:** rather than the planned elevated-connection + INSERT bypass, the audit is written UNDER the AFFECTED tenant's context on the app connection, **ATOMIC with the status change (one transaction)** — the strict INSERT passes with NO bypass or extra GRANT (least-privilege AND atomic, strictly better than the plan's best-effort split, and it sidesteps the cross-connection FK visibility problem the elevated path hit in tests). The planned `applySuperAdminBypass('audits',['INSERT'])` was **dropped as unused** (deferred to a future NULL-tenant platform-audit action). Records the acting super-admin's `user_id` (**user decision 2**; `is_system_action=false`, the tenant reads an opaque actor uuid its own `users` RLS can't resolve to a name); context save/restore via `applyLocal` so nothing leaks on the central host. **Read-only `GET /api/v1/audits`** (`ability:read:audit_log` + `can:viewAny,Audit`, cursor-paginated newest-first, RLS-scoped) + `AuditPolicy` (Owner/Admin) + `AuditResource` (returns already-redacted values); `openapi.json` regenerated (+`/audits`). **No UI screen** (spec §5 defers it; H4 is backend+api). **Gates: Pest 1091 (+27)** (`AuditRedactor`/`DomainEventEnvelope` unit + 3 `appendOnlySql` cases + Feature `AuditAppendOnlyRls`/`AuditSites`/`AuditSuperAdminSites`/`AuditApi`; full suite green under a 2G memory bump — the container's 128M CLI default OOMs the whole run, a known local artifact); **PHPStan L8 ZERO errors on every new H4 file** (app-wide baseline unchanged — the errors in edited files sit only in unchanged method bodies); Pint / migration-lint / controller-gate / job-payload-lint green; no `openapi.json` drift. Regression suites for the five modified services (Submissions/Scoping/Membership/Forms/Guest/Api/Admin, ~440 tests) all green — the added in-transaction audit writes broke nothing. **One CI-only follow-up:** the first `openapi.json` drifted because `scramble:export` had run before the `audits` table was migrated into the container's dev DB, so Scramble (which introspects DB column types) flattened every `AuditResource` field to `string`; regenerating **after** `php artisan migrate` produced the precise types CI expects (`is_system_action`→boolean, `user_id`/`ip_address`/`user_agent`→nullable, `redacted_fields`→`array|null`). **Gotcha to carry: always `migrate` before `scramble:export` or nullable/typed columns flatten to `string` and CI's drift diff fails.** Narrowings: no `Auditable` observer trait (deferred); super-admin SELECT bypass / cross-tenant audit search deferred to a support console; the redactor's `$piiAnswerKeys` branch is dormant until the `submissions.edit` fast-follow.

- **H3 (as-built)** — the **queued-mail substrate**, the first *consumer* of H2. Per user decision it takes ADR-0007's **framework-native** path (queued Notifications + the linter's now-populated `EXEMPT_JOBS`) over the recommended substrate-native `TenantAwareJob` wrapper, **and also queues the framework auth mail** (email verification + password reset). The engineering was making that path *safe* — three framework behaviours were **verified against `vendor/`** (the H2 lesson), and each shaped the design: **(1)** `NotificationSender::queueNotification` reads the **`#[Queue]` attribute** for notifications (`:246`), so the linter's R2 attribute and the *runtime* queue agree — no `viaQueues()` duplication; **(2)** `SendQueuedNotifications` serializes the notifiable via `SerializesModels` **before** the handler under a NULL GUC — so a queued `$user->notify()` would restore a `User` under fail-closed RLS (the §D5 hazard, worse for a *pending* invitee) → **every H3 mail is sent to an ON-DEMAND notifiable (`Notification::route('mail',$email)`) carrying SCALAR-only payload** (pre-built URLs + display strings resolved in-request), so no model is ever serialized and no tenant context is needed on the worker; **(3)** the fairness limiter closure returns `Limit::none()` for anything that is not a `TenantAwareJob` (`QueueServiceProvider:76`) — so **mail is deliberately NOT per-tenant fairness-limited** (documented; the new `mail` ceiling in `config/queue-fairness.php` is forward-looking for a future `TenantAwareJob` fan-out, commented as inert for queued notifications). New `QueueName::Mail = 'mail'` at **priority slot 3** (user-facing, above the pending-state queues, below `submissions`/`webhooks`), threaded **byte-identically** through the linter's `ALLOWED_QUEUE_CASES`, `config/queue-fairness.php`, the compose worker `--queue=`, `docs/deployment-infrastructure.md` §6/§8 and the observability doc — a test now guards `workerOrder()` === compose === docs (the one gate CI didn't enforce). `TenantInvitationNotification` → `ShouldQueue` + scalar (`tenantName` + a pre-built `acceptUrl` resolved in `TenantMembershipService::invite()` under live context — the old `acceptUrl()` moved to the service so no `Tenant` model rides the queue). Auth mail: `QueuedVerifyEmail`/`QueuedResetPassword` **subclass** the stock framework notifications and override only URL generation to return a pre-built scalar (reusing their wording/localization); `User::sendEmailVerificationNotification`/`sendPasswordResetNotification` sign the URL **in-request** (which also correctly captures the *request host*, unlike a naively-queued stock notification whose `toMail()` renders host-less on the worker). `app/Mail/TenantMail` is a thin **abstract, NOT-ShouldQueue** base — the H-map "app/Mail base" deliverable and forward seam for H9/H17 receipt/PDF Mailables (invisible to the job linter). **Gates: Pest +3 files** (`tests/Feature/Mail/QueuedMailContractTest` + `InvitationMailQueuedTest`, `tests/Feature/Auth/QueuedAuthMailTest`) + updated `MembershipLifecycleTest` (29 pass); Queue suite **44 unchanged**; **PHPStan level 8** clean on every H3 file (the 77 app-wide are the same documented local baseline, 0 on CI); `job-payload-lint` green (7 classes; `EXEMPT_JOBS` populated, R2/R3/R4 still enforced on exempt classes); Pint green. **Verification went two-process** (the path in-process tests structurally can't cover): a job dispatched to `mail` was drained by the real compose `worker` container and delivered to **Mailpit** with the correct subject and the pre-built signed URL in the body — proving the on-demand + scalar-URL override works end-to-end. Narrowings: mail is not fairness-limited (above); secret-in-queue accepted (invite/reset token + email transit `jobs`/`failed_jobs`, but token TTL 7d < failed-job retention 14d and `failed()` never logs the payload); `TenantMail` ships with no concrete subclass (forward seam by design).

- **H2 (as-built, split H2a + H2b per user decision)** — the async substrate proper, implementing ADR-0007 D1→D13. **The headline is that FIVE of the ADR's thirteen sub-decisions were contradicted by the installed framework (laravel/framework v13.18.1) and had to be corrected — the ADR itself (`:178`) instructed verifying its API claims at implementation time, and that instruction paid for itself five times.** Every correction is written back into ADR-0007 as a `> Corrected in H2 (as-built)` block, and every one is **proven by mutation**, not argued. **(1) §D4's after-edge is `JobAttempted`, not `JobProcessed`/`Queue::after`.** `Worker::process()` raises `JobProcessed` on the **success path only** (`:561`) and diverts to `handleJobException()` on a throw; `JobAttempted` is dispatched from the `finally` (`:566-570`). Implementing §D4 *literally* therefore leaks the tenant static out of **every job that throws or is released** — the §Context 6 defect merely relocated. Mutation: applying it fails `WorkerContextHygieneTest`'s "clean after a job that THREW" **and only that test**. **(2) §D4 must SAVE/RESTORE, not flush.** Under `QUEUE_CONNECTION=sync` — every CI job — `SyncQueue` raises both events **inline in the caller's stack**, so a blind flush wipes the ambient request context mid-request. A LIFO stack in a `private static` (load-bearing: `Dispatcher::createClassCallable` resolves a **fresh listener per dispatch**, so instance state would silently degrade back into a flush) is correct under both a worker (saved value null ⇒ restore *is* flush) and sync. Deliberately **NOT** gated on `connectionName !== 'sync'` — that gate would make the listener **dead code in every CI job**, i.e. exactly the unverifiability ADR-0007 was written to end. Mutation: a blind flush fails precisely the two sync tests and no worker test. **(3) §D7's `$tries = 3` INVERTS §D9.** `RateLimited` enforces its ceiling with `$job->release()`, and `DatabaseQueue::release()` (`:328`) re-pushes preserving the **already-incremented** `attempts` — so three fairness *deferrals* land the job in `failed_jobs`, converting "this tenant is throttled" into "this tenant's jobs permanently failed". `TenantAwareJob` therefore declares **no `$tries` at all**: `retryUntil()` (time-based, blind to releases) + `$maxExceptions = 3`. Mutation is instructive — adding `$tries = 3` *while keeping* `retryUntil()` changes **nothing** (a non-null `retryUntil` makes `maxTries` dead code in the worker), so the test kills the ADR-as-written combination (`$tries` **without** `retryUntil`) and the code comment says so rather than overclaiming. **(4) §D6's "every job MUST declare `$queue`" is a FATAL ERROR as written.** `Illuminate\Bus\Queueable:29` declares `public $queue;` **untyped**; redeclaring it with a different initial value fatals any class using the trait. Replaced by the framework's `#[Queue(QueueName::X)]` attribute — which takes a `UnitEnum`, is read first by `Dispatcher::pushCommandToQueue()`, **is inherited from a parent** (`ReadsClassAttributes::getAttributeInstance` walks `getParentClass()` in a do/while, so `MaintenanceJob` names its queue once for every sweep), and is *more* statically checkable than a string literal. **(5) §D13's status comparison would have taken the whole queue down.** `tenants.status` is a stancl virtual-column attribute **explicitly not cast** — a plain `?string` — so `$tenant->status !== TenantStatus::Active` compares string to **enum object**, is **always true**, and would release-then-delete **every job for every tenant on the platform**: a silent total outage no existing test would catch. Fixed structurally with `Tenant::isActive()` + `scopeActive()` (comparing `->value`/`tryFrom()`, matching `SuperAdminService`), so §D3's fan-out and §D13's guard share **one** definition; `JobContractTest` additionally pins `'status'` in `getCustomColumns()`, because removing it silently relocates the value into the `data` json column and the predicate then matches **nothing**. **Two feared regressions were REFUTED before a line was written and confirmed after:** the `after_commit => true` flip and the flush listener break **zero** existing tests — the only dispatch site (`AttachmentStorageService:82`) is reached by no un-faked test (`G6FieldTypesTest:49` is `Queue::fake()`; `:254` calls `->handle()` directly). Reassuring for the diff, damning for the status quo: it confirms the substrate was exercised by **nothing**, so the new integration test carries all the weight. **The enforcement is three independent layers**, per user decision to bump `composer.json` `"php"` `^8.3 → ^8.4` (every real environment was already 8.4; only the manifest lagged): a **PHP 8.4 abstract property hook** (`abstract public string $tenantId { get; }` — a job that forgets it is a **class-load fatal**, verified), the **runtime uuid assertion** in `final handle()` (which also names the all-zeroes `BelongsToTenant` sentinel separately, since a job carrying it serialized an *absent* tenant — a different bug from a malformed one), and **`scripts/job-payload-lint.php`**. `handle()`/`middleware()`/`failed()`/`retryUntil()` are all `final` because an override loses the assertion, `applyLocal`, the transaction or the §D9 limiter **with no runtime symptom on a warm worker**; extension goes through `handleForTenant()`/`jobMiddleware()`/`failureContext()`/`retryWindowHours()`. `$deleteWhenMissingModels` is `final false`, sealing §D5's named trap. **The linter departs from its two siblings in two documented ways:** it is **two-pass** (`NameResolver` resolves names but not ancestry, so pass 1 builds an FQCN→parent map; a parent resolving outside `app/` fails **loudly** rather than passing silently — the H3 queued-mailable case needs a decision, not a default) and it **never uses `->getLast()`** (unlike `migration-lint.php:146/:192`), so a shadow-named `TenantAwareJob` in another namespace cannot pass — **verified red**. Its §D5 rule is **inverted into a closed scalar whitelist** rather than a model detector, because "is this an Eloquent model" is **undecidable** from a type declaration: `SerializesAndRestoresModelIdentifiers` branches on the *runtime value* and `SerializesModels::__serialize` reflects over every non-static property, so `mixed`/untyped/`object` all reach `restoreModel()` invisibly. **Red-then-green proven per rule** (5 violations in one fixture, each with file:line + remediation), and it is wired into **BOTH** `composer.json`'s `quality` array **and a new `ci.yml` step** — mandatory, because **no CI job runs `composer run quality`**; `static-analysis` invokes the four scripts individually, so a composer-only registration would have gated nothing. **Verification went beyond the suite:** a genuine **two-process** run (dispatch in one container process, drain in a separate `queue:work`) took a real attachment `pending → skipped` — the path the in-process test structurally cannot cover — and then the **compose `worker` service** did the same unattended. That exercise found a real config defect the tests could not: `php:8.4-fpm-alpine` ships **no php.ini**, so the CLI `memory_limit` is exactly **128M**, and `queue:work`'s default `--memory=128` means the graceful over-budget recycle can *never* fire before PHP's hard limit does — turning a clean restart into a fatal OOM mid-job. Fixed with `--memory=112`. **`default` is deliberately ABSENT from the worker's `--queue=` ordering**: a job that escaped the linter should strand visibly (`select queue, count(*) from jobs group by 1`), not be silently rescued by the very worker whose gate it evaded. **Forbidden connections DELETED, not annotated** (user decision): `deferred`/`background`/`failover` are gone from `config/queue.php`, so `QUEUE_CONNECTION=deferred` now throws `InvalidArgumentException` loudly at boot — a gate rather than one more piece of prose-as-gate, which is the pattern ADR-0007 §Context indicts. `failover` had to go regardless since it referenced `deferred`. **Gates: Pest 1045 (+44)** across 7 new files in `tests/Feature/Queue/`; **PHPStan level 8 ZERO errors on every H2 file** (the 77 app-wide are the documented local baseline — `E2eSeeder.php` is untouched by H2); Pint green; `schedule:list` confirms `10 3 * * *`. **A PHPStan finding was a real bug, not noise:** it flagged `$job instanceof MaintenanceJob` as always-false inside a branch already narrowed to `TenantAwareJob` — dead code, since the two are unrelated classes and the null check already covered it. Narrowings recorded rather than papered over: `$timeout = 60` is **unverifiable in CI** (SIGALRM is armed only in `daemon()`, never under `--once`); the genuine §D8 **two-process race** is not reproducible in-process (the *ordering contract* is fully covered, the race is not — no committing-test precedent); `MaintenanceJob`'s **fan-out arm has no production caller** until the first rollup vertical, only `activeTenants()` tests; fairness buckets, overlap locks, the schedule mutex and `jobs` itself all contend on **one Postgres instance**; and `RateLimited` stores `md5($limiterName.$limit->key)`, so fairness buckets are **not greppable** by tenant in the cache table.
- **H1a (as-built)** — Phase 3's entry point: a **docs-only PR** carrying **ADR-0007 (async execution substrate)** + twelve document reconciliations, on the G5a/ADR-0006 precedent (PR #32 `d2a3ff7`). **The premise was that Phase 3 needs an async contract before code; the audit found the foundation is almost entirely prose.** Exactly one queued job exists (`ScanAttachmentJob`, `final`, defining **none** of `$queue`/`$tries`/`$backoff`/`$timeout`/`failed()`/`middleware()`); **nothing consumes the queue** (no worker or scheduler compose service; the only committed worker invocation is `queue:listen` in `composer.json`'s `dev` script); **the scheduler does not exist** (`routes/console.php` is 8 stock lines, no `withSchedule`, no `app/Console/`) while **four documents assert one** — `feature-backlog.md:85` said outright *"scheduler + email + async export already exists"*; and `onQueue(` has **zero hits** against a five-name catalog documented as binding in two places. **The keystone finding is a live defect two documents called "Mitigated":** `TenantContext::$tenantId` is a **process-lifetime PHP static** that `applyLocal()` never rolls back (it scopes only the DB GUC to the transaction), and there is **no `Queue::before`/`JobProcessing` listener anywhere** — so on a long-lived worker the GUC goes NULL while the static still holds the last tenant, and `BelongsToTenant` then injects it into the next job's reads and `creating` fill. RLS still fails closed (so no cross-tenant *disclosure* — reads return zero rows, inserts throw), but ADR-0002:125 and security-threat-model:40 both promised *"fails fast (explicit exception)"* and **it did not**; it survived only because no second job type exists. **ADR-0007 is `D1…D13`** and, per user decision, **deliberately departs from ADR-0006's skeleton by carrying no weighted scorecard** — that ADR scored a *spike* with measurements; this one has none, and §D9's ceilings are explicitly unvalidated assumptions, so a rubric would manufacture false precision. It uses ADR-0005's plain drivers + Concern/Choice table with ADR-0006's `**DN — …**` bullets. Substance: **D1** `database` driver on Postgres + plain `queue:work`, **superseding ADR-0005 rows 27–28 in part** (Redis/Memurai + Horizon) — the first *row-level* supersession in the repo, chosen over amendment because partial supersession is the honest description and avoids retiring a live ADR; **D2** `TenantAwareJob` (uuid assertion — the "fails fast" ADR-0002 §D6 promised — + `DB::transaction`/`applyLocal` + the `setPermissionsTeamId()` the HTTP middleware sets and **no job does**, without which any job calling `hasPermissionTo()` fails closed); **D3** `MaintenanceJob` for cross-tenant work, justified structurally — a cross-tenant transaction is **not expressible**, since the GUC is a scalar and `TenantIsolation::tenantMatch()` is flat equality with no set/any-tenant mode; **D4** the flush listener that makes those two docs true; **D5** scalar-ids-only (`SerializesModels::__unserialize` runs in `CallQueuedHandler::call` **before** `handle()` under a NULL GUC, so a model payload is a permanent failure) **with the `$deleteWhenMissingModels` trap named** so nobody "fixes" it into silent data loss; **D6** the five queue names now binding; **D7** `$tries`/backoff + `retry_after` **90→120** so `timeout < retry_after`; **D8** `after_commit => true` — **decided here, implemented in H2** — closing a real bug where `AttachmentStorageService:82` dispatches outside any transaction so a worker can beat the row and strand `virus_scan_status` at `pending` **forever**, structurally untestable under `sync` i.e. under every current CI job; **D9** the per-tenant job-rate ceiling that **closes R6**; **D10** no new tables (a `tenant_id`-bearing one trips migration-lint, and both escapes — RLS in the reservation hot path, or an exempt table holding tenant ids — are worse); **D11** `deferred`/`background`/`failover` forbidden for tenant work (under `deferred` the HTTP session GUC is still live, making a correct job and a free-riding one **indistinguishable in testing**); **D12** structured `failed()` + the never-stated constraint that any tenant-facing failure UI is a **cross-tenant read of an RLS-free table**; **D13** tenant-lifecycle handling + the explicit non-goal that `tenants.status` is read by **no** middleware/policy/RLS predicate today. **Verification caught six citation defects in the source plan before they were written** (three Explore agents, every `file:line` re-read against source): `handle()` is a `function (): void {}` closure not `fn() =>` (syntactically impossible for a statement list); "no guzzle/no Redis" was false (guzzle is transitive at `composer.lock:802`, Redis exists in infra — narrowed to *no first-party call site* / *no Redis-backed queue*); **`horizon:terminate` is not in `deploy.ps1` at all** (docs-only claim, so that correction stands but inverted); migration-lint Rule 1 is `:105-117` not `:99`; `TenantInvitationNotification` interpolates at `:33-35` not `:44`; and the webhook retry ladder is at §1 `:13`, not §4 `:49-61` — so the unbuilt banner was retargeted to the doc head. **Two H-map claims were REFUTED/downgraded and deliberately NOT written:** the *"RBAC docs say 27 permissions"* correction is **wrong** (§5 already enumerates 28 and names `scopes.manage` as the 28th;
