The first draft deferred — "a policy that already decided framing knows better" — which is false here: `PublicRuntimeSecurityHeaders` is *also* mounted on the authenticated manual-encode page (`routes/tenant.php`, for its OSM `img-src`), so deferring handed the guest runtime's `frame-ancestors *` to a **session-bearing page**, opening a clickjacking hole with the very change meant to close one. The sibling hazard is still live and is why the merge exists at all: if the middleware order ever reverses, `PublicRuntimeSecurityHeaders`' no-clobber guard trips and **the OpenStreetMap tile allowlist silently vanishes** — a blank map on one page with no error in any log. Both are pinned by `AppSecurityHeadersTest`'s last test, which asserts BOTH policies land on that one route. Do not add either middleware to `bootstrap/app.php`'s priority list. **Do NOT introduce `default-src`** — `PublicRuntimeSecurityHeadersTest`'s four negative assertions (`default-src`/`script-src`/`connect-src`/`font-src`) are a deliberate tripwire (piping-design §262); `frame-ancestors` was safe to state alone precisely because it has **no `default-src` fallback**, so it narrows nothing else. **`FormSlug::isTaken()` uses `withTrashed()` and that is a BUG FIX, not defensiveness:** `forms_tenant_id_public_slug_unique` has no `deleted_at` predicate, so a soft-deleted form squats its slug forever — a scope-respecting check calls it free and the write dies on a 23505. **The fix was mutation-verified**: reverting `withTrashed()` reddens the test with the exact wrong value. Note the two halves differ — `Rule::unique('forms', …)` queries the TABLE and already sees trashed rows correctly; the bug was only ever in the *suggestion* loop. **A slug change invalidates NOTHING** (asked and answered): share tokens are HMACs over (tenant, form, version) and resume links carry a token, not a slug, so a guest mid-form and a saved draft both survive a rename — only the old `/f/{slug}` dies, which is exactly what the modal warns about. Pinned in `FormShareTest`. **The guest toggle is `MdsCheckbox`, deliberately** — `MdsSwitch` is DSR §3.2's forward spec and belongs to **I5**; building it here would have collided with that row. **The Share panel's URL is a SERVER PROP, never `window.location`** — `TenantUrl::toPublic()`, because a custom domain serves the guest runtime only (ADR-0012 §D1) and a browser-derived host is wrong for exactly the Business tenants who paid for it. **The address preview is suppressed once it would restate the live link** — showing the same URL twice under two headings reads as two different addresses, the H24b1 contradiction class. **Per-form rate limiting / CAPTCHA is an UNBUILT PRD Feature #3 criterion**, not built here and not dropped: it is an explicit **I8** line item and a `docs/feature-backlog.md` row.

**H24b1 (do not re-open):** the chart scale is **derived**, and the derivation is in the tests, not in prose — `theme-overrides.test.ts` recomputes every ratio from `tokens/chart.json` and the CSS on each run, so darkening a series past its floor reddens the suite rather than the eye. **Do not "simplify" the two colour sets into one**: put the light column on the dark ground and four of five fail 1.4.11 (worst 1.56:1); the raw Okabe-Ito anchors fail white from the other end. **Do not add a `[data-accent]` rule for a chart token** — the pre-existing accent-scope guard tested only the five `--mds-color-*` families and would not have caught it, so a second assertion was added for `--mds-chart-*` specifically. **`--mds-chart-other/-grid/-axis` alias NEUTRAL primitives on purpose** and must not be given a dark re-point: the dark block already flips the ramp underneath them, and re-pointing them would freeze them against the flip. **Do not emit the sequential scale** until something consumes it. **NEVER interpolate a token name into `var()`** — `token-references.test.ts` regexes source and would hunt for a token literally named `--mds-chart-series-`; series colour is a static CSS class per index, which also keeps `stroke-dasharray` off presentation attributes. **The §D12 data table is pinned by VITEST and cannot be pinned by axe** — axe passes a chart that is a beautiful unreadable picture, so deleting a table assertion loses coverage no gate replaces. **The value domain always includes zero and the API does not offer to turn it off**; a degenerate domain (an all-zero month — the ordinary state of a quiet tenant) is widened rather than returning one tick, because every consumer divides by the span and the alternative is a silent `NaN` path. **No text lives inside the time-series SVG** — the canvas is stretched with `preserveAspectRatio="none"`, so text would stretch too; axis labels are HTML on a CSS grid and the y-column's half-line-height bleed is what puts a tick label's centre on its gridline, not a nudge. **The bar chart's label is HTML beside a one-`<rect>` SVG** for the same class of reason: SVG text neither wraps nor ellipsises. **Past five series the extras are TABULATED, not dropped** — folding them into an *Other* bucket is the caller's call. **The form label is resolved in `DashboardMetricsService`, never in `AnalyticsMetricsService::breakdown()`** (that shape is the byte-diffed API response) and the lookup is **`withTrashed()`**, because an org-wide reader's visible set is rooted on `Form::withTrashed()` and a soft-deleted form otherwise renders as a bar with no name. **The two draft tiles do not share a sentence**: `conversion_rate` is null only at a zero denominator, `median_seconds` is *additionally* null when the denominator is positive and nothing converted — collapsing them put "0% of 6 saved drafts" beside "No drafts were explicitly saved" on one screen. **Standing lesson reinforced — a defect the gates cannot see is found by LOOKING.** Every gate was green when that contradiction was on screen; it was caught in a screenshot, then turned into a test that was run against the unfixed component first and genuinely reddened. **Second standing lesson — a recorded flake is a liability, not a fact of life.** `token-references` had timed out intermittently since H21b and H21d1 found a real dangling token behind one such timeout; this increment adds files to the tree it walks, so the 5 s default was replaced with an explicit 30 s budget rather than pushing a known-dangerous flake closer to the edge.

**H24a (do not re-open):** the shape is ADR-0011's, implemented. **The three post-approval corrections are load-bearing and were each verified against the tree before being believed** — the join-side gap in §D2, the `E2eSeeder` no-op, and the `date_trunc` timezone hazard. **TWO MUTATIONS CAME BACK GREEN AND BOTH INDICTED THE CODE, NOT THE SUITE**: `applyCountableJoin()` had ZERO callers (the aggregator roots on `submissions` and joins OUTWARD, which is strictly better and makes §D2 structural rather than remembered — the helper and its drifted docblock are gone), and the export's three §D13 lines were ceremony until the aggregation moved INSIDE the closure. A third mutation — the permissions team-id restore in the export closure — stays green, and that one indicts the TEST: every analytics route's `can:` gate warms Spatie's role memo on the same User instance first, so the unwarmed case cannot be constructed on any route that exists. It is kept anyway, because the alternative is depending on an invariant enforced in another file, and the docblock says so rather than pretending the line is covered. **§D13 NAMES applyLocal() BUT THE GUC IS ONLY HALF THE TORN-DOWN CONTEXT** — the permissions TEAM id is gone too and `AnalyticsFormSet` consults `$user->can()`, so an org-wide reader could silently export their granted-forms subset: a SMALLER number, in a file, with no error. **The index addendum stayed ONE by WIDENING H12a's capacity index in place** rather than adding a second; the net count is asserted, so a later increment cannot quietly append one. **The shape is pinned STRUCTURALLY, not by `EXPLAIN`** — a repo lesson reused rather than relearned (`ScopeNodeScaleTest:120-128`: on a small table the planner tie-breaks unpredictably, so a plan-shape assertion measures the cost model, not the property). **§D6's scope-node axis has THREE dispositions, not two**: forms on a deactivated node fold into *Unassigned*, because dropping them makes `sum(buckets) < total` on the same screen — the §D2 disagreement in miniature. **§D5's denominator is `source IN (guest, manual)`**, a forward-dated defect fixed before it lands: `SubmissionDraftService` is a SERVICE seam and every `createDraft()`/`updateDraft()` path stamps `last_saved_at`, so the moment H18/H19 ship, OCR-staged drafts would turn the median into encoder review latency with no visible change. **Suppression has THREE states, never two** — suppressed / unavailable / a number — because `percentile_cont` returns NULL on an empty set and a careless shape renders suppression as 0%, which is §D5's failure wearing a different hat. **Both `hidden` and `calculated` are Indexable**: the projector never consults `field_type` and `SubmissionFinalizer` merges `$result->computed` into the map before projecting, so classification follows what happens, not what feels like respondent data.

**H1e (do not re-open):** the doc is **`docs/adr/0011-analytics-substrate.md`** — an **ADR, not a numbered design doc** (ADRs share Doc #7, so `docs/PRD.md:13`'s index correctly still tops out at **#27**), and it is **0011, not 0010**: 0010 stays reserved for H1d, and the ADR-0009 number-reuse precedent does **not** transfer, because payments was *cancelled and never written* (a permanent hole) whereas H1d is *blocked* and still expected (a temporary one). **THERE IS NO FORM-ENGAGEMENT EVENT STREAM IN PHASE 3** and H-map row 259 + its hazard note are rewritten in place to say so — a future session reading the original row will design a `form_events` table, a Group-C beacon, a Dexie telemetry queue and a partitioning scheme that this ADR deliberately declined, because `PRD.md:203-206`'s three acceptance criteria need none of them and the alternative was a fourth unauthenticated ingress class writing the highest-volume table in the system onto one self-hosted box. Do not "restore" it: the deferral is reasoned, and its two costs (offline respondents emit no events; "step 3" is not the same node for two respondents under a relevance-filtered graph, so branching funnel semantics is its own unsolved problem) are recorded for Phase 4. **THE DENOMINATOR ARGUMENT IS THE POINT OF §D5, NOT A CAVEAT.** A respondent who abandons leaves **no server row at all**, and the explicitly-saved drafts that do exist are hard-deleted nightly — so a completion funnel built on `count(status='draft')` returns **100% on a default-configured form and rises every night as the reaper deletes its own denominator**. `PRD.md:198` is amended in-repo; do not un-amend it, and do not ship an unqualified "completion rate" or "average completion time". `submitted_at − created_at` is ~0 for a direct submit (same transaction) and `finalized_at` is a **reviewer** timestamp, not a respondent one. **ANSWER-VALUE AGGREGATION IS OPT-IN AND PROSPECTIVE-ONLY.** `submission_answer_index` projects only `is_queryable` top-level scalars, only at finalize, and is **never backfilled** — flipping the flag indexes future submissions only *and* is a breaking-but-permitted schema change needing a new version. That is why the Phase-3 promise is submission **metadata**; do not widen it to "aggregate any question" without shipping the backfill job the ADR defers. Scalar *includes* `likert_scale` (a single chosen value) — do not write "likert is not indexable"; it is the two **grid** types that are not. **CHARTING IS IN-HOUSE SVG AND THE ARGUMENT IS NOT AESTHETIC:** `build-tokens.mjs` emits `var()` **references**, not resolved colours, and `useTheme` flips `data-theme-mode`/`data-accent` live, so a canvas library cannot resolve a token at paint time and goes stale on a theme flip. Five series is **derived** from the light+dark 3:1 luminance band, not chosen — which is also the proof that the dark re-point is mandatory. **No contrast ratios are printed in the ADR by design**; H24b measures and records them in DSR §4.1, because an ADR that prints ratios nobody computed is worse than one that prints none. **The surface is HIDDEN, not locked-with-upsell**, because Business is `is_active = false` — an upgrade CTA to an unpurchasable plan is a dead end, and the same fact is why H24b's e2e fixture must seed a Business-entitled tenant or the axe gate stays green over a page it never loaded. Finally: **`read:analytics` is a NEW ability but coins NO new RBAC permission** — `dashboard.org.view`/`dashboard.form.view` already encode exactly the org-wide-vs-own-forms split an analytics read needs. Do not fold the ability into `read:submissions` "to avoid another ability": that retroactively arms every already-minted token, which is the failure `ApiAbilities` documents twice in prose.

**H21d2 (do not re-open):** there is **ONE classifier**, `condition-model.ts`'s `toCondition(Node) → Condition | null`, and `condition-describer.ts` renders its sentence FROM the `Condition` it returns — so `say()` is TOTAL. Do not re-fuse a second classification into the describer "for prose": that is exactly how the rail and the editor would come to disagree about which expressions are safe to touch, which is the failure Doc #27 amendment D2 spent an increment avoiding. **The model must stay exactly as expressive as the describable set**, quirks included — a reversed comparison (`18 < ${age}`), a field-vs-field one (`${end} > ${start}`), a literal-vs-literal one (`1 = 1`). Do not "tidy" it into subject-is-always-a-field: that would silently narrow the RAIL's prose, and it is why both sides of a comparison are the same control rather than a subject and a value. `condition-describer.test.ts` passing byte-unchanged is the guarantee that the classification moved without its boundary moving. **THE PRINTER CHECKS ITSELF.** `serialize()` re-parses its own output and returns text only if it classifies back to a structurally equal `Condition`. Do not replace that with byte/token/depth constants — it is what turns three engine budgets into one refusal, and it is the only thing standing between a bad print and a draft that saves cleanly then fails at publish naming an expression the author never typed (**nothing validates `relevant_expression` on write**; `UpdateFieldRequest` is shape-only by decision). **The three refusals are real limits of grammar v2.0, not policy** — the lexer has NO escape sequences (so a value with both `'` and `"` is inexpressible), `lexNumber` has no exponent form, and `${…}` is ASCII `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*`. **NO NEW WRITE ENDPOINT, and do not add one.** The editor writes `target.relevant_expression = text; store.touch(uid, kind)` into the existing PATCH; that is what inherits `builderClient`'s typed 409 rather than re-implementing it, and `FormBuilderController::respond()` stays the single place a 409 is minted. **`ConfigPanel` is REPLACED, not coexisting** — one stored value, one editor on screen, mode DERIVED from the value and never stored. **"Read-only" constrains the rows, not the author**: an opaque condition keeps a fully editable textarea, and the guarantee is that nothing parses, re-prints or touches it. That guarantee is mechanical — emit only from a user mutation, never from a watcher, never on mount, and never a value equal to the stored one. Do not add a canonicalize-on-open convenience; two shapes in the never-rewritten test (`${age}   >   18` and `(${age} > 18)`) exist to catch precisely that. **A half-built row is held, not written** (`isComplete()` gates the write) — writing the complete subtree and dropping the rest would be a silent partial save. **Standing lesson reinforced for the third increment running — a mutation that reddens NOTHING indicts the SUITE.** Deleting the printer's deep-equality check was green across 106 tests because the case that looked like it covered that arm is caught one branch earlier by the re-parse throwing. **Standing lesson reinforced twice now — re-run `token-references` in isolation.** It hid a real dangling token in H21d1 and again in H21d2.

**H21d1 (do not re-open):** the canvas is a **second view of the builder's centre pane**, not a page — one `useBuilderStore`, both views kept mounted, and `store.select` drives the existing `ConfigPanel`. Do not "extract" it into its own route: an author reads the map and edits in place, and a second page needs a second copy of the draft. **THE RAIL IS CLIENT-DERIVED AND LIVE; THE GRAPH ANALYSIS IS SERVER-DERIVED AND FLUSH-SCOPED**, and the split is the decision rather than an accident. Node order, each raw predicate, its reading, its `${key}` chips and its syntax errors come from the store and the TS `ExpressionParser` between keystrokes; the three §6 notices come from `GET /forms/{form}/graph`. **Do NOT port `StepGraphInspector` to TypeScript** — that was weighed and rejected as a sixth R3-style mirror pair with no corpus standing over it, and the one-save lag it costs is **shown** on screen (a "rechecking" line; a failed check that says so) precisely so nobody "fixes" the lag by building the mirror. The refetch is gated on `store.whenIdle()` and keyed on a **logic fingerprint**, not a deep watch — a label edit cannot create or clear a cycle. **THE DESCRIBER REFUSES TO PARAPHRASE, BY DESIGN.** One operand it cannot express makes the whole reading opaque; there are no partial readings, and grouping is preserved rather than flattened. Do not "improve" it into describing more shapes without deciding, per shape, that the sentence is *complete* — a confident wrong sentence about a form that decides who is asked what is the failure §8 calls the disqualifier. **`status: 'opaque'` IS H21d2's "non-representable" predicate**; reuse it rather than writing a second classifier. **Nothing here PRINTS an expression** — the AST→text printer is still H21d2's to build. **The rail draws AUTHORED order, not the taken path**: it is deliberately not built on `StepProjection`/`visibleSteps`, because there are no answers while an author is editing; a simulator is the backlog's residue. **The `${key}` chips are a display affordance, not an authority** — the publish gate still decides whether a reference resolves. **`emptyAtOpen()`'s `ExpressionException` catch is load-bearing**, not defensive: it wraps the whole evaluation because both settle loops can throw, and it exists because this is the first caller to run the inspector against an unvalidated draft. **New standing lesson — a known flake can be hiding a real failure, so re-run it in isolation before waving it through.** `token-references` fails as a timeout in the full Vitest run and has been recorded as a flake since H21b; in H21d1 the isolated re-run reported a genuinely undefined `--mds-type-body-font-size`, which without a fallback invalidates the whole declaration and makes the element scale by inheritance under `[data-font-size]`. **Second standing lesson — check new copy against the DARK palette specifically.** `--mds-color-danger-text` is `danger-300` in dark and fails contrast on `bg-surface` at 13 px; the `status-*-fg` family is the on-surface pair. The dark scan caught it, not the light one.

**H21c (do not re-open):** the encode page **MOUNTS `createFormRuntime()`** — the guest SPA's own store — so client-side parity is ONE implementation, not two that agree. Do not "decouple" it into a shared `projectSteps()` helper plus a local cursor: that was weighed and rejected, because Encode.vue would then re-implement the watcher, the rescue and `goToStep` — a second copy of everything H21b just built and pinned. The store was importable as it stood (`vue`, `../engine`, `../lib/schema-mapping`, `../lib/prefill`, `../lib/uuid`, `../lib/types`; no api-client, no Dexie, no DOM), and both tenant-app hazards were already handled — `randomUuid()`/`uuidv7()` are secure-context-safe and `buildPrefill(schema, '')` is a no-op for `url` sources, which is the correct reading of H7 here because **a keyer is the SOURCE of such a value, not a recipient**. **THE "REFERENCE FIELDS" BLOCK SITS OUTSIDE THE STEP LIST BY DECISION.** A section holding nothing but a `url`-hidden gate can never be a step (`RENDERS_NOTHING` drops `hidden`, and predicate 2 is a static type test), so those rows render in a labelled keyer-only block; a hidden field ALONGSIDE ordinary questions still renders inline where its author put it. The alternative — an audience axis on `StepProjection` and the TS store — was rejected because byte-identical step lists are what make §9's parity assertion a clean equality instead of an equality-modulo-hidden-only-sections. **THE PARITY SUITE ASSERTS THE INPUT** (amendment C4): with both channels on one function, running two projections and comparing them is a tautology in a cross-channel costume, so `EncodeStepParityTest` asserts that `version.schema` is byte-identical to `PublicFormPresenter`'s and that the taken path rebuilt FROM THE SHIPPED SNAPSHOT reproduces each fixture case. Do not "strengthen" it into a both-sides comparison. **`blocks` STAYS**, and its PHP↔TS normalizer duplication is deferred by name in Doc #27 §10 with a specific revisit trigger — the first time the two are found to disagree about a control's config. **THE CLOCK IS SERVER-STAMPED on this channel** (`version.now` → `RuntimeOptions.now`), unlike the guest's `isoClock(new Date())`, so the server-rendered `steps` and the client's first `visibleSteps` cannot disagree across midnight or a device skew. **NEXT NEVER BLOCKS** — `attemptNext()` is the guest's gate and would be a new refusal on a channel whose F4b contract is "the pipeline is the sole authority"; that is *why* Submit raises a FORM-WIDE summary whose jump links change step before they focus, and why the POST body is the full answer map rather than `effectiveAnswers` (which is what keeps the prune report meaningful as a divergence alarm). `preserveState` is a predicate, not a constant: a 422 must keep what was typed, a success must not. **`SubmissionPdfPresenter` IS NOT REFACTORED ONTO `StepProjection`** despite that class's docblock saying H21c "collapses them" — the PDF's "No entries." notice and em-dash blanks are distinctions the step model cannot express, so the honest collapse is the cross-check §9 asks for in words. **`min_instances` starter rows are still seeded here**, unlike the guest, which opens a repeat group empty so an empty guest load stays axe-clean. **New standing lesson — RUN A REGRESSION TEST AGAINST THE UNFIXED CODE BEFORE BELIEVING IT.** The first test written for the repeat-mask defect passed against the broken version: with one surviving row and one pruned row, the two implementations name different rows and render the SAME string. A test written to prove a fix is exactly as vulnerable to the more-than-one-path trap as a test written to specify a feature, and the check costs one `git show HEAD:<file> >` and one run. **Second standing lesson — a green mutation can indict an ACCIDENT rather than a test.** `EncodeFormPresenter::OMITTED` ⊂ `PdfFieldRole::Omitted` held by coincidence of two lists answering different questions, enforced nowhere; the mutation that exploited it reddened nothing, and the fix was to pin the relation rather than to add a test for the behaviour. Also: an assertion that reads a model relation by the wrong name yields `[]`, not an error, and `[]` makes every relevance gate read ABSENT — assert the fixture is non-empty before comparing anything derived from it.

**H21b (do not re-open):** the scope was the **whole of Doc #27 §9's H21b obligation**, not just the five live defects — §4.2, §5.2, §5.3 and §4.4's step notice ship here, so **H21c inherits nothing from H1f**. **The empty graph is a TERMINAL STATE**: the progress nav is suppressed entirely (never "Step 1 of 0"), `isLastStep` is `length > 0 && index === length - 1`, and a separate **`isTerminal`** computed carries the view — do not "simplify" it away, because with the guard in place `isLastStep` is correctly false at zero steps and the template would render Next instead of the single labelled Submit. **ONE `watch(visibleSteps, (next, prev))` replaces the membership-boolean watcher** and does seed-or-self-heal · rescue-by-identity · visited set · `StepChange`; the old boolean source was the bug (false at t=0, still false when steps appear, so it never fired). **§4.2's walk goes OUTWARD — backwards THEN forwards — and both arms are load-bearing**; the forward arm was pinned by nothing until mutation testing caught it (amendment **B2**, which corrects the doc's own wording). `nearestSurvivor()` returns **null** on failure ON PURPOSE so each caller keeps its own fallback — the rescue watcher takes the first step, `goToStep()` falls through to §5.3's first-incomplete — and folding either in makes the other unreachable. **"Done" comes from the store's VISITED SET**, never `index < currentIndex` (a step can become relevant behind the respondent). **§5.5: Submit reports FORM-WIDE errors, a blocked Next stays STEP-scoped**, and `SummaryBanner` got a `beforeJump` prop because a jump link cannot resolve a field on an unmounted step. **The replay guard reads `client.fetchSchema()` after `remint()`, memoized PER PASS**, sits BEFORE the media leg (the attachment controller carries the identical check against the same fresh token), and leaves `row.checksum` unread — the server compares the version id and matching it exactly is the point. **Resumed-draft rows park by decision, not oversight.** **The respondent-facing "% complete" is GONE** and its absence is asserted; coverage stays on tenant surfaces. **`__lead__` is in `goToStep`'s walk order only when the version still has section-less fields** — that is what degrades §5.3's case (c) into case (a). **New standing lesson — a mutation that reddens NOTHING is the loudest result you can get.** It does not mean the code is redundant; it means no test reaches that path, and here it exposed a gap in the *design doc*, not just the suite. Probe every arm of a branch separately, including the one the doc only mentions in passing.

**H21a (do not re-open):** the **mirror pair is a shared FIXTURE** (`tests/fixtures/step-projection.json`, 16 cases, outside `tests/golden/` so the 296/114 counts do not move), driven through both `App\Support\Forms\StepProjection` and the store's `visibleSteps`. Doc #27 §9's prescribed source-parsing device was **rejected as vacuous** — it works for `PdfFieldRole` because it parses DATA, while a projection's rule is control flow, so parsing it pins predicate NAMES that either side could implement with inverted polarity. Do not "restore" it. **`min_instances` is narrowed in BOTH engines** — `processRepeats()` has a byte-for-byte TS twin the golden runners reach, so a PHP-only fix is an R3 break no vector can see; that is why `RENDERS_NOTHING` now lives in `engine/field-roles.ts` (lib re-exports it, `PdfFieldRoleTest` parses the new home). **`max_instances` deliberately keeps its position ahead of the per-instance settle** — it is the abuse guard — and takes no narrowing. **`count(${section})` is seeded at BOTH scopes**, into the intersect ARGUMENT and never into `$relevant` (which would make the fixed point unreachable), repeatable sections only, with a **collision guard**: section and field keys are NOT globally unique (independent per-table indexes), so seeding blindly re-admits a pruned field's answer. **Predicate 3 splits by mask** — flat fields and the `__lead__` block read `fieldRelevance`, repeat sections read `repeatFieldRelevance` because a member is ABSENT from the flat mask, and **zero instances is vacuously visible**. `step.fieldKeys` stays unfiltered. **The clock is UTC in Carbon's exact `2026-08-01T12:34:56+00:00` shape**, frozen at session construction — the device offset would silently prune answers server-side, and `toISOString()`'s millis/`Z` would be a new mirror divergence. **A1 correction: `${d} >= today()` was always-false in both engines already** (ordering is numeric-only), so date-RANGE branching still does not exist; the fix restores `= today()`, `count()`, `if()`, `int()`. **The warnings are computed in `FormPublishController` after `publish()` returns**, never inside `PublishService` — that keeps the service binary and `openapi.json` drift-free — and they are **warnings, never refusals** (the retro-gate argument). **New standing lesson — a mutation that comes back green may be indicting the FIXTURE, not just the assertion.** Dropping the post-loop mask tightening was green for two compounding reasons: the assertion was an `if/else` invariant satisfied by both behaviours, AND the settle bound `fields + sections + 2` means the field COUNT decides which phase of an oscillation the loop halts on — at 3 and 5 fields the fix is a genuine no-op, at 4 it is not. Probe the mechanism empirically before trusting either the test or the mutation.

**H1f (do not re-open):** the doc is **`docs/workflow-branching-design.md` = Doc #27** (not an ADR; **0010 stays reserved for H1d**). **The step graph is a TOTAL ORDER FILTERED BY RELEVANCE** — no edge table, no successor column, no `next_step_id`; a "branch" is a predicate on a node, so the H21d canvas draws **a rail with conditions, not a flow chart**, and "jump to section 7" is N predicates, a cost recorded in §10 rather than solved with a "skip to" sugar that would be an edge in disguise. **Step visibility gains a THIRD predicate** — section relevant AND ≥1 field that renders something AND ≥1 field *currently relevant*; the third is net-new because the as-built emptiness test is a **static type predicate** (`rendersNothing`) that never consults `fieldRelevance`. Do not "simplify" it back: without it a field-gated section renders a heading over nothing, which is the grayed-out step the UX spec rejects. **Forward references and cycles are WARNED, NEVER REFUSED**, and the argument is the **retro-gate shape**, not tolerance: `PublishService` step 9 clones forward, so a new refusal locks an author out of editing a form that is **already live**, with an error naming a rule that postdates their form — and there is no grandfather seam for publish gates. The non-throwing twin to copy is `TemplateValidationGate::confirmationMessageViolations()` (Doc #26 A3). A cycle detector must also discriminate a cycle from a legitimate **chain** (`chained_fixed_point` pins one as correct) or it warns on correct forms. **The empty graph is a SPECIFIED TERMINAL STATE**, not an error: suppress the counter (never "Step 1 of 0"), explicit terminal panel, and `isLastStep` becomes `length > 0 && index === length - 1` instead of the `-1 === -1` accident. **Two progress numbers, deliberately**: the step counter is *position on the taken path*; `completeness_percent` is *coverage of the authored form* and stays **relevance-UNAWARE** (the denominator is pinned in `data-dictionary.md`) — do not "fix" the disagreement by making it relevance-aware, which would run the full validator on every autosave and make the inbox column non-comparable between rows. The respondent-facing "% done" is dropped instead. **No new golden vectors, and the step projection is a FIFTH R3 MIRROR PAIR, not a fifth corpus** — pinned by a test that PARSES `useFormRuntime.ts`, the H17 `PdfFieldRole` device, because a hand-copied predicate list keeps passing after someone edits the `.ts`. **Two H-map corrections are load-bearing for H21a's plan:** the "snapshot freeze" line item is a **no-op** (the snapshot already IS the step model) so **H21a is M and H21c is the L**, and **no AST→expression-string printer exists**, so H21d2 must ship a serializer and H21d1 stays read-derived. **The nine recorded defects are H21's work, not H1f's** — H1f fixed none, by construction.

**H17 (do not re-open):** the taken path is **re-derived, never stored** — relevance is persisted nowhere, and `effectiveAnswers()`'s pruning makes "key absent" ambiguous with "answered blank", so `SemanticValidator::validate()` gained a trailing **`?string $now`** (null keeps every pre-H17 caller byte-identical) and the PDF passes the submission's own `finalized_at`. Do not "simplify" that to `Carbon::now()`: a `today()`-dependent `relevant_expression` would then be judged against the day someone clicked Generate. The replay over an already-pruned document is a **fixed point**, pinned by an idempotence test rather than argued. **`PdfFieldRole` is the PHP twin of the TS `RENDERS_NOTHING`** — a `default`-less 31-case match (27 answer / 1 prose / 3 omitted) whose parity test **parses `schema-mapping.ts`**, because a hand-copied list keeps passing after someone edits the `.ts`. It is NOT a reuse of `PipingEligibility`: the two disagree on exactly `hidden` and `calculated`, and that disagreement is correct in both directions — a `calculated` total still reaches the PDF through any label that pipes it. **Supersede is overwrite-in-place on a deterministic key**, and the absence of a delete is the design, not an omission: nothing in this codebase reclaims storage, `TenantAwareJob` owns the transaction and exposes no post-commit hook, and `DB::afterCommit` is verified never to fire under the suite's outer transaction — so every delete ordering leaves a rollback window with a dangling row that charges quota and 404s. **Quota is charged on the DELTA** (the replaced row already counts) and a refusal is **caught and emailed, never rethrown** — `$maxExceptions = 3` would re-render three times for a limit that cannot change, and with no bell, no polling and `BROADCAST_CONNECTION=log` a dead-lettered job tells the user nothing. **The scan gate is synchronous and pre-write**, and must not take `WebhookPayloadArchive`'s hardcoded `Skipped` — that shortcut is justified by "never served to a browser" and a PDF is. **`$timeout` is 110, not 60**, and 110 is the ceiling: the base class's invariant requires it below `retry_after` = 120.

**New standing lesson — commit before mutation testing.** Four mutations were applied with `sed` and reverted with `git checkout --`, which silently failed on files that were still UNTRACKED, so all four stacked and had to be unpicked by hand. Commit a restore point first; and strip ANSI codes before grepping Pest output for `⨯`, because the escape sequence sits between the leading spaces and the symbol, so `^\s+⨯` matches nothing and every mutation looks green.

**Second lesson — `->not->toContain($needle, $message)` is not an assertion with a message.** Pest's `toContain` takes VARARGS NEEDLES, so the "message" becomes a second needle and the negated form passes whenever EITHER is absent. A guard written that way is green by construction; the mutation pass is what caught it. The same shape sank the delta test, where `assertSentTimes(…, 2)` counted a failure notification as happily as a success one — **when a mutation comes back green, suspect the test before the mechanism.**

**Third lesson — a redundant-looking guard can still be load-bearing on one path.** Removing the section-relevance skip reddened nothing, because for a NON-repeatable section the per-row filter had already emptied the block. Only a *repeatable* section takes the path where it matters. The mutation was right and the test suite was incomplete.

**Two pre-existing defects recorded, not fixed:** `domains.domain` stores the SUBDOMAIN LABEL, not an FQDN, so `TenantMembershipService::buildInviteAcceptUrl()` and `GuestDraftController::resumeUrl()` both emit `https://acme/…` — a URL that resolves nowhere — and both hard-code `https://`, wrong in local dev. `App\Support\Tenancy\TenantUrl` composes label + host + port + scheme from `app.url` and inherits neither; retrofitting those two call sites is a small, self-contained follow-up. Also still unfixed and now recorded in the narrowings: `displayValue()` renders a media answer as raw JSON blobs (`{"id":"018f…","mime":"image/jpeg"}`) on the inbox, the CSV/XLSX export and the PDF alike, pinned by no test.

**H7 (do not re-open):** the source model is `App\Enums\PrefillSource` — **`Fixed`** (server-authoritative, written from `default_value`, submitted value never inspected on any channel) / **`Url`** (the only client-writable one; scalar-only, capped at 2000 bytes) / **`None`**. It is **NOT** a `default`-less match over the 31 `FieldType` cases and must not be described as one: it classifies a hidden field's *config*, and a 32nd field type is correctly `None` without anyone deciding it — claiming the H8 forcing device where the dimension does not apply would be cargo-culting it. **The expression source was DELIBERATELY DROPPED** (`FieldType::Calculated` already is one; widening both compute passes to `hidden` opens a new R3 mirror surface for no new capability) — the H-map row 227 is amended, not unimplemented. **The prefill pass MUST stay inside `StructuralAnswerNormalizer::normalize()`** — both write paths call it then immediately hash with `AnswersContentChecksum`, so moving it to a caller either strands drafts or makes a replay that omits the injected value 409 against one that includes it. It needs a second pass over the FIELDS because the main loop walks the client's keys. **`hidden` never produces a respondent-facing error** — enforced twice, and both halves earn their keep: the two-engine Stage-3 narrowing (what makes dropping hidden fields from `visibleSteps` *safe*, not merely tidy) and the four publish refusals (which are **not retroactive**, hence the narrowing). **`RENDERS_NOTHING` (`hidden`/`calculated`/`page_break`) deliberately does NOT mirror `EncodeFormPresenter::OMITTED`** — H7 makes the channels asymmetric on purpose. **Seeding order:** prefill → restore, so a restored draft always wins; the store stays DOM-free (`App.vue` reads `location.search` once). **The over-cap asymmetry is intentional** (client drops, server 422s) and cannot desync, because the SPA never submits an over-cap value. **Known narrowing:** an installed PWA launches at the manifest `start_url` (`/f/{slug}`, no query), so a prefilled link opened from the home-screen icon prefills nothing. **New standing lesson — check whether two mechanisms are independently pinned or merely redundant.** `coerceHidden`'s source guard and the injection pass both make "a fixed value cannot be steered" true, so a single test passes under either mutation alone; separate mutations proved they redden *disjoint* test sets, and the checksum-stability test was caught **passing vacuously** (neither document carried the key when injection was removed) and now asserts presence as well as equality. **Second lesson:** `form_field_validations` carries the draft-child RLS shape too — an insert without `form_version_id` violates the row-level policy, it does not merely look untidy. **Third lesson, and the one that cost this PR a red cycle — a "flaky" gate can be catching a real race.** `builder-axe` failed on `scrollable-region-focusable` over `.palette`; a re-run reproduced it, and checking `main` showed **the same two tests failing at `e04e68b`** (the H6a merge) with the same rule. It was never H7's: `FieldPalette` is `overflow-y: auto` over 31 add-buttons and satisfies the rule *through* them, but `:disabled="saving"` makes every one non-focusable during a debounced save, leaving a scroll region with no keyboard access at all (WCAG 2.1.1). Fixed with `DataTable.vue`'s **measured** pattern (a palette that fits adds no tab stop; `role="group"` not `region`), pinned by three tests including the all-buttons-disabled case. **Before re-running a red a11y gate, check whether the same test has failed on `main`** — "intermittent" and "wrong" are not exclusive.

**H6b (do not re-open):** the increment was reclassified from the H-map's `ui, M` to **full-stack, M/L** by four decisions locked with the user, each recorded as a Doc #26 amendment. **A7 — the scalar→string rule is PINNED in BOTH engines.** `displayValue()` ended in a bare `(string)` cast that JavaScript **cannot** reproduce, and both divergent branches are reachable: `ExpressionEvaluator::normalize()` passes a native bool through, so a `calculated` field holding `${age} >= 18` stores one (PHP `'1'`/`''` vs JS `'true'`/`'false'`), and any `calculated` division holds a non-integral float (PHP `'0.3'` under `precision=14` vs JS `'0.30000000000000004'`). **H6a's only float vector was `3.5` — exactly representable in binary, so it concealed the whole problem while appearing to pin it.** The new rule: bool → `'1'`/`''`; non-finite → `''`; integral float → no fraction; else fixed notation to 10 decimals, trailing zeros trimmed (`sprintf('%.10F')` / `toFixed(10)`). It avoids the `precision` ini entirely (runtime-configurable, so the old cast was not stable across deployments) and avoids E-notation on both sides; verified equal across 17 probed values in both containers. **One accepted divergence with a precise boundary: |v| >= 1e21**, where JS `toFixed` falls back to exponential — hence the `< 1e21` guard, which documents where the mirror stops. **A second is unfixable and must never be vectored:** a `yes_no` of JSON `1.0` is `No` in PHP (`1.0 === 1` is false) and `Yes` in JS. **A8 — `displayValue()`/`options()`/`TemplateRenderer::render()` gained `?string $locale`**: §4's order bound the sentence but not the VALUE dropped into it, so a Filipino respondent read an English choice label mid-Filipino-sentence AND the inbox disagreed with the respondent's own screen. Null keeps every pre-H6b caller byte-identical, which is what made it safe on two shipped read surfaces. **Two fallbacks at two levels, deliberately different:** the locale variant falls back on missing/non-string/**blank** (`resolveText()`'s rule), the base label on **null only** (an explicit `"label": ""` still wins). The exporter passes the EXPORT's locale, not the submission's — one spreadsheet, one language. **A9 — the inbox is repeat-aware**, and closing it meant fixing a bug that was not about piping: `SubmissionInboxPresenter` was repeat-**blind**, emitting one row per member field and reading `$answers[$field->key]` while a repeat member's answers live nested under the SECTION key, so **an answered roster displayed as a column of em-dashes**. A repeatable section now emits one BLOCK PER INSTANCE against `array_merge($base, $instance)`, numbered as `RepeatGroup.vue` numbers it; an empty repeat shows its heading with no rows ("No entries."), because it was not answered blank, it was not answered. SPA-only was rejected: respondent and reviewer would read different question text for the same submission. **A10 — A3's residue closes as a WARNING, not a 422**: the publish gate resolves against the version being PUBLISHED while a request can only resolve against the CURRENTLY PUBLISHED one, so a hard reject would refuse an author who adds the field to their draft and then writes the message. The controller replaces its success toast with an `info` one naming the key and why; a form with no published version says nothing. **The SPA seam lives in the STORE, not the components** — `labelFor`/`hintFor`/`placeholderFor`/`sectionTitleFor`/`sectionDescriptionFor`, all resolve-then-render. Composing per-component would have left a raw `${key}` in the aria-live announcer and the error-summary banner, invisible to sighted QA. **The render document is `{ ...effectiveAnswers, ...computed }`** mirroring `SubmissionPipeline.php:147` — `SemanticResult.computed` is a SEPARATE map, so reading `effectiveAnswers` alone (the natural choice) renders every `calculated` hole blank in the SPA and correct in the inbox, killing the case §3.1 cites as the *reason* `calculated` is pipeable. **A hole-FREE label takes no dependency on the answer document at all** (`includes('${')` guard, pinned by a getter-evaluation-count test), so every pre-H6b form keeps its exact reactive graph. **The confirmation renders inside `RuntimeSession` and is emitted as a finished string** — `App.vue` renders `ConfirmationScreen` and `RuntimeSession` as mutually exclusive branches, so the store does not outlive the emit; it replaces CONFIRM and RESOLVED but **not QUEUED**, which reports delivery state rather than gratitude. A blank render collapses to null (an empty focused `<h1>` is an axe `empty-heading`). **`placeholder` was passed through RAW** before this — the one live §3.4 violation in the runtime. **Corpus 53 → 76, `engines` gone from the tree**; the TS runner had **no render branch at all**, so deleting the key alone would have failed all 13 on a shape mismatch rather than running them, and it was missing the fourth guard (a guard on one side only is itself runner drift). **New standing lesson — a vector reachable by two paths pins neither:** `fmt_hole_named_constructor_renders_empty` was green against a prototype-UNSAFE mirror because with no answer the empty guard short-circuited before the unsafe lookup mattered; found by mutation, fixed by giving it an answer. **The G11 token guard earned its keep a second time:** `--mds-color-text-muted` does not exist and is literally one of the 16 names G11 removed — grepping the source found REFERENCES, not definitions. **The registry is `packages/design-system/tokens/*.json`.**

**H1h (do not re-open):** the doc is **`docs/piping-output-encoding-design.md` = Doc #26** (next free number; the `docs/PRD.md:13` index topped out at #25). Four locked decisions: **(1) SIBLING template grammar** — `TEMPLATE_VERSION = '1.0'` beside a frozen expression `GRAMMAR_VERSION = '2.0'`, its own `tests/golden/templates/` + manifest + PHP⇄TS runner. A template yields **text** and participates in no evaluation, so it is a different language that happens to share one lexeme. Rejected bumping to v2.1 because both runners assert `grammar_version` **per vector** and the string sits at **288 verified sites** in `tests/golden/**` (286 vectors + 2 manifests) — a bump rewrites all of them in files whose meaning did not change, and falsifies the corpus's own "grammar stays v2.0" marker. **(2) DOCUMENTED per-surface rule + one escaping test per surface**, not a `__toString()`-less value object; the convention-not-compiler weakness is recorded as `security-threat-model.md` §9 **item 9** (connector custody stays item 8) and the escalation is named: adopt the value object if a *second* surface is ever found unescaped. **(3) The contract binds EVERY untrusted value** on a server-rendered surface, not only piped ones — an escaper cannot tell where a string came from, and H6a escaping the answer while leaving the form title raw beside it in the same Slack message is the failure mode avoided. **(4) Confirmation piping PINNED**, with net-new `forms.confirmation_message` + `_translations` assigned to **H6a** (today's copy is three hardcoded constants at `public-runtime/App.vue:29-31`; no such column exists in any migration (there were 64 at the time; H6a added the 65th)). **Grammar:** `${key}` reused verbatim; **`$${` is the ONLY escape** and yields a literal `${`; a bare `$` is literal everywhere else (the expression lexer throws on it — in prose a lone `$` is a currency sign); total under leftmost-longest scanning, `$$${` → `$` + `${`, no escape-of-escape regress. Malformed hole = publish error, never silently literal. **The piping-source allowlist is a `default`-less exhaustive `match` over all 31 `FieldType` cases** (the H8 device) and is **deliberately NOT a reuse of `OcrFieldEligibility`** — `calculated` and `hidden` are **pipeable** where OCR excludes/neutralises them (19 pipeable / 2 no-answer / 10 excluded). **THE REAL R3 HAZARD IS NOT THE PARSER** — it is `SchemaValueFormatter::displayValue()`, which has **no TS twin**; H6b must build one and vector-pin its choice-label/`yes_no`/multi-select/decimal rendering. `Coercion::toStr()` is explicitly **barred** as the primitive (non-integral float→string parity is documented as unpinned). **Scope rules:** backward-reference-only over `(section_sequence, sequence)`; same repeat instance; no cross-repeat, no repeat-to-flat; **a section key is never a hole** (legal `count()` operand, illegal hole) — which is precisely why H6a **cannot** just call `assertReferencesResolve()` unchanged: its `$knownKeys` is a flat field ∪ section union with **no scope information** (`ExpressionValidationGate.php:47-61`). Unresolved at render = **empty string**, never the raw token; publish refuses an unresolvable *reference*, render tolerates an unanswered *value*, and render must never throw. **Multi-locale:** every variant independently a template; **parity across locales NOT required**; **order is normative — resolve the locale, THEN render**. `*_translations` has **zero** validation rules anywhere today (the builder UI can't even author them) — H6a owes them one. **THREE LIVE DEFECTS the audit found for non-piped strings, now named + assigned:** Slack `mrkdwn` is unescaped (raw form title → **H6a**), `Markdown::withSecuredEncoding()` is off so `[`/`!`/`*` are live markdown in every notification email (→ **H3/H23**), and there is no CSV/XLSX formula-injection prefixing (→ **its own row**). **CSP is no defence and the doc says so** — no `default-src`/`script-src`, 4 routes; output encoding is the sole control. **One agent claim REFUTED before it was written down:** Inertia's `{!! json_encode($page) !!}` is **not** a `</script>` breakout sink — `json_encode` escapes forward slashes by default (proven in-container: it emits `<\/script>`), so it is recorded as a **dependency to preserve**, not a defect. Corrected in passing: `technical-architecture.md` §4.3's drifted vector count ("55 validation" → **106**) and `PRD.md:13`'s stale ADR list (stopped at 0007).

**H6a (do not re-open):** the template code lives in a NEW `App\Services\Templates` + `App\Exceptions\Templates`, not beside the expression engine — Doc #26 §2's "a template is not a mode of the expression grammar" is a locked decision and filesystem adjacency would erode it. **`TemplateSyntaxException` deliberately does NOT extend `ExpressionException`**: `bootstrap/app.php` renders the two expression *leaf* types by name, so a third leaf under that base matches NO callback and produces a raw 500 with no API envelope — inheritance would have been a trap dressed as reuse. No render callback is registered at all, because `parse()` (the only throwing method) has exactly one production caller, the gate, whose catch is total; every render path uses `parseLenient()`, which cannot throw. **`PipingEligibility` is a `default`-less `match` over all 31 `FieldType` cases** (19/2/10) in `app/Enums/` — it MUST live under `app/` or the forcing device evaporates, since `phpstan.neon` scans only `app|database|routes` and there is no baseline file. It is NOT a reuse of `OcrFieldEligibility`: `calculated` and `hidden` are pipeable where OCR excludes/neutralises them, so piping deliberately WIDENS — and H8's monotonicity invariant is therefore *not* inherited (a test pins the widening so nobody "restores" it). **The scope resolver does not call `assertReferencesResolve()`** — its `$knownKeys` is a flat field ∪ section union with no scope information, so it would accept `${roster}` in a label and accept a cross-repeat reference. **Three PHP surfaces interpolate**: `SubmissionInboxPresenter` (the ONLY one where a hole actually fills — it alone holds label + catalog + answers), and `EncodeFormPresenter` + `SubmissionExporter::header()` with an EMPTY answer map, because §3.4 says a hole is never the raw token even where it is structurally unfillable. Export **cells** are untouched (the spreadsheet-injection row is its own increment). **Confirmation validation is split by decidability**: grammar at request time (`ValidTemplate`), references at publish — the column is form-level and editable on a form with no published version at all. **FIVE Doc #26 amendments, A1–A5, recorded inline beside the clauses they correct.** The two that matter downstream: **A2** — §3.3's `(section_sequence, sequence)` ordering tuple is NOT implementable (`form_fields.section_sequence` is a nullable *order-within-section* column, null on nearly every row; a section's own order is `form_sections.sequence`), so the real tuple is *(owning section's `sequence`, field's `sequence`)* compared strictly, **and a positional TIE is a rejection** — `addFormField()` defaults `sequence` to 0, so every gate test must pass distinct sequences or it fails for the wrong reason; and **A5** — §6's "runs before the freeze so nothing rejected reaches `schema_snapshot`" credits the ordering for what the **transaction** guarantees. Proven by mutation: moving the gate below the freeze leaves every gate test green, and no honest test can pin the ordering (it has no observable effect inside the transaction, and `SchemaSnapshotSerializer` is `final` with `PublishService` type-hinting it, so it cannot be spied on). **Golden corpus = 53 vectors, the third in `tests/golden/`**; the 13 formatting vectors carry `"engines": ["php"]` and the TS runner counts but skips them until H6b builds the `displayValue()` twin. Both existing corpora are byte-untouched (`grammar_version` still at exactly 288 sites — verify this at the end of any increment that touches `tests/golden/`). **Pest one-process collision is real**: `goldenDir`/`buildExpression`/`loadVectorFiles` were already taken, so the new runner uses `templateGoldenDir`/`buildTemplate`/`loadTemplateVectorFiles`. **New standing lesson — G11's `token-references.test.ts` earns its keep:** it caught three `--mds-*` tokens invented out of thin air in a new modal (`--mds-color-surface-sunken`, `--mds-type-font-mono`, `--mds-type-body-sm-size` — the real names are `--mds-color-bg-sunken` and `--mds-font-family-mono`, and there is no `body-sm-size`). **Grep the token registry before writing a `var(--mds-…)`, and do not assume a plausible name exists.** Second lesson: `form_fields` carries the **draft-child RLS shape**, so an UPDATE against a *published* version's field rows affects zero rows and silently defeats a test — author on the draft, then publish.

**H8 (do not re-open):** the rule is an **ALLOWLIST** — `App\Enums\OcrFieldEligibility::for()`, a **`default`-less exhaustive `match`** over all 31 `FieldType` cases (the `XlsformTypeMap::toXlsform()` device), because every *other* `FieldType` predicate has a `default` arm and a rule composed from them absorbs a new type as eligible silently. Three verdicts: **extractable (17)** / **neutral (3: note, page_break, hidden)** / **excluded (11)**. Four locked decisions: **(1) charter-exact membership** — §2's literal 12-item list is an under-enumeration (it would disqualify a form for one `email` field or one `page_break`), so §2 was **amended**, not implemented, and the net behaviour change is exactly `matrix` + `likert_matrix` + repeat groups + nothing-extractable; **(2) the backfill ships now** even though production does not exist yet; **(3) the primitive is SNAPSHOT-DRIVEN** — `CapabilityFlags::forSnapshot(array)`, static-pure, fed by the snapshot `PublishService` already froze at step 3 (it used to re-read live rows at step 8 to answer a question step 3 had already frozen); **(4) non-vacuity is IN** (a version with no extractable field is ineligible). **The monotonicity invariant is load-bearing, not decoration:** H8's permitted set is disjoint from the pre-H8 denylist (geo ∪ media ∪ `calculated`), so `new ⇒ old` — which is what licenses the backfill to examine only stored `true`s (lossless, not an optimization) and write a constant `false`. **A future increment that WIDENS the allowlist breaks it and needs its own backfill.** The backfill **restates nothing in SQL** (a test asserts the SQL text mentions no field type and no `is_repeatable`) — it read-computes-writes through the same `forSnapshot()`, with `jsonb_set` on the single key so a future flag survives, and **`updated_at` deliberately untouched** (it is an optimistic-concurrency token). New guard the two precedent backfills lack: **`assertPrivileged()` asks `pg_roles` whether `current_user` bypasses RLS** — `select 1` proves only that the connection OPENS, and a NOBYPASSRLS role would read zero rows, flip nothing and pass its own postcondition (**vacuous green**). **H18a/H19 must NOT gate on `forms.capability_flags`** — it is a display cache for the current published version; derive per-version via `CapabilityFlags::isOcrCompatible($version)`. `form_versions.capability_flags` was **rejected**, and §2's third gap bullet is **corrected**: an older version's eligibility IS recoverable, from its own frozen `schema_snapshot`.

**H15b (do not re-open):** page shape = **list + per-RULE detail**; the rule routes are **flat** (`/integrations/rules/{connectionSubscription}`) because `disconnect()` soft-deletes the grant but keeps its rules, so a nested `{connection}` binding 404s exactly when a tenant is cleaning up — which is why a **`ConnectionSubscriptionPolicy` now exists** even though H15a needed none. The OAuth return is **PRG** (query param → session flash → clean URL), and `connect_error` is **untrusted input used only as a lookup key**, never rendered. Channel listing is an **optional `ListsChannels` capability** resolved through the registry, not a fifth `ConnectorProvider` method. **No server-side cache anywhere** — the repo still has zero `Cache::` usage in `app/`, and a picker was the wrong place to make it one. The sidecar **always answers 200** with `error` in the body. The test send goes through the adapter's own `deliver()` and reuses H14's `testResult` flash + `TestResultModal` verbatim. Log column is the **Result excerpt, not the HTTP status** (Slack fails at HTTP 200). **New standing lesson (the THIRD instance of this shape, after H14`s `.page-header__actions`):** any `overflow` or non-wrapping flex container in a SHARED component is a latent responsive/axe gate failure waiting for the first page whose content is big enough — H15b fixed `TestResultModal`s `<pre>` pre-emptively and still had `.mds-table__scroll` (MdsDataTable`s own horizontal scroller) fail `scrollable-region-focusable` at tablet on the first CI run. **Audit the shared primitives a new page leans on, not just the page.** The DataTable now measures its own overflow and becomes a named focusable `group` only when it actually scrolls. **Also:** `Http::preventStrayRequests()` in a `beforeEach` turns **every Inertia page render into a 500** — the SSR gateway POSTs to the render server while building the root view. Use it only in suites that never render a page (the channels sidecar suite does); elsewhere assert with `Http::fake()` + `assertNothingSent()`. **Second lesson:** never run two Pest processes against `meridian_testing` at once — `RefreshDatabase`'s `migrate:fresh` drops the schema under the other one, and the failures look like unrelated "relation does not exist" errors in `beforeEach`.

**H1g + H15a (do not re-open):** ADR-**0009** (0009 was the cancelled payments number, never written — the Status block says so). Four locked forks: **`webhook_deliveries` EXTENDED** into the shared outbound ledger (nullable owner FKs + an XOR CHECK + two PARTIAL uniques), not a second table; **fixed Block Kit message** (templating waits for H6a — `SlackMessageFormatter` is the seam); **hand-rolled `ConnectorProvider` adapter**, no Socialite; **ADR-0009**. The load-bearing constraint: **`SESSION_DOMAIN` is null ⇒ host-only cookie**, so the central-domain callback has neither session nor tenant (and a context-less INSERT under FORCE RLS writes **zero rows without erroring**) — identity crosses the boundary in a **signed, domain-separated, provider-bound HMAC `state`** verified **pre-RLS**, the return host comes from the **`domains` table** (never echoed), and the outcome is a **query param, not a flash**. Tokens are `encrypted`-cast **`text`**, `$hidden`, **never returned in any form, not even masked**. **Two Slack gotchas for H15b/H16:** it reports failure with **HTTP 200 + `{"ok":false}`**, and its bot tokens **don't expire** by default (so the refresh sweep skips them). Full detail in the H15a + H1g (as-built) bullets under Current Status + ADR-0009 §D1–§D10.

**H14 (do not re-open):** the webhook mgmt UI is a **session-authed web layer** (`Tenant\WebhookController` + `WebhookEndpointPresenter` + `routes/tenant.php`) delegating to the **existing** `WebhookEndpointService`/`WebhookTester` — NOT the bearer `/api/v1/webhooks` surface (a session carries no token) → no `/api/v1`/migration/OpenAPI change. One-shot plaintext secret (create/rotate) + the synchronous test.ping result both ride the shared `flash` prop bridge (`newSecret`/`testResult`), read once by their modals. Sub-Starter tiers HIDE the surface (nav gated on `manageWebhooks` AND `entitlements.features.webhooks`; the route's `feature:webhooks` bounces a direct visit with a toast). Delivery log is OFFSET-paginated (MdsPagination needs page counts). **Gates already verified locally VIA THE DOCKER CONTAINERS** (`docker exec …-app-1 … vendor/bin/pest` for Pest, `…-node-1 npx vitest run` / `npm run build` for frontend — the host CLI lacks `pdo_pgsql` + the `rolldown` win32 binding): Pint, PHPStan L8 (0 on touched), controller-gate/migration-lint/job-payload-lint, vue-tsc (both), **Pest 19/19 (webhook files), Vitest 489/490 (1 pre-existing timeout flake), Vite build** — all green. **Storybook axe + Playwright responsive-axe ran for the first time in CI and were GREEN — 6/6 on the first run, no red cycle** (PR #69 `c5be98f`). **New standing lesson (the H12b toolbar-overflow lesson generalized):** the 375px no-horizontal-overflow gate is tripped by ANY non-wrapping flex row of actions, not just the builder toolbar — the shell's shared `.page-header__actions` had `display:flex` with no `flex-wrap`, and the H14 detail page puts FIVE buttons in that slot. Fixed pre-emptively (`flex-wrap: wrap` in `PageHeader.vue`, now shared by every page) together with the list's URL cell (`max-width: min(22rem, 100%)` — a fixed `22rem` = 352px exceeds the ~343px a 375px viewport gives the DataTable's card-mode cell). **Audit any new multi-button action slot / fixed `max-width` against the 375px budget BEFORE pushing.**

**H13a+H13b design (do not re-open):** monthly `webhook_deliveries` quota = HARD cap (over → `dead_lettered` `[quota_exceeded]`, not sent); subscribable events = all four `DomainEventType` cases (REUSED, no `WebhookEventType`); `secret`/`secret_previous` are `text` (encrypted-cast overflows varchar 255); retry ladder lives in `next_retry_at` swept by `SweepWebhookRetriesJob` (NOT `$tries`/`backoff()`); SSRF is one `OutboundUrlGuard` at BOTH save + every delivery; endpoint cap = gauge `UsageMetric::WebhookEndpointsCount` (Starter 3 / Pro 10). **H13b:** test.ping = SYNCHRONOUS inline (`WebhookTester`, no delivery row, `test.ping` never in the DB `event_type` column); redeliver = EXEMPT from re-metering (`DeliverWebhookJob` scalar `skipMeter`, re-runs SSRF, 404 cross-endpoint / 409 not-active); payload archival = OVERSIZED-AT-CREATION over `payload_archive_threshold_bytes` (`WebhookPayloadArchive` → `payload_attachment_id` seam + `webhook_delivery` morph alias, read back before signing); dual-secret rotation signs with BOTH via `WebhookSigner::signatureHeaderFor` during the `secret_rotation_grace_seconds` window; auto-disable = queued `WebhookAutoDisabledNotification` to the tenant owner on the breaker-trip edge. **CI GOTCHA (cost H13a a red PR run):** local openapi verification runs only the drift-diff, NOT `redocly lint` — every new controller method that becomes an operation MUST carry a PHPDoc first line (Scramble → operation `summary`; the `operation-summary` rule fails without it). `gh` is NOT pre-authed — export `GH_TOKEN` from `git credential fill` first (see Env notes). PHPStan: run `phpstan analyse --memory-limit=2G` locally (128M OOMs); the ~76 full-analyse errors are the known local baseline (CI is clean), so only ensure ZERO in touched files. **Full detail in the H13a + H13b (as-built) bullets under Current Status.**

**H5a…H11 + H12a are MERGED** (PR #54–63; all CI-green) — **the entitlement track, the server-side draft substrate (H9→a/b), the save-and-resume UX (H10), the basic dashboard KPIs (H11), AND the scheduled-forms backend (H12a) are on `main`.** **H12a is MERGED** (PR #63 `a07beae`; Pest 1271, all gates green) and **H12b (scheduled-forms UI) is MERGED** (PR #64 `2278f2d`, all 6 CI green) — **next = H13a.** (H12b CI gotcha for H13a: adding a builder toolbar button overflowed the non-wrapping `.builder__actions` at narrow viewports / large personalization fonts, which cascaded into axe color-contrast flags as buttons spilled off the light toolbar — fixed with `flex-wrap`; watch the responsive-overflow gate whenever you add a toolbar control.) **H12b design (do not re-open):** the builder config is a **modal from a "Schedule" toolbar button** (Inertia `useForm().patch` for inline 422s); the request **interprets the naive wall-clock in the chosen tz then stores UTC** (Eloquent drops a non-UTC offset otherwise); the guest **acceptance gate is fresh-load only — the resume path is never gated** (H12a grace window); **offline fail-closed is scoped to scheduled/capped forms** (unscheduled forms keep the G8b outbox); the encode path is **banner + disabled submit** (form stays visible); both presenters share **`FormScheduleView`** so the wire shape can't drift. A one-off **supply-chain remediation** (PR #61 `7154d5f`) landed between H10 and H11: two npm advisories (a prod-reachable postcss high + a **dev-only brace-expansion DoS**) turned the repo-wide `npm audit` gate red overnight. postcss is pinned via a `package.json` **override** (`^8.5.23`), and **the CI audit gate is now `npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high`** (production deps only — the remaining brace-expansion copy is an **override-immune *bundled* dep** of `@bundled-es-modules/glob` via `style-dictionary`, dev-only/non-shippable; a full-tree gate would hard-block every PR on it). Do not revert the `--omit=dev` scoping without accepting the breaking `--force` storybook bump it exists to avoid. `gh` is NOT pre-authed — export `GH_TOKEN` from `git credential fill` first (see Env notes). Plan of record: `C:\Users\DOH\.claude\plans\read-progress-md-if-the-melodic-swan.md`. **H11 design (do not re-open):** live COUNT-under-RLS aggregation service (`DashboardMetricsService`, `scoped()`), NO materialized rollup/table/job (the H2 dep is satisfied by the substrate merely existing); numbers are **visibility-scoped** via `Submission::scopeVisibleTo`/`dashboard.org.view` (org-wide vs own-forms; the Members tile is null-omitted for form-scoped roles); a reusable **`MdsStatTile`** DS primitive; the route stays **ungated** (every role lands on the dashboard). **New standing lesson:** run the full Pest suite as **`php -d memory_limit=-1 vendor/bin/pest`** — `-d …` on `artisan test` doesn't reach the child runner process (128M OOM at ~full-suite scale, on the container's php.ini-less CLI). **With H10 the save-and-resume vertical is COMPLETE** (respondent "finish later" + welcome-back restore + tenant expiry surface + per-form toggle + admin draft filter). **H10 design (do not re-open):** the guest SPA reuses the draft's `client_submission_uuid` so a resumed submit PROMOTES the draft row (never a duplicate); `resumeDraft()` is standalone (its own auth context, not the share-token client); two-tier reconciliation is **newest-wins, non-destructive** (`lib/reconcile.ts`); the tenant `draft_ttl_days` is **stamp-once** (a change only affects future drafts; the reaper is untouched); the per-form toggle gates the draft-SAVE (`save_resume_disabled` 403) but the resume-READ stays open so saved data is never stranded. **Forward seam left for H12a:** the draft-vs-**close** rule (closed-form/scheduled/`max_responses` don't exist yet; only version-superseded is real and already handled via the API 409 + drift path).

**H9a design (do not re-open) — carry these into H9b:** promotion is **in-place** (the SAME `submissions` row flips `draft→submitted`; a stable id H9b's resume token binds to). `SubmissionDraftService::saveDraft()`/`promote()` is the seam H9b's routes + H18a's OCR job call; **`submit()` was left byte-identical** — a same-uuid finalize MUST go through `promote()`, never `submit()` (H9b/H18a's invariant). The 409 checksum is suspended for drafts + re-armed on promote; the shared Stage-4 tail lives in `SubmissionFinalizer`. `draft_expires_at` is **stamped once** at draft creation (`DRAFT_TTL_DAYS=30`); H9b's reaper reaps `draft_expires_at < now()` and should **hard-delete** (the softDeletes × partial-unique-index re-used-uuid edge); H10 makes the 30-day tenant-configurable. `draftAlreadyFinalized()` reuses the 409 `submission_conflict` envelope — give it a distinct API code in H9b when the route ships. `save_and_resume` (Starter+) gates at H9b/H10's route layer, not the substrate.

**H5c inherits from H5b (do not re-open H5b's decisions):** the `legacyOverrides()` seam in `EntitlementService::feature()` is empty and resolves AHEAD of plan flags — H5c backs it with per-tenant storage + a merge-day backfill stamping every then-existing tenant (grandfather **indefinitely**, ADR-0008 §D5, zero merge-day regression). The `api_requests`/`webhook_deliveries` **monthly-quota 429** was deliberately DEFERRED from H5b to H5c (Free's `api_requests`=0 would back-door the `api_access` gate before the override exists); the meter already populates `usage_counters` for both. Feature-gating for xlsform/offline/templates/field-library/api_access is H5c (H5b gated only quotas, not features). Queue-fairness stays uniform (matrix §4); the §D9 `tier_resolver`/`tiers` config is the untouched Phase-4 seam.

**H5a design (do not re-open):** `plans` = global, no `tenant_id`, no RLS, **not** in `EXEMPT_TABLES`; `subscriptions`/`usage_counters` = strict RLS. Admin-assigned, **no Cashier** (Stripe columns dormant). Current plan = active subscription's plan (`Subscription::scopeActive`), else the seeded **`free`** fallback; **no `SubscriptionStatus`/`PlanStatus`** enum. `EntitlementService` (`scoped()`, memoized per tenant) with a `legacyOverrides()` grandfather seam (empty until H5c). Super-admin assign uses **adopt-tenant-context** (NOT `elevated()`) + a `subscription.updated` audit through the H4 `AuditLogger`. "Read-only props" shipped as the **`entitlements` shared Inertia prop** (fail-closed off-tenant); the console plan-selector UI is a deferred fast-follow. Full detail in the **H5a (as-built)** bullet under Current Status + ADR-0008.

**H5c (entitlement retro-gate) is DONE + MERGED** (PR #56 `a65437d`) — see the H5c as-built bullet. It closed the three deferred items: the per-tenant `legacy_overrides` grandfather storage + merge-day backfill, the five feature gates, and the `api_requests` 429. **H9a (server-side DRAFT substrate) is DONE + green on branch** — see the H9a as-built bullet. **Next is H9b** (resume token + Group-C/B routes + draft-expiry reaper) — confirm the exact scope against the H-map.

**The rest of the ADR wave still to write** (all docs-only, same shape as H1a): H1d (**OCR provider bake-off — blocked on the user's document sample, keeps its day-one start for external lead time**), H1e, H1f (step graph over relevance), H1h. H1b (entitlements → ADR-0008) and H1g (OAuth token custody → **ADR-0009**) are written. H1c was removed with payments. Phase 3 = **Increment H**: the ADR wave, then four backend substrates (**H2 async / H3 queued mail / H4 audit+event catalog / H5 entitlements**), then the **server-side draft substrate (H9)** shared by save-and-resume + OCR, then the feature verticals.

**The 5 open questions — ANSWERED 2026-07-21 (do not re-litigate; cite these):**
1. **Workflow branching = relevance-derived step-skipping**, not true goto. No `ExpressionKind::Branch`, no evaluator-mirror change, no new golden vectors — the R3 gate is untouched by H21. **Consequence: R5's DB-level version-immutability trigger loses its forcing moment** and becomes its own small hardening PR (**H25**), operation/column-scoped. Do not let it drop silently.
2. **Payments DEFERRED out of Phase 3 → Phase 4.** **H1c (ADR-0009) and H20a/b/c are removed.** Knock-ons: H9a no longer needs a payment holding-state; the H7↔H20 amount-tampering hazard is moot in-phase (but H7 still ships hidden fields as *server-constrained untrusted input*, recorded so Phase 4 inherits it); H17 queued PDF is unaffected.
3. **Gating:** (a) **grandfather indefinitely** — tenants existing at the H5c merge keep every ungated Phase-2 feature permanently via a per-tenant legacy override; zero merge-day regression. (b) native connectors = **Starter+ uniformly** (one `native_connectors` key). (c) branding **unbundled from custom domains → Starter+**; `custom_domain` stays Business.
4. **Business tier = build + gate, HOLD FROM SALE** until Track B exists. **No 99.5% contractual clause is published in Phase 3.**
5. **OCR: user supplies the document sample (incl. real linelist sheets); H1d then bakes off Google Vision vs Azure Document Intelligence vs AWS Textract** and recommends one with a measured cost/page model. H1d keeps its day-one start (external lead time) and is **blocked until the sample arrives**. The `<15%` correction-rate target stays a live contingency — miss it and H18/H19 leave Phase 3 wholesale. **H8 is independent and lands early regardless.**
- *Secondary:* **H16 (Google Sheets + Airtable) moves to Phase 3b.** H15 (OAuth framework + Slack) stays in-phase; Zapier (H14) already reaches Sheets/Airtable. **H1g stays** — H15 needs it.

**Revised size: ~46 PRs** (7 docs + ~38 code + H25), down from ~52. PRD coverage = **11 of 12 in-phase**, payments explicitly cut and recorded.

**New standing lesson from H2 — the framework is also "reality", not just migrations.** The existing lesson says *§-numbered docs describe intent; migrations and code describe reality*. H2 extends it: **an ADR's claims about a FRAMEWORK API are intent too.** Five of ADR-0007's thirteen sub-decisions were wrong against the installed version — not vaguely, but in ways that would have shipped a leak (`JobProcessed` misses the failure path), a self-inflicted outage (`$tries` inverting the fairness it sits next to; the §D13 enum-vs-string comparison releasing every job on the platform), or a fatal error (`$queue` as a property). The ADR anticipated this at `:178` and said so; **the discipline that worked was reading `vendor/` for every API the plan leaned on, and then proving each correction by mutation rather than by argument.** Related, cheap, and worth repeating: `sed -i` with a `$` anchor silently no-ops on this repo's CRLF files — a mutation that appears to apply but doesn't will quietly tell you a test is stronger than it is. Verify the mutant landed before trusting the red.

**Standing lesson reinforced by H1a — the third instance:** §-numbered docs describe *intent*; migrations and code describe *reality*. Verification refuted **six** `file:line` citations in an otherwise-correct plan, plus two H-map claims that would have written new errors into the corpus (the "27 permissions" correction — the doc already says 28 — and an 8-item PRD roadmap row that does not exist). Before G11 it was the `accent` column that "already existed" and didn't. **Grep every claim of the form "X exists" before writing it into a document**, and prefer characterising the *absence* precisely ("no first-party outbound HTTP call site") over the sweeping version ("no guzzle"), which was false.

**PR #48 is MERGED** (squashed to `506ff97`, 2026-07-21, all 6 CI jobs green on head `7fe07be`; branch `g11-personalization` deleted). **Increment G (G1→G11) and Phase 2 are COMPLETE** — roadmap table updated.

**Track B (the Windows Server host, `docs/deployment-infrastructure.md` §8) is DEFERRED until app development is done — user decision, 2026-07-21.** Do **not** open it as an alternative track at the top of a session; the H-map is the single next move. Rationale: standing the box up now buys nothing Phase 3 needs (local Docker Compose is the parity source and CI runs on Linux runners either way) while adding a live production host to patch, back up, TLS-renew and supervise across all of Phase 3. **Nothing is blocked by this** — the deploy *pipeline* is already committed and dormant by design: `deploy.ps1` at the repo root and `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`, gated on the repo variable `DEPLOY_ENABLED == 'true'`, which does not yet exist. §8 is purely the one-time box topology (PHP 8.4 / PostgreSQL + the non-superuser `meridian_app` role / Memurai / nginx / php-cgi / NSSM services / Task Scheduler / win-acme / the self-hosted runner). When it is time: work §8 steps 1–8 first (app stack), then 9–10 (runner + `DEPLOY_ENABLED`) as a deliberate second step, since step 9 is what arms automatic production deploys on every green `main`. **Watch the clock on this one** — ADR-0005 flags Windows Server 2016 end-of-support at ~Jan 2027, so a very long deferral turns "stand up the box" into "stand up the box on a newer Server OS."

**CI took two follow-up fixes, both harness-shaped and both predicted as the top risks:**
1. `frontend` — `token-references.test.ts` threw `ERR_INVALID_URL_SCHEME` on CI while passing locally. The suite runs under `environment: 'happy-dom'`, whose DOM `URL` global shadows Node's, and resolving a five-level **directory** URL (trailing slash) through it does not preserve the `file:` scheme. Its sibling suites use the identical `import.meta.url` pattern and pass because they resolve concrete **file** paths. Fixed with `process.cwd()` (Vitest's cwd is its config's directory = repo root). **New standing gotcha: prefer `process.cwd()` over `import.meta.url` arithmetic in Vitest suites that need the repo root.**
2. `tests` — the new guest-shell test threw `Vite manifest not found`. It is the only test in `GuestRuntimeTest` that renders the SHELL rather than hitting a JSON endpoint, so the only one evaluating `@vite`; the Pest job builds no assets. It passed locally purely because assets happened to be built. Fixed with `withoutVite()` (the existing convention). **Reproduced deliberately by moving `public/build/manifest.json` aside — worth doing for any test that renders a blade view.**

The two jobs feared most (`design-system-a11y` for story attribute leakage, `e2e` for the font-loading race and 375×extra_large reflow) **passed on the first run** — the mitigations held.

**Correction recorded (this tracker was wrong):** earlier entries claimed "the `user_ui_preferences` columns already exist (C2 shipped `theme_mode` + `accent` through them), so this is a settings-surface + token-plumbing increment, not a schema one." **C2 shipped `theme_mode` only.** `accent` was a hardcoded PHP literal in `User::uiTheme()`, never a column — the accent *plumbing* (blade emission + theme CSS) existed while the *storage* did not. Building on that premise would have skipped the migration entirely. The claim originated in the G-map plan's exploration notes, which read data-dictionary §19 (the target schema) rather than the migration (the actual schema) — worth remembering as a general failure mode: **§-numbered docs describe intent, migrations describe reality.**

**Local-environment caveat recorded while building G10b2 (not a code issue):** `builder-axe` fails locally in this Windows/Docker setup with `color-contrast` on `.mds-button--secondary`, in a theme/viewport combination that **changes between runs**. It reproduces on **`main` at `ca92379` too** (verified by checking out main and re-running), and CI is green on main — so it is a local rendering artifact, not a regression.
